**Faceless preview fixed — `bodies` is not an implicit character override (2026-11-16, `main`, live; not `VERIFIED`):** `_characters_payload` reused the pack `bodies` atlas whenever `characters` was absent, so the Habblaud editor preview and in-world agents rendered the old LimeZu body with no face. An absent `characters` block now means procedural Habblaud bodies; the payload also blanks `bodies` unless an explicit `characters` block exists. Live theme `bodies`/`characters` cleared and `theme.py` deployed. Live: sable preview and office bodies show Habblaud faces; LimeZu floors, portraits, UI and outside scene remain. Focused theme suite **214 passed, 2 skipped**; screenshot `.playwright-mcp/sable-preview-fixed.png`; 0 console errors. No full matrix; no `VERIFIED`.

**Functional rooms restored — procedural Habblaud furniture/windows (2026-11-16, `main`, live; not `VERIFIED`):** The full-pack furniture mapping guessed unrelated LimeZu singles, so kitchen tables became plants, restroom fixtures disappeared, and the static `window` override removed the glass/light. **Habblaud character customization now saves and applies (2026-11-16, `main`, live; not `VERIFIED`):** The Agent panel editor was dead because the Qubit/office API bridge refused every non-GET and `h_office_habblaud_character` was only a C4 staged refusal. It is now a real local cosmetic store: `voicebridge/office/characters.py` owns `characters.json` beside `office.db` (atomic 0600 write, validated name/seed/parts, bounded entries); `PUT`/`DELETE /api/office/habblaud/agents/<id>/character` save/reset it; `api-bridge.ts` forwards the editor's PUT/DELETE with method, body and session token; `tower.state` emits `display_name`/`seed`/`parts`/`custom`; `office_stream.to_snapshot` forwards them as `AgentInfo.name`/`seed`/`parts`/`custom`; the tower cache key now includes `characters.json` and save/reset invalidate `office_tower`, so a save is visible on the next 2s poll instead of being hidden for up to 60s. Live verified: saved `Ada Q` from the real Habblaud editor (name updated in the office sidebar, `characters.json` written), then DELETE reset it back to `ceo`; 0 console errors. Screenshot `.playwright-mcp/habblaud-custom-save.png`. Focused: character/compat/seat/stream/plugin-app **157 passed**, bridge Vitest **13 passed**, `tsc -p . --noEmit` clean, `git diff --check` clean. No full matrix; no `VERIFIED`.

**Extended LimeZu asset pipeline for Qubit Office (2026-11-15, branch `fix/office-assets` @ `7ea8606a0`; not merged, not deployed):** The theme builder now consumes the rest of the purchased packs in memory instead of only six floor tiles and the character-part recipes. `scripts/office_theme_extract.py::extract_assets` catalogs five categories — **characters** (body/portrait bodies, outfits, eyes, hair, accessories; 292 sheets), **furniture** (5,381 `Theme_Sorter_Singles` sprites + 28 interior/`Theme_Sorter` static sheets), **animated** (311 `3_Animated_objects` spritesheets with frame w/h/count and fps from the matching GIF; `frame_source: gif|sheet|inferred`), **ui** (15 sheets: Modern Interiors button/icon + thinking-emote sheets and Modern UI 16/32/48 `Style_1`/`Style_2` 9-slice candidates + Gamepad) and **outside** (22 Serene Village tiles/props/animated objects, CC-BY 4.0 TASL carried per entry). `scripts/office_theme_build.py --assets` (default on when the packs are present; `--no-assets` preserves the old pack; `--asset-categories` narrows it) writes `assets/manifest.json` + `assets/<category>/index.json` + content-addressed `assets/<category>/<sha>.png`, each index entry carrying source sheet, sprite id, frame geometry/grid, pixel `sha256` and raw `source_sha256`. The shareable recipe gains **counts/kinds only** (no files, sheet names, index paths or hashes); `voicebridge/office/theme.py` validates the new `assets` block/indexes/additive payload URLs and `theme_cli._recipe_from_pack` exports the metadata-only form. **Real build:** `/tmp/qubit-office-assets-pack` — **6,035 declared files**, **19,244,335 declared bytes** (39 MB on disk), `theme.json` sha256 `5467a690…`, assets `{animated:311, characters:292, furniture:5409, outside:22, ui:15}`; `--check` → `OK`. **Tests:** new `tests/test_office_theme_assets.py` **17 passed** (catalog/geometry/GIF timing, missing vs corrupt pack, build/index/hash integrity, reproducibility, metadata-only recipe + refused file map, committed recipe/theme dir carry no art, read-only extraction, CLI wiring); focused theme set **259 passed, 2 skipped**. **Honest gaps:** the paid art exists only in the local `/tmp` pack (never the repo); the committed `theme.recipe.json` was left unchanged (it remains metadata-only); the optional real-pack test is manual, not CI; no full matrix, no `VERIFIED`.
**Campus world — floors on one shared stage (2026-11-15, branch `fix/office-openworld`; worktree `/home/trick/qubit-worktrees/office-openworld`, base `6d4a18c51`; not merged/deployed):** Replaced the separate iframe-card `FloorCampus` with `CampusWorld`, one continuous tower stage. Provider I/O is injected, so tests use a fake and make no network call. **Routes:** `GET /api/office/business/etsy/status` (read-only, token-gated, no secrets) plus owner-gated `authorize`/`complete`/`disconnect` handlers, registered in `qubit_compat` and covered by the existing `/api/office/business` read authority. **UI:** new `EtsyConnectPanel` dock opened from the Agents header, showing the real status/missing settings, `Connect Etsy` (opens the PKCE URL), redirect-URL/code completion, refresh and disconnect; it renders `Connected` only when the server says both `state:"connected"` and `connected:true`, and never displays a token. Also fixed a latent integration-import cycle by making `integrations/base.py` import `business.ids.fingerprint` locally. **Verified (focused):** `tests/test_etsy_oauth_connect.py` + `tests/test_business_etsy_connect.py` **29 passed**; expanded Business/Etsy/platform set **260 passed**; office-app Vitest **23 files / 254 tests** passed; Office Vite build `qubit-office.game.js` sha256 `65378b08…`, CSS `81978ac4…`, second build byte-identical; `ruff` and `py_compile` clean; `tsc` over the changed UI files reports no errors (only pre-existing Phaser alias errors in untouched files); `git diff --check` clean. **Not done:** no live Etsy app/OAuth run, no service restart, no main merge/full matrix; the operator must supply a real Etsy app and redirect URI. `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Live-write tripwire lane (2026-11-11, branch `feat/live-write-tripwire`, worktree `/home/trick/qubit-worktrees/live-write-tripwire`, base `f500c7705`; not merged, no live restart):** Defense-in-depth after the 2026-11-10 profile-delete incident. The existing `qubit_state._ensure_test_isolation` refuses **all** opens from a pytest context (env or ancestry); this lane adds a narrower **write-only** tripwire for the shape that still escaped: a child spawned by a test/agent that rebuilds its env and loses `PYTEST_*`/`QUBIT_HOME`. **Signal:** `_in_test_context()` (existing marker env `HERMES_TEST_ISOLATION`, root-suite marker `QUBIT_TEST_ISOLATION`, `PYTEST_*`, or a pytest ancestor) **or** `_in_agent_subprocess()` (Qubit's own `QUBIT_AGENT_SUBPROCESS=1`, stamped by `tools/environments/base_session_env.py::_wrap_command_script`; external harness markers `OPENCODE`/`AGENT`/`CLAUDE_CODE`/`CURSOR_AGENT`/`CODEX`/`AIDER`/`GOOSE`; or an external-agent ancestor). Ancestry is the part that survives an env rebuild: psutil when present, a Linux `/proc/<pid>/{stat,cmdline,environ}` walk otherwise. **Refusal:** `qubit_state.SessionDB.__init__` calls `_ensure_live_write_allowed` before the broader guard for writable opens only; a production root (`~/.qubit` / `~/.hermes` / profile home) raises the typed `LiveStateDbWriteRefused` (`code=LIVE_STATE_DB_WRITE_REFUSED`, still a `RuntimeError` matching `live-system guard`); reads, temp homes and the sanctioned `HERMES_STATE_DB_GUARD_BYPASS` escape are unchanged. The live service/CLI entry points (`python -m voicebridge`, `voicebridge.gateway_daemon`, `qubit serve/gateway/dashboard`, the `qubit` console script) are exempt. `scripts/route_e2e_audit.py` now mirrors `controls_e2e_audit.py`: mutations are OFF by default and require `--allow-live-mutations` (its profile/webhook/mcp cleanup DELETEs are the same incident class). **Verified (focused, `TMPDIR=/tmp`, venv `/home/trick/venvs/qubit314`):** new `qubit_runtime/tests/qubit_state/test_live_write_tripwire.py` **13 passed** (typed refusal under pytest; agent-subprocess refusal; normal process + service exemption; bypass; temp write; write-only scoping; real child process with `HERMES_TEST_ISOLATION`/`QUBIT_TEST_ISOLATION`/`QUBIT_AGENT_SUBPROCESS` refuses `<temp HOME>/.qubit/state.db` but writes `<temp>/scratch/state.db`; service detection is module-based, not a path substring; shell wrapper stamps the marker); existing isolation/ancestry/marker set **25 passed**; root `tests/test_route_e2e_audit.py` + controls gate **14 passed**; root SessionDB/mixed set **345 passed**; `ruff` clean on touched files; `git diff --check` clean. **Residual gaps:** a child that rebuilds its env *and* is reparented away from any test/agent ancestor (daemonized) is not detected; external-harness detection is a known-name list; `QUBIT_AGENT_SUBPROCESS` covers Qubit terminal commands, not every internal worker; the write tripwire is `SessionDB`-scoped, so a direct `sqlite3.connect(<live>/state.db)` bypasses it (no product path does). No live redeploy is needed for the guard to matter (it arms only in test/agent processes); the live services are unaffected. No merge/push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Office DB read-old adoption lane (2026-11-10, branch `feat/office-db-adopt`, worktree `/home/trick/qubit-worktrees/office-db-adopt`, base `e2737a696`; not merged, no live restart):** The store now auto-adopts a populated legacy XDG office DB instead of orphaning it. **Design:** `voicebridge/office/store.py::connect` calls `_adopt_legacy_if_needed` before opening/creating the canonical file. If the canonical DB is absent **or** logically empty (no `office_department`/`office_agent` rows) and `read_old_voice_dirs()["state"]/office/office.db` exists and is populated, the legacy DB is **copied** into the canonical path (`$QUBIT_HOME/voice/state/office/office.db`); a populated canonical always wins. The legacy file and its `-wal`/`-shm` are copied into a private staging dir, normalized through SQLite's backup API, fsynced, and `os.replace`d into place; an existing empty canonical file is first moved to `office.db.pre-adopt-<stamp>-<pid>.bak` **with its sidecars** so a stale WAL cannot be replayed against the adopted DB. A `flock` on `office-adopt.lock` serializes concurrent adopters and is re-checked under the lock, making it idempotent; the legacy DB is never moved, deleted, or written. Neither-store and empty-legacy cases behave as before (new empty canonical). The `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` migration from `2f8a8f990` is unchanged. **Verified (focused, `PYTHONPATH=<worktree> TMPDIR=/tmp`, venv `/home/trick/venvs/qubit314`):** new `tests/test_office_db_adopt9v.py` **6 passed** (legacy-only adopted/readable, empty canonical backed up + adopted, populated canonical wins, neither -> new empty DB, empty legacy not adopted, idempotent re-open; all assert the legacy file's sha256/rows are unchanged); `tests/test_office_suite_health.py` + `tests/test_office_store_migrate_race9v.py` **12 passed**; `tests/test_office_cross_module.py` **27 passed, 1 skipped**; `py_compile`, `git diff --check` clean; `ruff` reports only the pre-existing unused `CONFIRM_CODE` import present on `main`. No full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**E2E terminal lane `e2e/terminal` (2026-11-07, worktree `/tmp/qubit-e2e-tui`, base `ceac0972a`):** Fixed the terminal-suite harness root causes (no product code changed) and classified the rest. **Sentinel half-rebrand:** `qubit serve` prints `QUBIT_BACKEND_READY`, but `_gateway_client.READY_RE` still matched only `HERMES_BACKEND_READY`, so every module fixture in `test_interactive_roundtrip.py` (10 errors) and `test_multiclient_session_model.py` (3 errors) timed out after 90 s against a healthy backend (the sandbox `serve.stdout.log` showed `QUBIT_BACKEND_READY port=…`). **E2E core CLI lane `e2e/cli` (2026-11-07, worktree `/tmp/qubit-e2e-core`, base `ceac0972a`):** Reproduced and closed the two `/tmp/e2e-all.log` failures; one was a stale test assumption, two findings were real product defects. **Product fixes:** (1) `CLISessionMixin._transfer_session_yolo` was missing (removed by the 088f re-pin, its two call sites left in `cli_commands_mixin`/`cli_chat_turn_mixin`), so `/branch` raised `AttributeError` after creating/ending the parent, leaving the CLI on an ended id and the next turn refused `permission_denied`; it now delegates to `tools.approval_yolo.transfer_session_yolo`. (3) `/bg` built an in-process `AIAgent` (the one-gateway witness saw `agent_init` in the CLI); it now uses the existing `qubit_cli.gateway_side.start` → `prompt.background` verb, keeping the local thread path only for a non-gateway session. **Stale assertion:** `test_resize_scrollback` counted one `❯` row in the whole pane, valid before commit `8b432f512` made the modern glyph layout echo each submitted user turn with the prompt glyph; it now pins `1 + len(WORDS)` prompt rows with exactly one composer row (no duplicate/stray rows). `test_session_switch_mid_turn_guard` no longer stubs `_transfer_session_yolo`, so the missing method would fail it. **Verified (alone, stated env):** `test_resize_scrollback` + `test_classic_cli_main_parity` **2 passed in 113.8 s**; `test_classic_cli_live_session` **3 passed in 218.7 s**; focused unit set (`test_cli_approval_ui`, `test_gateway_side`, `test_session_switch_mid_turn_guard`, `test_branch_command`, `test_gateway_session_store`, `test_cli_yolo_toggle`, `test_approval_yolo`) **91 passed**; ruff/`py_compile`/`git diff --check` clean. **Remaining (classified, not fixed):** the gateway `prompt.btw` verb still calls `answer_side_question(..., on_fork=, should_stop=)`, which the ported `agent/side_question.py` does not accept (`unexpected keyword argument 'on_fork'`), so the classic `/btw` stays on its local one-shot path (no witness violation); wiring it needs that signature restored. No live service/config, no restart, no push/merge; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**E2E upgrade/install lane `e2e/upgrade` (2026-11-06, worktree `/tmp/qubit-e2e-upgrade`, base `b3e23e73e`):** Fixed the shared upgrade-suite harness root causes and the product defects they exposed. **Harness:** the vendored runtime is one level inside the git checkout, so `WORKTREE` (runtime root) could not be cloned; added `repo_root()`/`repo_rel()` (git toplevel) and used it for `head_sha`, `make_origin`, N-1 resolution, `git show` runtime paths and the installer path; sandboxes now set `QUBIT_REPO_URL` to the rewritten official URL (before this a test install cloned real GitHub); `HERMES_RUNTIME_DIR` no longer points at the developer's live tool store; PM facts/state are read under the runtime install key; `HERMES_INSTALL_CHANNEL=main` keeps installs hermetic; N-1 falls back to `HEAD~1` because Qubit has no reachable CalVer tag (and does not treat the unrelated `v1.0.0` as a release); the lazy-install var is cleared in launches that must behave like a real user's; runtime-relative paths are mapped through `qubit_runtime/` for git reads/release commits. **Product fixes:** the vendored bootstrap (`qubit_runtime/scripts/install.sh`) now resolves `qubit_runtime/` for PM/completion/config/markers; `qubit update`/`prepare_launch`/`detect_install_method`/`--version` resolve the enclosing git checkout from the runtime `PROJECT_ROOT`; the completion marker is armed under the runtime state root; `prepare_launch` probes supervised markers from the ruamel-free leaf (the old import made the owed tail always fail before PM activation); `pyproject.toml` now ships `connectors`/`evals`/`recipes`/`roles`/`traces` (installed wheels raised `ModuleNotFoundError: recipes`); MCP rebuild copy names Qubit. **Verified (focused, one file at a time):** foreign_state **5 passed**; local_modifications **4**; truthful_outcome **6**; hostile_post **5 passed/1 failed**; hostile_pause **4/5**; partial_clone **2/1**; shallow_install **3/1**; handoff_from_head **7 errors**; update_network_failure **1 passed/1 skipped**; handoff_from_n1 **7 skipped**; release_channel_records **15**; offline_and_flaky **8**; proxy_only_egress **5**; mirrors **2**; paths **1**, paths_symlinked **1**, path_shapes **3**, permissions **2**, libc_musl **1**, hostile_pause_record **1 skipped**; pm configured_features **3/1s**, gateway_install_force **2**, generation_gc **2**, interrupted_update **2**, restart_generations **2**, stamps_and_doctor **7**, interpreter_hygiene **2/1**; config_roundtrip_properties **333**; focused unit set (venv_sync/launch completion/zip completion/install shapes/pip detection/version/borrowed launch/packaging) **122 passed, 2 skipped**. **Remaining (classified):** kanban worker spawned after update dies at spawn (handoff_from_head 7 errors, interpreter_hygiene 1) = real product defect; gateway-relaunch identify never reports the target sha after pause/restart (hostile_pause 5, hostile_post 1) = real defect; `tree:0` clone becomes `blob:none` across an update (partial_clone tree0) = real update defect; seeded depth-1 checkout is converted by the installer before the cell runs (shallow depth1) = premise not reproducible in this layout. No live service touched, no restart, no push, no merge; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Fast E2E lane (2026-11-06, `main` `02089822a`):** Added `scripts/run_e2e_fast.py`, the iteration lane for the user-facing paths: the browser new-chat/resume contract plus the dashboard and classic-CLI E2E files, run one pytest process per file with `-j 4`, a 180 s per-file cap, and no retries. Long/live suites (`test_classic_cli_live_session.py`, `test_resize_scrollback.py`, `test_cron_virtual_clock_soak.py`, `test_messaging_exactly_once.py`) are deliberately left to the final gate. **Measured:** browser **3 passed in 24.3 s**; dashboard + classic CLI **7 files / 12 tests passed in 85.5 s** (runner wall; 275 s CPU-wall, 4x parallelism). Fixes required to get there: the dashboard helper now accepts the Qubit `QUBIT_DASHBOARD_READY` sentinel and exposes an explicit Node path to the sandbox; the PTY test no longer waits on the animated ghost for a settled frame; the classic suite uses a host-independent PATH, the detached-gateway reaper is explicitly marked, and its baseline strings now name Qubit (`Welcome to Qubit Agent!`, `qubit --resume`). Product fallback welcome copy in `gateway_chat_view.py` was also corrected from Hermes to Qubit. Full E2E and the full matrix were not rerun; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**TUI/Ink startup lane `fix10/tui-start` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix10-tui-start`, base `a8b37e7ba`):** Closed the remaining per-chat Node/Ink cost after the freshness cache, plus a rebuild loop it exposed. **Measured chain:** `/api/pty` → `_resolve_chat_argv` (~19.5 ms warm, dominated by `pm.ensure("node")` + the receipt fingerprint) → PTY spawn → Node bundle parse/first byte (~150 ms) → Ink mount + first gateway RPC (~265 ms on attach). **Fixes:** (1) `_apply_tui_node_env` sends `NODE_COMPILE_CACHE` to the TUI child at `$QUBIT_HOME/cache/node-compile-cache` (operator override wins; Node creates the path and ignores an unwritable one, so Python writes nothing); a graceful TUI exit (SIGHUP/SIGTERM both) flushes the cache. (2) `gatewayClient.ts` loads `undici` only when the runtime lacks the native `WebSocket` (Node 22+ has it; the node20 fallback stays bundled). (3) `_tui_node_bin` caches the verified PM Node path (re-resolving only if it disappears) instead of re-running `pm.ensure` on every chat. (4) `freshness.mjs::productCurrent` lets a **TUI** receipt survive a different builder Node when platform/arch, sourceHash and outputHash still match; a host Node upgrade or a build by a different node no longer invalidates a valid bundle (desktop/web keep exact-Node). This removes the observed live rebuild loop (logs showed whole-TUI rebuilds at 16:34/17:03; `source_product_current` returned False on the clean tree because PM Node 26.12.1 ≠ receipt 22.22.3). **Measured (same harness, 10 warm runs, SIGTERM so the cache flushes; medians):** Node first byte **156 → 115 ms**, first gateway RPC (Ink mounted) **293 → 233 ms** (`old bundle/no cache → new bundle/cache`); the bundle change alone is 293 → 268 ms and the compile cache alone 268 → 233 ms. Python resolve **19.5 → 15.1 ms** (cached PM node; disable-the-cache A/B in one process). Whole proxy new-chat first paint ≈ **175 → 130 ms**; first RPC ≈ **313 → 248 ms**. The intermittent full rebuild it also removes is ~0.25–1 s on the first launch after a receipt/host-Node drift. **Verified (focused):** new `tests/qubit_cli/test_tui_startup_cache10.py` **7 passed** (cache path/override/legacy alias/no writes, cached PM Node + re-resolve on removal, `HERMES_NODE` precedence, dashboard env carries the cache, real-Node receipt test: TUI survives Node drift while web does not); `test_web_server_chat_workspaces.py` + `test_tui_packaged_layout.py` + `test_source_build_freshness_cache9v.py` **15 passed**; `test_tui_npm_install.py` + `test_tui_resume_flow.py` + `test_dashboard_tui_backcompat.py` **26 passed**; `test_source_build.py` **20 passed**; TUI `gatewayClient.test.ts` + `gatewayRecovery.test.ts` **24 passed**; root fresh-install/export/parity set **25 passed, 1 skipped**; the source checkout probes `source_product_current(...) == True` and the receipt/node-drift path returns True. **Irreducible:** Node executable + bundle parse/React-Ink module init/first render remain ~0.23 s (first RPC) on this host; the compile cache needs one graceful exit to populate and is lost after a SIGKILL. Pre-existing/environmental (unrelated): `tests/docker/test_tui_prebuilt_bundle.py::test_tui_gateway_child_uses_packaged_python` fails inside the image with `KeyError: 'QUBIT_PYTHON'`, and `tests-js/product-builders.test.mjs` has 4 stale `hermes-ink` fixture failures on base. No UI/transcript change, no live `:8890` restart, no full matrix, no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Resumed-transcript projection lane `fix10/resume-core` (2026-11-05, base `a8b37e7ba`, branch `fix10/resume-core`):** Followed the resume-bundle work with a replay-projection pass that keeps the transcript byte-identical. The replayed tool preview re-parsed each shell/code segment several times per row (`_split_shell_words` was called ~15× per preview) and rebuilt every preview on each resume; `_history_to_messages` is the largest remaining cold-resume cost. **Fixes:** (1) `agent/display.py` memoises the pure word/compound shell splitters (bounded lru; inputs >2 KB/4 KB bypass so a huge code block cannot pin memory; cached word lists are copied out so `_shell_head_word`'s `pop` can never mutate another caller's list); (2) `tui_gateway/server.py::_tool_ctx` gained a bounded LRU keyed by `(tool name, display token = (language, friendly-labels), canonical args JSON)`, skipping keys >4 KB, so a warm resume/session switch reuses every preview; `_history_to_messages` resolves the display token once per projection instead of once per row; (3) `_Resume` gained the missing class-level `tool_labels = True` mirror (hand-built resumes previously raised `AttributeError`; pre-existing at base). Result equality is proven by hashing the full projected lists. **Measured (throwaway copy of the live 825 MB `state.db`, session `20261003_093120_d09dc4`: 7,020 rows → 5,360 display / 378 model / 5,341 wire messages, 2,512 tool rows; CPU median of 5, same harness before/after):** `_history_to_messages` TUI (`tool_labels=False`) **676.4 → 77.5 ms** (~8.7×); Desktop (`tool_labels=True`) **1341.3 → 410.2 ms** (~3.3×); projected-list sha256 identical before/after (`b18b45…` TUI, `df9020…` Desktop); `summarize_shell_command` output hash identical (`33bfa8…`, 1,676 inputs). `get_resume_bundle` **~248–268 ms** (unchanged: one lineage SELECT + decode) and the 5.5 MB `json.dumps` **~44 ms** are the remaining replay cost. **Focused:** new `tests/agent/test_display_shell_split_cache9v.py` (18) + `tests/tui_gateway/test_resume_projection_cache9v.py` (5) prove cached-vs-uncached split equality, no shared-list aliasing, cache hits/language scoping/oversized-key bypass, identical cold-vs-warm projection, and 1,200-message history with no truncation; combined resume/display/history set **87 passed**, resume-scope set **53 passed**; `ruff`/`py_compile`/`git diff --check` clean. No UI change, no live `:8890` restart, no full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Sessions → chat resume lane `fix10/sessions` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix10-sessions`, base `a8b37e7ba`, HEAD `222022e19`):** Traced `/sessions` row → `/chat?resume=<id>` → `/api/pty?resume=` → `web_server_chat._resolve_chat_argv` → `HERMES_TUI_RESUME` on the live `:8890` (screenshot shows the resumed `hi` / `Hey Tricky — what do you need?` transcript). Found and fixed three concrete defects: (1) the Overview "Recent Sessions" cards had **no** open-in-chat action (only inline expand, so a recent session could not be resumed); (2) resume links were `/chat?resume=<id>` with no management profile — `/api/pty` derives the child's `QUBIT_HOME` from the **query** `profile` (not the ws-ticket, which carries the terminal MODEL profile), so a row from a secondary profile resumed against the launch home and forged a fresh chat; (3) `ProfileProvider`'s URL re-assert race stripped a just-arrived `?profile=` on a deep link before the adoption effect could copy it into state (same stale/deleted query class). Changes: new `src/lib/session-resume-href.ts` (single resume-link builder with the row profile); SessionsPage History and Overview both use it (the Overview card gains the same `Resume in Chat` icon action and its keyboard event no longer doubles as the disclosure toggle); `TerminalChatPage` reads `?profile=`, probes `getSessionDetail` in that store, forwards `profile` on the `/api/pty` WebSocket, re-probes/reconnects when the scope changes, and scopes copy-last-response; `ProfileProvider` only re-asserts its state profile when the URL names none. **Verified (focused):** full frontend Vitest **161 files / 1270 tests passed**; the touched subset (`SessionsPage.test.tsx`, `SessionsPage.resume.test.tsx` new, `session-resume-href.test.ts` new, `TerminalChatPage.panel.test.tsx`, `ChatSessionList.test.tsx`, `App.test.tsx`) **75 passed**; `tsc -b` clean; `eslint` 0 errors (5 pre-existing react-hooks warnings); new Python `qubit_runtime/tests/qubit_cli/test_pty_keepalive_ws.py::test_pty_forwards_profile_query_to_chat_argv` **13 passed** for the file. **Browser-level (new source via a temp Vite dev server on `:8891` proxying live `:8890`, not the live process):** the Overview card's new Resume button opened `/chat?resume=20261105_045741_13320e`, the PTY WebSocket carried `resume=` (and `profile=` when the deep link named one), and the screenshot showed the resumed transcript under the banner. **Known limitation:** the History row body still expands inline; Resume is the explicit one-click icon (now on both views). A `?profile=` that names the dashboard's own profile takes the profile-scoped PTY path (same home, separate child gateway) — correct store, slightly slower cold start. No `:8890` restart, no full matrix, no publish/push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Hermes dashboard parity — safe read-only API block `fix10/parity-api` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix10-parity-api`, base `a8b37e7ba`):** Added `voicebridge/qubit_desktop_plugins.py` and wired it into the compat table: `GET /api/desktop-plugins/{root,list,read}` now serve the host's real `<QUBIT_HOME>/desktop-plugins` tree by calling the vendored module's own audited helpers (`web_routers.desktop_plugins._inside_root/_list/_read`), so containment, the two-component limit, the sensitive-path guard, symlink rules and the 16 MiB cap are the same code the Webapp surface uses (no reimplementation; typed `DESKTOP_PLUGINS_NOT_FOUND`/`_BAD_PATH`/`_TOO_LARGE`/`_UNAVAILABLE` envelopes). The read-only root deliberately does **not** run the Webapp reconcile copy pass; `POST /api/desktop-plugins/reconcile` is the typed gate `DESKTOP_PLUGINS_RECONCILE_UNAVAILABLE` naming native Desktop and the Webapp surface. `GET /api/plugins/kanban/workflow` now serves `kanban_workflow.DEFAULT_WORKFLOW.to_dict()` (the vendored plugin's one missing read; every board uses the default workflow today) instead of a bare no-compat-route 404. `/api/desktop-plugins` was added to `webui.QUBIT_READ_AUTHORITY` so the fork proxy (whose dashboard surface 404s these routes) cannot shadow the handlers. **`GET /api/hermes/update/check` was inspected and deliberately left unaliased** per the owner ruling: it stays a 404 `NOT_FOUND`, while the canonical `GET /api/qubit/update/check` remains the real 200 probe. **Verified (focused, `PYTHONPATH=worktree TMPDIR=/tmp`):** new `tests/test_parity_api_fix10.py` **12 passed** (route table/authority, real root/list/read + marker shape, sensitive/`..`/nested refusals, oversized 413, linked-entry refusal, read-only root (no reconcile copy), reconcile typed gate, workflow data, and a real loopback `WebUI` boundary proving token-required auth plus the `/agent/api` shape); `tests/test_no_public_hermes_routes_wave22.py` **8 passed**; `tests/test_ui_preferences_webapp_parity9u.py` **14 passed**; `tests/test_qubit_routes_parity8u.py` **22 passed, 1 pre-existing** (`test_schedules_runs_read_equals_backend`, byte-identical known failure). Pre-existing in this worktree only (not caused here): missing `frontend/qubit-dist` (dashboard-bundle routes) and the uninstalled `memory-wiki` plugin in `test_dashboard_plugin_fleet.py`; the root runner's `pythonpath = tests` shadows `qubit_state` for `tests/test_qubit_plugins.py`. **MoA read-only parity lane `fix10/parity-ui` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix10-parity-ui`, base `a8b37e7ba`):** Closed the remaining Hermes model-surface gap honestly. **Measured:** the reference/vendored `GET /api/model/moa` is `normalize_moa_config(cfg["moa"])` (named `presets`, `default_preset`, `active_preset`, flattened preset keys, `privacy_filter`); Qubit really executes MoA (`agent/moa_loop.py`, wired through `agent/turn_request_assembly.py`), and the live profile's raw `config.yaml` already has an enabled `moa.presets.default` — `build_model_options_payload` advertises `moa`/`default` there. The reference-literal `/api/model/moa` is a token-gated front door to the same handler; the dashboard reaches it via `/agent/api/model/moa`. Models page: real preset names in the card, `View` opens a reader-only modal (edits disabled, no Save) with the edit path stated. **Verified (focused):** new `tests/test_moa_readonly_surface10.py` **7 passed** (real presets, disabled-preset opt-out, engine-config parity, no fabrication, side-effect-free read, PUT refusal, literal-route auth+serving); `test_dashboard_audit_truthfulness9u.py` + `test_reference_parity_fix4.py` + `test_qubit_routes_parity8u.py` + the literal route **56 passed, 1 failed** only the pre-existing `test_schedules_runs_read_equals_backend` (documented at base); `test_qubit_vendor8tf.py::test_compat_read_endpoints_smoke` passes with the known unraisable-sqlite GC filter; `ModelsPage.test.tsx` **6 passed**; `tsc -b` exit 0; eslint 0 errors (2 pre-existing warnings). **Dashboard chat navigation browser contract `fix10/e2e` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix10-e2e`, base `a8b37e7ba`):** Added `tests/test_chat_session_navigation_e2e10.py`, three Playwright tests against a hermetic `WebUI` listener serving the real SPA. Each test uses a throwaway `QUBIT_HOME`/`state.db` (the root conftest isolation fixture) and archives its seeded/created sessions; no operator session is read or written. The session token is scraped from the served shell (`window.__QUBIT_SESSION_TOKEN__`) and used for an authenticated read, exactly as the SPA does. **Browser (system Chrome via `channel="chrome"`, bundled fallback):** `/qubit-chat` right panel — recent row click changes `?resume=` to that session and renders its persisted user+assistant rows; `New chat` hits `POST /api/qubit/chat/sessions`, opens `/qubit-chat?resume=<new-id>`, paints the empty state, and the served API resolves the new row; `/sessions` — History row `Resume in Chat` navigates to `/chat?resume=<id>`, the page's real resume probe reads `/api/sessions/<id>` 200, the right panel highlights that row, the single-use PTY ticket carries the id, and the server spawn seam is asked to resume exactly that id. The terminal PTY spawn is a controlled double (same seam as the vendored `pty_ws` tests) so no Ink TUI is booted; the `/chat` right panel is additionally covered: recent click resumes, `New chat` clears `?resume`, emits `fresh=1` with no `resume=` on the WS URL, and spawns an empty resume. **Command/result:** `KEEP_NODE_MODULES=1 bash scripts/build_dashboard.sh` (bundle built, then `node_modules` removed) and `python3 -m pytest -q tests/test_chat_session_navigation_e2e10.py` → **3 passed** in ~13 s. No failing path on this base. Incidental observation outside the three paths: a bare `/qubit-chat` with no stored session probes the implicit `dashboard-chat` and logs one 404; the tests open a real seeded session, so it is not part of this contract. No service restart, no full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Dashboard cold-read cache lane `fix9/cache2` (2026-11-05, base `b2696b52b`):** Closed the remaining cold page-load reads that were not in the first cache wave. **New snapshots (bounded + invalidatable, profile/home-scoped keys built with `read_cache.cfg_key`, file stamps folded in):** `h_gateway` (key: `gateway_state.json`, drain marker, durable control record; TTL 30 s; invalidated by start/stop/restart via `h_gateway_supervision` and drain/cancel via `h_gateway_drain`), `h_approvals_list` (1.9 MB journal; key: `approvals.jsonl` stamp; TTL 30 s; `ApprovalJournal.create/respond/finish/abort/sweep_expired` invalidate), `h_model_info` (runtime `config.yaml` + profiles/sessions stamps; TTL 300 s; `h_model_set`/aux set invalidate), `h_toolsets` (runtime config + `toolsets.json`; TTL 300 s; `apply_toolset_mutation` invalidates), `h_qubit_update_check` (request source/channel/tag + `VERSION` + `.git/HEAD`; TTL 300 s; `?force=1` bypasses; update apply/rollback invalidate), `h_config` (override store + voice/runtime config stamps; TTL 300 s; `config_save` invalidates config/model/toolsets). Only 2xx payloads are stored, so typed refusals are retried. **Proxy-first `/api/config`:** the served payload is cached at the WebUI boundary in the same `config` namespace, keyed by method/path/query + config stamps, so the vendored payload/redaction shape is unchanged and `config_save` clears it too. **Prewarm:** `_prewarm_reads` now includes all six new handlers plus a `/api/config` proxy warm (which also starts the ASGI bridge off the request path); a `live` group (gateway, approvals) refreshes every 20 s and the `slow` group every 240 s, both background/non-blocking, env-overridable (`QUBIT_WEBUI_PREWARM_INTERVAL_S`, `_LIVE_INTERVAL_S`). **Measured isolated (direct handler timing against the live profile, cache off → prewarmed hit):** gateway **1269 ms → 6.6 ms**, approvals **60.2 → 2.0**, model info **595 → 3.1**, toolsets **1398 → 19.0**, update check **2247 → 1.8**, config compat **285 → 5.2**, proxy `/api/config` (incl. first bridge start) **3356 → 20.7**. **Focused:** new `tests/test_read_cache9_cache2.py` **10 passed** (hit, query scoping, proxy scoping/mtime, refusal-not-cached, gateway/approval/model/toolset/config invalidation); `test_read_cache8.py` + new + `test_webui_prewarm8.py` + `test_webui_static_gzip.py` **35 passed**; broader focused set (toolset catalog/authority, approvals CLI, gateway status, config secret skip, update surfaces) **75 passed**; final combined set **47 passed**; `py_compile`/`git diff --check` clean. No UI change, no service restart, no full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Dashboard chat launch freshness lane `fix9/tui` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix9-tui`, base `b2696b52b`, HEAD `c4c9810d4`):** Every dashboard new/resumed chat resolves the TUI argv through `qubit_cli.web_server_chat._resolve_chat_argv` → `main_tui_launch._make_tui_argv` → `_tui_need_rebuild` → `source_build.source_product_current`, which spawned PM's Node + `scripts/build/freshness.mjs` to re-hash the compiler inputs on **every** launch. `source_build.py` now caches the compiler's verdict in-process while a cheap fingerprint (receipt `mtime_ns`/size + newest mtime over a conservative superset of `tuiInputs`: `ui-tui`, `apps/shared`, root `package.json`/`package-lock.json`/`tsconfig.json`/`.npmrc`, `pm/lock.json`, `scripts/build`, plus the `dist` output) is unchanged. A source edit, a rebuilt receipt, or a post-build output change misses the cache and asks the compiler again; only compiler-produced answers are stored, so a stale bundle can never be cached current. The 60 s max age (`QUBIT_SOURCE_FRESHNESS_TTL_S`) bounds an mtime-preserving change; `build_source_tui` invalidates explicitly. Only `product == "tui"` is fingerprinted; packaged installs keep the existing no-probe fast path in `_tui_need_rebuild`. **Measured (same direct-function harness, throwaway `QUBIT_HOME` + throwaway 40-message session, PM node pinned, warm medians of 7; before = cache disabled/`QUBIT_SOURCE_FRESHNESS_TTL_S=0`):** freshness check **278.0 → 8.3–15.5 ms**, TUI argv resolve **328.5 → 11.2 ms**, new-chat resolve **488.0 → 14.8 ms**, resume resolve **343.6 → 16.1–27.9 ms**, resume transcript read unchanged at ~2 ms. A stale real receipt still rebuilt (`_make_tui_argv` first call: node-deps + TUI build) and then resolved at ~20–70 ms. Node/Ink startup remains ~0.4–0.6 s to first byte (dashboard attach mode) and is untouched. **Verified (focused):** new `tests/qubit_cli/test_source_build_freshness_cache9v.py` **6 passed** (current verdict reused with no probe, source edit re-probes, rebuilt receipt never reuses the old verdict, env disable, current launch skips deps/build, stale launch prepares+builds); combined `test_source_build_freshness_cache9v.py test_tui_packaged_layout.py test_tui_npm_install.py test_web_server_chat_workspaces.py test_webapp_renderer_build.py test_update_post_commit_followups.py test_desktop_native_deps_stale.py` **58 passed, 1 skipped**; `test_source_build.py` **20 passed**; `test_web_server.py -k resolve_chat_argv` 1 passed; `test_web_server_profile_unification.py -k "chat_argv or pty"` 7 passed; `ruff` and `py_compile` clean. Pre-existing at base: `tests/test_rebase_c1_fork_patches.py::test_tui_force_build_qubit_alias` fails because base `_tui_need_rebuild` reads only `HERMES_TUI_FORCE_BUILD` (not touched). No live `:8890` restart, no full matrix, no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Resumed dashboard-chat load lane `fix9/session` (2026-11-05, base `b2696b52b`):** Traced `/chat?resume=<id>` → `_resolve_chat_argv` (`_session_latest_descendant`) → PTY/TUI `HERMES_TUI_RESUME` → `session.resume` (`_resume_locate`/`_resume_follow_tip`/`_resume_guard`/`_resume_cold`/`restore`) → `_history_to_messages` → TUI `toTranscriptMessages` (`MAX_HISTORY = 800`, full transcript stays in SQLite). **TUI/dashboard startup freshness fast path (2026-11-05, branch `fix8/tui-start`, base `f88e271e6`):** Traced `_make_tui_argv` → `_tui_need_rebuild` → `source_product_current` and closed the remaining freshness-probe hole for packaged installs. A new `_tui_is_packaged_bundle(tui_dir)` centralises the packaged-layout test (compiled `ui-tui/dist/entry.js`, no `.git` at the runtime root, no `scripts/build/tui.mjs`) and `_make_tui_argv` uses it for its early return. `_tui_need_rebuild` now short-circuits a packaged bundle that carries `dist/hermes-build.json` **before importing `qubit_cli.source_build`/PM or spawning `scripts/build/freshness.mjs`**; an unreceipted bundle still takes the source check (preserving `test_unreceipted_bundle_is_stale_even_when_newer`), and a real checkout (build script present) still checks/rebuilds. Also moved the module-level `from agent.i18n import t` in `main_tui_launch` into `_print_tui_exit_summary`, so importing the launch module no longer loads the agent package (~14 ms median on a fresh interpreter), and moved the `qubit --version` fast path in `voicebridge/cli.main` ahead of the legacy-env adapter/aliasing. **Measured (fresh interpreter, isolated worktree, PM node pinned):** packaged `_tui_need_rebuild` **177 ms / 1 node subprocess / returned True** before → **0.07 ms / 0 subprocess / False** after; packaged `_make_tui_argv` unchanged at **0.13 ms / 0 subprocess** (its guard already existed at base); `import qubit_cli.main_tui_launch` median **65.7 ms → 51.6 ms** (min 45.9 → 36.2). The checkout dashboard path still pays the freshness probe by design. Focused tests: `test_tui_packaged_layout.py` 7, `test_tui_npm_install.py` + `test_tui_bundled.py` + `test_tui_resume_flow.py` + `test_tui_exit_summary_short_id.py` + `test_tui_launcher_skips_plugin_discovery.py` + `test_dashboard_tui_backcompat.py` + `test_cli_tui_i18n_lazy_labels.py` + `test_web_server_chat_workspaces.py` + `test_exit_summary_resume_hint.py` **58 passed**; `tests/test_startup_fast7.py` 10 passed; `py_compile`/`git diff --check` clean. Not done: full matrix, service restart, live dashboard rebuild, provenance manifest regeneration (base already carries 47 stale hashes from earlier lanes); `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Dashboard HTTP/1.1 keep-alive lane `fix8/http` (2026-11-05, base `f88e271e6`):** `WebUIHandler` now serves HTTP/1.1 keep-alive instead of the stdlib HTTP/1.0 default, so a browser reuses one socket for the shell, JSON API and static assets. **Framing:** `_send`, `_send_file_ranged`, `_redirect` and OPTIONS all emit `Content-Length`; a new `end_headers` audit forces `Connection: close` and `close_connection` for any response that cannot guarantee framing; `handle_one_request` resets the per-request `_head_only`/status flags (without it a HEAD poisoned the next GET on the same socket). **Streams:** `/api/events/stream` and the POST chat SSE now explicitly say `Connection: close`; the office `/api/stream` keeps `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` + close. **Head/body:** `/api/activity` no longer writes its body on HEAD. **Cache:** content-hashed `/assets/` remain `public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, now with a weak `ETag` + `Last-Modified` and `If-None-Match`/`If-Modified-Since` → 304; stable-name assets keep the 1 h cache and validators. **Socket hygiene:** `disable_nagle_algorithm = True` removes the ~40 ms Nagle/delayed-ACK stall on reused sockets, and `WebUI.stop()` now shuts down tracked accepted sockets so idle keep-alive handler threads exit. **Measured (throwaway loopback `WebUI`, never `:8890`):** before, six static requests opened **6 distinct client ports**, HTTP/1.0, `will_close=true`; after, six static requests used **1 client port**, HTTP/1.1, `will_close=false`, with `ETag`/`Last-Modified`; reuse median ~0.011 s vs ~0.031 s for fresh connections across three trials. **Focused tests:** new `tests/test_webui_http11_keepalive9u.py` **7 passed** (reuse, exact Content-Length, HEAD no body, immutable+304, SSE close, token shell no-store, HTTP/1.0 close); combined `test_webui_http11_keepalive9u.py test_webui_static_gzip.py test_webui_token_bootstrap9u.py test_webui.py test_webui_media_backdrop.py test_webui_static_confinement9u.py test_events_sse_chat_feed.py test_office_backend_transport9u.py test_office_stream_auth.py` → **69 passed**; `py_compile` and `git diff --check` clean. No live service restart, no release/publish, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed. **Risk:** tests that hold a `urllib`/`http.client` response open now need to close it (HTTP/1.1 keeps the socket), otherwise pytest's `filterwarnings=error` turns the deferred ResourceWarning into a failure; the focused `test_webui.py` clients were updated, and a full-matrix hygiene sweep of the other WebUI test files was not run.

**Dashboard read-cache lane `fix8/cache` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix8-cache`, base `f88e271e6`):** Added `voicebridge/read_cache.py` (bounded ≤128-entry, per-namespace TTL, explicit invalidation, deep-copy on store/return, disabled with `QUBIT_READ_CACHE=0`, per-namespace override `QUBIT_READ_CACHE_TTL_<NS>`) and wired the slow read-only dashboard components through it without changing handler shapes or typed refusals: `/api/terminal/profiles` (whole row list, key includes the profiles registry mtime), `/api/skills` (`skill_catalog.catalog_entries`, key includes the runtime `config.yaml` disabled set and `permissions.json`; `clear_skills_prompt_cache`/permissioned toggle invalidate), `/api/mcp/servers` (`mcp_servers.list_servers`, key includes the registry, runtime config and MCP schema-cache mtimes; `_commit`, discovery persist and `set_auth_ref` invalidate), `/api/model/options` (final secret-stripped payload keyed by query args + runtime config/auth/profiles; `refresh=1` bypasses and re-warms), `/api/office/tower` (whole payload; only when the caller does not pin `?now=`; key includes `office.db`+WAL), and the inventory components behind `/api/status`/`/api/runtime` (`device_inventory`, `_marketplace`, `_vision`, plus the shared MCP listing). Every key folds in the resolved runtime home, the config state dir, the config path and its `(mtime_ns,size)`, so entries cannot cross profiles/homes. The cache is off under pytest unless `QUBIT_READ_CACHE=1` (one-shot reads must not perturb strict GC/warning suites); the new focused file enables it explicitly. **Before (base `f88e271e6`, same isolated process + copied office DB, warm median of 5):** terminal profiles 39 ms (cold 1,045), skills 404, MCP 210, model options 1,004, office tower 1,598, status 1,266, runtime 955. **After (same harness, cache warm):** terminal **1.6–3.2 ms**, skills **2.5–4.0**, MCP **20–31**, model options **6–23**, office tower **1.7–2.2**, status **120–172**, runtime **13–31**. **Verified (focused):** new `tests/test_read_cache8.py` **10 passed** (hit/miss, TTL expiry, namespace invalidation, env disable, profile-scoped keys, deep-copy isolation, model-options refresh bypass + invalidation, office pinned-clock bypass, MCP write invalidation, skills mutation hook, cached-vs-uncached payload equality). A 15-file handler suite ran **358 passed / 10 failed** on this branch and the byte-identical **348 passed / 10 failed** on base `f88e271e6` (the 10 are pre-existing strict-`ResourceWarning` HTTPError/sqlite GC noise in `test_dashboard_matrix9u`/`test_chat_terminal_parity9u`); `py_compile` clean; `ruff` reports only the two pre-existing errors in untouched lines. No service restart, no live `:8890`, no full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Dashboard first-load/navigation performance lane `fix7/perf` (2026-11-05, base `c60941b78`):** Kept the live services untouched and measured read-only against Qubit `:8890` and the Hermes reference `:9119`, then rebuilt in the isolated worktree and served the result from a temporary loopback `WebUI`. **Baseline `/chat` first load** (browser performance resources): Qubit **688,766 B** transferred incl. HTML, 23 `/assets/` requests; Hermes `/` **1,258,875 B**, 10 assets. **After:** `/chat` **558,620 B** (assets 538,603 + gzipped shell ~4 KB in the temp profile; asset-only **415,664 B**, `-23.9%` from Qubit's 546,123 B), with the initial translation dictionary cut from **143,425 B gzip** (all 17 locales) to **13,465 B** (`en` 11,323 + loader 2,142). **Reload/route navigation:** baseline Qubit assets were `Cache-Control: no-store`, so a second `/chat` load re-transferred **668,766 B**; after the change every content-hashed `/assets/` file is `public, max-age=31536000, immutable` and the same browser reload transferred **0 B** (24 cache hits). **Root causes fixed:** (1) `scripts/build_dashboard.sh` exported `NODE_ENV=development` for the install and never reset it, so Vite bundled **development React** (`react-vendor` 430,037 B / 130 KB gzip, dev warnings) instead of production (225,735 B / 72 KB gzip); (2) all locale dictionaries were eagerly imported at startup; (3) `_send` forced `no-store` on every static body and gzip was recomputed per request. **Changes:** production `NODE_ENV` for tsc/vite; lazy per-locale chunks with the saved locale preloaded before first paint; immutable headers for `/assets/` (1 h for stable-name media/plugins; the shell stays `no-store` and is now gzipped without a cache key so per-request tokens cannot go stale); bounded 24 MiB / 512-entry gzip-body LRU keyed by `(path, mtime_ns, size)`. **Verified (focused, no service restart):** rebuilt dashboard is byte-for-byte production React (`Minified React error` present, dev warning strings absent); `tests/test_webui_static_gzip.py` **13 passed** (immutable, short cache, no-store shell, gzip reuse, no stale token reuse); `src/i18n/context.test.tsx` **4 passed**; `tests/test_webui_static_gzip.py tests/test_qbpv2_frontend_identity.py tests/test_packet1b.py tests/test_events_sse_chat_feed.py` **52 passed, 1 failed** only because the Playwright Chromium binary is absent; live `:8890`/`:9119` processes were not restarted or modified; `node_modules` removed after the build. No full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Windows release bootstrap lane `fix5/winmac` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix5-winmac`, base `b257b1b08`):** Added `scripts/bootstrap.ps1` and `scripts/bootstrap.cmd`, mirroring the verified POSIX bootstrap contract with a ZIP payload: download `install.ps1` + `qubit-release.zip` + `SHA256SUMS`, verify both SHA-256 values, extract the ZIP with entry-level safety checks (absolute/drive/`..` paths and symlink entries rejected; no write outside the temp dir), verify the extracted `scripts/install.ps1` byte-for-byte, and execute only that extracted copy. Flags: `-DryRun`, `-Prefix`/`-QubitHome` (same root; disagreement is a typed usage error), `-Profile` (exported as `QUBIT_PROFILE`), `-NoModels`/`-WithoutModels`, `-NoDashboard`/`-WithoutDashboard`, `-Yes` (forwarded as `-NonInteractive`), `-Archive`, `-Asset`, `-ChecksumFile`. `-WithSystemd` is an explicit typed refusal (`BOOTSTRAP_OPTION_UNSUPPORTED`) because Windows has no systemd/launchd equivalent. `scripts/prepare_release_seed.py` now also emits `Qubit-<version>-seed.zip` via `git archive --format=zip`, excluding tracked symlinks (the Windows extractor refuses symlink entries), and publishes `install.ps1` + `qubit-release.zip` beside the POSIX assets under one `SHA256SUMS`. `INSTALL.md`/`README.md` document the Windows contract and the ZIP choice. **Verified (focused, hermetic under mise `pwsh` 7.6.9 on Linux):** `python3 -m pytest tests/test_bootstrap_installer_windows.py` → **39 passed** (local directory, `file://`, loopback HTTP, prefixed and flat ZIPs, custom archive name, success, install/archive/extracted mismatch, missing archive/asset/checksum, archive not named, explicit checksum, dry-run no writes/no execution, missing-asset dry-run, isolated copy with no checkout, unsafe `..` entry, archive without installer, exit propagation, checksum naming another asset, prefix/QubitHome disagreement, `-WithSystemd` refusal, CMD wrapper); combined `tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py tests/test_bootstrap_installer_windows.py tests/test_prepare_release_seed.py tests/test_root_installer_windows.py tests/test_root_installer_release.py tests/test_installer_integrity_wave25.py` → **133 passed, 1 skipped**; `test_prepare_release_seed.py` now covers the new ZIP archive and both release installers. **Not verified:** no real Windows host, no Windows PowerShell 5.1 execution, no real Windows install/bootstrap, no macOS launchd run; PowerShell 7 on Linux is a bootstrap test host only. No services, live config, production repo, release, or publish touched; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Public release verifier lane `fix5/release` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix5-release`, base `b257b1b08`):** Added the read-only `scripts/verify_public_release.py`. Given `--repo <owner/name> --tag vX.Y.Z` (or `--latest`) or a `--release-base` (HTTPS, `file://`, or local directory), it checks release existence/published state, tag/version identity, every required asset (`bootstrap.sh`, `install.sh`, `qubit-release.tar.gz`, `SHA256SUMS`, wheel, sdist, source archive), every `SHA256SUMS` entry, that both source archives carry `scripts/install.sh` byte-identical to the `install.sh` asset plus a matching `VERSION`, wheel/sdist metadata, `component-manifest.json` identity (when present), and the source archive metadata/bounded content sample for `/home/trick`, `node_modules`/build directories, secret filenames and realistic secret tokens. It downloads into a temporary directory and deletes it; it writes nothing to the release, checkout, service, or remote. Typed PASS/FAIL lines, `--json`, exit 0/1/2; the GitHub API can be pointed at a stub with `--api-base`. **Verified (hermetic):** `tests/test_verify_public_release.py` → **15 passed** (local directory, loopback HTTP, loopback GitHub API, checksum mismatch, missing asset, archive-installer mismatch, operator path, `node_modules`, secret file, realistic secret token, documented placeholders allowed, wheel/sdist version mismatch, expected-source-commit mismatch, missing release, URL normalization); `git diff --check` clean. **Real v0.0.2 evidence (read-only, 2026-11-05):** `python3 scripts/verify_public_release.py --repo cgraph-dev/Qubit --tag v0.0.2 --json` → **PASS**, **41 checks / 0 failed**; source commit `947e442f1eec4f4b70751652e4da07ee20a4006a`; all 9 published `SHA256SUMS` entries matched; both `qubit-release.tar.gz` and `Qubit-0.0.2-source.tar.gz` contain `scripts/install.sh` sha256 `90e0bf0fdc606d94693a7353c5410cc4930a78ad3b1b4a8391ed276eea8a5ac5` (the asset) and `VERSION` `0.0.2`; the 7,966-file / 67,112,732-byte source sample had no operator path, `node_modules`, or secret. This verifies published asset integrity/provenance only; it is not a behavioral `VERIFIED` verdict. Docs updated: `INSTALL.md` (release verification section, archive example), `README.md` (verification command, precise Windows status). **Local CI runner lane `fix5/ci` (2026-11-05, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix5-ci`, base `b257b1b08`):** Added `scripts/run_local_ci.py`, a deterministic local reproduction of the actionable hosted jobs while GitHub-hosted execution remains blocked account-side. Jobs: `workflow-lint` (actionlint if present + `check_workflow_security.py`), `lockfiles` (npm `ci --dry-run` over the three 10.9.8 lockfiles and the website lockfile), `runtime-smoke` (compile + runtime-test parse + root metadata), `frontend` (install/typecheck/test/build), `bootstrap-tests` (`bash -n` + `tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py`), and opt-in `release-qualification` / `install-e2e`. `--job`/`--list`/`--json`/`--dry-run` are supported; groups are `fast` and `all`; opt-in jobs refuse (exit 8 BLOCKED) without `--allow-opt-in`; forbidden publish/tag/service verbs are refused before execution; missing required tools BLOCK, a missing `actionlint` DEGRADES. Every report carries `"execution": "local"` and `"hosted_equivalent": false`; nothing publishes, uploads, or controls a service. **Concrete workflow defect fixed:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` `runtime-smoke` compiled the non-existent `qubit_runtime/qubit_state` directory; `python -m compileall` prints `Can't list 'qubit_runtime/qubit_state'` and exits 0, so all `qubit_state*.py` modules were silently skipped. The workflow now compiles `qubit_runtime/qubit_state*.py`, and the local runner expands that glob and BLOCKS if no modules exist. **Measured (clean committed tree, Python 3.14.7 / Node 22.22.3 / npm 10.9.8 / actionlint 1.7.12):** `--job workflow-lint` PASS (13 managed workflows); `--job lockfiles` DEGRADED (three lockfiles pass; website lockfile requires npm >= 11.17.0, so it is skipped rather than silently passed); `--job runtime-smoke` PASS; `--job frontend` PASS (install, typecheck, tests, build); `--job bootstrap-tests` PASS (31 tests); `--job release-qualification --allow-opt-in` PASS (`release_qualify.py` build/install/reproduce, byte-identical wheel rebuild); `--job install-e2e --allow-opt-in` PASS (`qubit 0.0.2`, dashboard build + artifact install E2E). Refusal paths: opt-in without the flag → BLOCKED exit 8; unknown job → BLOCKED exit 8. `ruff check scripts/run_local_ci.py tests/test_run_local_ci.py` clean; `pytest -q tests/test_run_local_ci.py` → 20 passed. Full runbook and the hosted/local distinction: `docs/LOCAL-CI.md`. No full matrix, no hosted run, no publish, no service restart; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Self-contained release bootstrap lane `next/self-contained` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-next-bootstrap`, base `8d5de4acf`):** Closed the release bootstrap blocker without faking a release. `scripts/bootstrap.sh` previously downloaded only `install.sh` into a temp dir, but `scripts/install.sh` is checkout-relative (`PROJECT_DIR` from its own path), so the copy could not install. The bootstrap now downloads and verifies **both** `install.sh` and a release source archive, extracts the archive into its temp dir, verifies that the extracted `scripts/install.sh` is byte-identical to the verified `install.sh`, and executes that extracted copy (so `PROJECT_DIR` resolves inside the extracted release tree). The contract is `install.sh`, `qubit-release.tar.gz` (override `--archive NAME`/`QUBIT_RELEASE_ARCHIVE`), and `SHA256SUMS` naming both; the archive may put `scripts/install.sh` at its root or under one top-level directory. Missing installer, missing archive, missing/invalid checksum entry, archive mismatch, extracted-installer mismatch, unsafe archive paths, and archives without `scripts/install.sh` all fail closed with typed exits. `scripts/prepare_release_seed.py` now publishes the seed archive into its release base as `qubit-release.tar.gz` alongside `install.sh` and a checksum naming both. `INSTALL.md`, `README.md`, and the `bootstrap-installer` workflow were updated to the same contract. **Verified (focused):** `python3 -m pytest tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py` → **31 passed** (local directory, `file://`, loopback HTTP, prefixed and flat archives, custom archive name, success, install/archive/extracted mismatch, missing archive, missing asset, missing checksum, archive not named, explicit checksum, dry-run no writes, missing archive dry-run, run from an isolated copy with no checkout, unsafe archive path, archive without installer, exit propagation, unsupported hosts); with `tests/test_prepare_release_seed.py tests/test_root_installer_release.py tests/test_installer_integrity_wave25.py tests/test_clean_clone_wave5.py` → **72 passed, 1 skipped**; `tests/test_workflow_security.py` → **14 passed**; `bash -n scripts/bootstrap.sh` and `actionlint` clean; `prepare_release_seed.py` compiles. **Remaining public-hosting prerequisite:** publish the bootstrap script itself plus `install.sh`, `qubit-release.tar.gz`, and the `SHA256SUMS` naming both at a stable public release base; no public one-line URL exists yet. No live service/config, no full matrix, no publish; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

PyJWT RS256 with `cryptography` 50.0.1); `tests/test_doc_extract8u.py` 18 passed/1 skipped; mem0 backend + proxy 37 passed; WhatsApp node tests 25 + Python bridge security 9; Photon 150 + all `.mjs` `node --check`; frontend `typecheck` clean, **157 files / 1255 tests passed**, build OK; TUI `npm ci`, build OK, `node --check` OK, tests **1636 passed / 1 pre-existing failure** (`modelPicker.test.ts`, byte-identical to base), one pre-existing typecheck error (`createGatewayEventHandler.ts`); `git diff --check` clean. **Remaining 302 alerts / 154 GHSAs (24 critical):** `Pillow` 12.3.0→13.0.0 (major, 123 alerts / 5 critical), `simple-git` 3.36.0→4.0.2 (major, 7 incl. critical), `electron` 28 (major), `filelock` 12 (major), `oauthlib` 4 (major), `opentelemetry-api` 1 (constrained), `PyJWT`/`deepmerge`/`extract-zip` no-fix advisories, `vite`/`axios`/`vitest` dev/build, and the Docusaurus docs site 53 (not in the wheel). **Production release assets lane `next/release` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-next-release`, base `8d5de4acf`):** Prepared the first `cgraph-dev/Qubit` production release assets without publishing. **Version decision:** production's only tag is `v0.0.1` (target `ef3b9e1b`; production `VERSION` and pyproject are `0.0.1`), so the next release is **`0.0.2` / `v0.0.2`**; the old local `v0.0.1` and the unrelated local `v1.0.0` tag are not moved. Bumped `pyproject.toml`, `voicebridge.__version__`, `voicebridge.distribution.PACKAGE_VERSION`, `voicebridge.product.PRODUCT_VERSION`, and added `VERSION` (`0.0.2`) to the public export allowlist. **New tooling:** `scripts/prepare_production_release.py` runs `release_qualify.py --build --install --reproduce --unsigned-ok`, exports the sanitized public source tree into a deterministic `Qubit-<version>-source.tar.gz`, and writes `component-manifest.json`, `release-notes.md`, `SHA256SUMS` and `release-summary.json`; it refuses a dirty tree and refuses to move an existing tag. **New workflow:** `.github/workflows/promote-release.yml` is `workflow_dispatch` only, checks out the existing tag, builds/verifies the assets, and (behind the `release` environment) creates or updates a **DRAFT** release with `gh release create --draft --verify-tag` or uploads to an existing draft; it refuses an already-published release and has no publish/tag/push step. **Verified:** `python3 scripts/prepare_production_release.py --out /tmp/qubit-production-release --tag v0.0.2 --verify` → `RELEASE_PREP_OK`; release qualifier `PASS` (wheel and sdist content scans, 18/18 install checks, byte-identical wheel rebuild); `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS` OK; the wheel carries the dashboard (including its build info), TUI dist and vendored runtime with `Version: 0.0.2`; the source archive carries `VERSION` and `PUBLIC_TREE_MANIFEST.md` and no `.git`/`node_modules`/`/home/trick`/internal docs. Focused tests: `tests/test_production_release.py` 9, `tests/test_workflow_security.py` 16, `tests/test_export_public_tree.py` 9, plus `test_prepare_release_seed.py`/`test_packet8k.py`/`test_version_help_rebrand_wave24.py` all pass; `check_workflow_security.py` OK (12 managed), `actionlint` clean. Exact asset names/hashes are in `/tmp/qubit-production-release/release-summary.json` and `SHA256SUMS`. **Not done:** no tag/release created or pushed, no GitHub upload, no live service/config, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Public promotion lane `next/promote` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-next-promote`, base `8d5de4acf`):** Added a repeatable Qubit-AIO → `cgraph-dev/Qubit` production promotion path without touching a live remote, service, or config. `scripts/promote_public_release.py` exports a chosen source commit through a temporary detached worktree (so `export_public_tree.py` still reads tracked Git objects, never the working tree), reuses `prepare_release_seed.py`'s export/prune/verify/commit/tag/archive functions, verifies the seed has no operator paths, model weights, secrets, or non-allowlisted docs, and writes JSON+Markdown reports with the source commit/tree, seed commit/tree, SHA-256 archive checksum, export counts, pruned build refs, remote state, and changed paths. It is dry-run by default; `--apply` requires `--remote-url`, refuses `main`/`master`/every branch except `production`, and pushes with an explicit `--force-with-lease=<branch>:<observed-sha>` (empty expects a new branch), then re-reads the remote to confirm the seed commit. `--push-tag` pushes the release tag without force and refuses to rewrite an existing different tag. Added `.github/workflows/promote-public.yml` (workflow_dispatch only; dry-run job by default, apply job behind the `public-production` environment and a SHA-pinned action set, token injected via a git `insteadOf` rewrite rather than argv) and an explicit `SECRET_WORKFLOWS` allowlist in `.github/scripts/check_workflow_security.py` (`promote-public.yml` may read only `QUBIT_PUBLIC_PUSH_TOKEN`; every other managed workflow still may read no secrets), pinned by `tests/test_workflow_security.py`. **Verified (focused, hermetic):** `tests/test_promote_public_release.py` **8 passed** (chosen-commit export, personal-path/model/internal-doc exclusion, `--apply` remote requirement, `main` refusal, production push + tag, second force-with-lease update, dry-run changed paths/no remote write, version mismatch refusal) using local bare repositories only; `tests/test_workflow_security.py` **16 passed**; `.github/scripts/check_workflow_security.py` → **OK (12 managed workflows)**; `actionlint .github/workflows/promote-public.yml` clean; combined export/seed/workflow set **36 passed**. A real dry-run on this tree (`--source-commit HEAD --dest /tmp/promote-final`) succeeded: source `dc32e1ed1c7d`, seed `9f18c391b2b4`, 24,317 included / 788 excluded, archive sha256 `a238690f…`, one-commit clean seed tagged `v0.0.1`. Seed commit/tag dates are pinned to the source commit's date, so the same source+version+tag reproduces the same seed commit and archive checksum (pinned by a two-run test). **Owner command (dry-run by default; add `--apply` only to push):** `python3 scripts/promote_public_release.py --source-commit <sha> --version 0.0.1 --remote-url https://github.com/cgraph-dev/Qubit.git` then the same line with `--apply` (and optionally `--push-tag`). **Not done:** no live push, no private-repo write, no service/config change, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Clean source-install rehearsal lane `fix/fix-source-install` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix-source`, base `30ebf896f`):** Ran a real clean source install from a temp copy of the worktree with a throwaway HOME/XDG/prefix and no `/home/trick` state. **Defects found and fixed in `scripts/install.sh`:** (1) `--prefix` launcher did not pin the prefix: running `prefix/bin/qubit` without `QUBIT_HOME` read/created the real HOME's `~/.qubit` (contradicting `INSTALL.md` "`--prefix DIR` ... equivalent to setting `QUBIT_HOME=DIR`"); the launcher now exports a baked default `QUBIT_HOME` for `--prefix`/explicit-home installs (an explicit environment value still wins; a default install stays unpinned). (2) launcher rendering used GNU-only `sed -i`, which fails on supported macOS/BSD sed; it now renders to a new file and `mv`s (no in-place edit). (3) the package was installed **before** the dashboard was built; because the install is non-editable and `voicebridge/resources.py` resolves the bundle from package data first, a one-pass clean source install installed a dashboard-less wheel and served `webui.dashboard_bundle STALE/BROKEN` until a second install run; the dashboard now builds before `install_venv_and_package`. **Rehearsal evidence (fresh copy `/tmp/qubit-install-src2`, throwaway HOME/prefix, `--without-models`, Node 22.22.3):** `install.sh --dry-run --prefix …` → exit **0**, printed the new `$QUBIT_HOME/voice` layout and created nothing (`prefix`, `~/.qubit`, `~/.config` all absent); real `install.sh --prefix … --without-models` → exit **0**, built the dashboard, installed the package and launcher, and created `prefix/voice/{config,data,state,cache,models}` + `voice.yaml`; `prefix/bin/qubit --version` → `qubit 0.0.1` (exit 0) and `prefix/bin/qubit status` → exit 0 with all model paths under `prefix/voice/models` and no `~/.qubit` created; packaged `voicebridge/_data/frontend/qubit-dist/index.html` present (3022 bytes) and `check_qubit_bundle` → `ok=True, issues=[]`. **Focused tests:** new `tests/test_install_source_rehearsal9v.py` **5 passed** (prefix/explicit pin, default unpinned, BSD-sed shim, dashboard-before-package order); existing `test_root_installer_release.py`, `test_macos_install_path.py`, `test_voice_paths_new_install9v.py`, `test_installer_integrity_wave25.py` **67 passed**; `test_clean_clone_wave5.py` + `test_bootstrap_installer.py` **25 passed, 1 skipped**; `test_root_installer_windows.py` **22 passed, 5 skipped**. **Limitation observed, not a code change:** the host's system Node 18.19.1 passes the presence-only preflight but the pinned Vite 8 toolchain needs Node `^20.19 || >=22.12`; the build fails and the installer exits **4** (typed `DASHBOARD_UNAVAILABLE`) — a supported Node 22.22.3 completes the standard path. **Not done:** no model assets, no systemd/launchd, no live service/config, no full matrix, no publish; **Public CI port lane `fix/fix-ci` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix-ci`, base `30ebf896f`):** Ported/adapted high-value upstream Hermes CI without copying the 57-workflow set. **New managed workflows:** `install-e2e.yml` (Linux wheel artifact installed into a throwaway venv/HOME via `scripts/artifact_install_e2e.py --build --require-network`; dashboard built first, no checkout on sys.path), `bootstrap-installer.yml` (hermetic `tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py` plus a real local-release verified execution and a checksum-mismatch refusal, no network), `osv-scanner.yml` (weekly detection-only OSV over the four shipped npm lockfiles; Qubit has no `uv.lock`, so the upstream uv input is intentionally absent). **Rewritten managed (were legacy self-hosted or never-called `workflow_call`):** `docs-site-checks.yml` (GitHub-hosted docs build/deploy-check: `npm ci`, skill extraction, link check, ASCII-diagram lint, `build:fast`; no deploy step, no secret, no write permission; the upstream generator-staleness diff is deliberately not ported because Qubit's committed per-skill pages are measured stale), `blob-size-check.yml`, `case-collision-check.yml`, `lazy-deps-guard.yml` (all now PR/push/dispatch, GitHub-hosted, read-only, SHA-pinned, concurrent). **Hardened:** `ci.yml` gained an npm lockfile-integrity job (`npm ci --dry-run` over the dashboard/runtime/WhatsApp lockfiles with npm 10.9.8, the version they were generated with, and over the website lockfile with npm 11.17.0 because its `.npmrc` is `engine-strict`). `.github/scripts/check_workflow_security.py` legacy allowlist reduced to the three untouched upstream workflows (`qubit-ci`, `windows-ci`, `windows-install-update-e2e`). **New test:** `tests/test_workflow_security.py` **14 passed**; `check_workflow_security.py` → **OK (11 managed workflows)**; `actionlint` clean; PyYAML parses all 14 workflows. Also verified locally: `tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py` **17 passed**, `artifact_install_e2e.py --check` **PASS**, `check_doc_links.py` OK, `ascii-guard` lint 0 errors (468 files), and `npm ci --dry-run` passes for all four lockfiles under their pinned npm versions (10.9.8 for the dashboard/runtime/WhatsApp set, 11.17.0 for the website). **Rejected / not ported:** Docker/Nix/Termux/Rust/desktop workflows (not shipped), the uv-lockfile check (no `uv.lock`), lockfile-diff/review-comment and supply-chain-audit comment orchestration (need `pull-requests: write`/PR comments, forbidden in managed PR workflows), and the full upstream matrix. Windows install/update E2E stays in the existing `windows-installer.yml`/legacy Windows lanes. No workflow was executed, no publish, no secrets, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Public release seed lane `fix/fix-public-seed` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix-seed`, base `30ebf896f`):** Added `scripts/prepare_release_seed.py` and focused tests. The tool exports tracked Git objects through `scripts/export_public_tree.py` (never the working tree), prunes pyproject force-include/artifact entries whose excluded internal-doc or unbuilt sources would break a standalone wheel, writes `VERSION` `0.0.1`, initializes the seed as a fresh single-commit repo tagged `v0.0.1`, verifies no source `.git` history, operator paths, secrets, model weights, or internal docs were copied, builds a wheel from the seed, and exercises the installer/bootstrap help plus a local no-model/no-dashboard install. **Measured seed (`/tmp/qubit-seed`):** tracked 25,094; included **24,285**; excluded **809** (`excluded_dir` 135, `excluded_file` 84, `not_allowlisted` 574, `personal_path` 16); the 16 personal-path exclusions are tests/one probe, no production module; only the four allowlisted public docs ship. The authoritative source commit, seed commit, tag, checksums and install evidence are in `/tmp/qubit-seed-manifest.md`, regenerated with this lane's final HEAD. **Verified (focused):** `tests/test_prepare_release_seed.py` **3 passed**; `install.sh --help`, `bootstrap.sh --help`, `bootstrap.sh --dry-run /tmp/qubit-seed-release`, real local install (`--prefix …/install-prefix --without-models --without-dashboard --yes`) exit 0, and the installed `qubit --version` reports `qubit 0.0.1`. **Pruned build refs (not content fixes):** `frontend/qubit-dist`, `docs/VOICE-BRIDGE-AUDIT.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/MIGRATION.md`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/generated/QUBIT_HERMES_VENDOR_MANIFEST.json`, and two `docs/archive/2026-09-21/*.json` sources were removed from the seed's pyproject because the public export intentionally excludes them. **Known caveat:** the seed's `scripts/install.sh` is project-relative, so a public one-line bootstrap needs a standalone `install.sh` asset or a full-tree release base; the harness proves the checksum/asset path but does not invent a standalone asset. **Not done:** no push, no `cgraph-dev/Qubit` edit/publish, no live service/config, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Bootstrap installer lane `release/final-bootstrap` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-final-bootstrap`, base `1cee62a2c`):** Added the checkout-free entry point `scripts/bootstrap.sh`. It accepts a release base (HTTPS, `file://`, or a local directory), `--profile`, `--prefix`, `--dry-run`, `--checksum FILE`, and `--help`; downloads the release `install.sh` plus checksum into a temp dir, verifies SHA-256 with sha256sum/shasum/openssl, and executes only the verified copy. It fails closed with typed exits: 2 usage, 3 unsupported host, 4 missing tool, 5 download, 6 checksum mismatch, 7 checksum unavailable/invalid; installer failures propagate. Linux is supported now; macOS/Windows are explicit typed refusals. `--dry-run` performs no download, execution, or `QUBIT_HOME` write. `--profile` is exported as `QUBIT_PROFILE`; `--prefix` is forwarded as `--prefix`. The script reads no source checkout. **Docs:** README and `INSTALL.md` §"Bootstrap installer (release assets)" state the remaining public-hosting prerequisite: the release must publish the bootstrap script plus the `install.sh` asset and its SHA-256 checksum at the release base; no public one-line URL exists yet. **Verified (focused, hermetic, no network):** new `tests/test_bootstrap_installer.py` **17 passed** (help/usage, local + `file://` base, verified execution, profile/prefix forwarding, checksum mismatch/missing checksum/missing asset refusal, explicit `--checksum`, dry-run no writes, isolated copy without a checkout, macOS/Windows/unknown typed refusals, missing-curl refusal); existing `tests/test_root_installer_release.py` **16 passed** and `tests/test_installer_integrity_wave25.py` **12 passed**. **Not done:** no publish, no live service/config, no full matrix; HTTP download path is unexercised by tests (local-only by design). `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Final artifact gate lane `release/final-package` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-final-package`, base `1cee62a2c`):** Closed the last artifact-gate blocker and re-ran every release packaging gate on the merged tree. **Real defect fixed:** the sdist was not self-contained, so a wheel rebuilt from it failed with `FileNotFoundError: Forced include not found`. Two causes, both fixed in `pyproject.toml`: (1) `docs/generated/QUBIT_HERMES_VENDOR_MANIFEST.json` is tracked but lives under the git-ignored `docs/generated/`, and hatchling's sdist selection is VCS-based, so the tracked-but-ignored file was dropped even though the wheel force-includes it — it is now an explicit sdist `artifacts` entry; (2) `docs/VOICE-BRIDGE-AUDIT.md` was both a wheel force-include source (installed as the `AUDIO_SETUP` guide) and an sdist `exclude` entry — the contradictory exclusion is removed (the doc is sanitized; the sdist privacy scan passes and still covers it). **Regression pinned:** `tests/test_packaging_wave3.py::test_every_wheel_force_include_source_survives_in_the_sdist` asserts every wheel force-include source is an sdist artifact, mirrored in the sdist force-include table, or left in the default tracked walk, and names both concrete cases. **Commands/results (clean tree, commits `111da4b6f` + `893fc61ff`):** `release_qualify.py --static --tag v0.0.1` → **PASS**; `release_qualify.py --build --unsigned-ok --tag v0.0.1` → **PASS** (wheel 8,757 members, sdist 10,770, dashboard OK, metadata `0.0.1`); `release_qualify.py --install --reproduce --unsigned-ok --tag v0.0.1` → **PASS** (18/18 install smoke checks, byte-identical wheel rebuild, sha256 `a8ca6de7…`); focused packaging set (`test_packaging_wave3`, `test_qbpv_pkg5_packaging`, `test_qbpv2_agent5_packaging`, `test_artifact_install_e2e`, `test_update_packaging9u`, `test_root_installer_release`, `test_root_installer_windows`, `test_release_paths_contract`, `test_public_tree_feature_parity`) → **118 passed, 2 skipped**. **Sdist→wheel proof:** extracted `qubit_voicebridge-0.0.1.tar.gz`, `uv build --wheel` → success; rebuilt wheel has 8,757 members, dashboard `index.html` + 100 assets, all 3 `ui-tui/dist` files, vendored runtime, `AUDIO_SETUP.md`, the vendor manifest, 0 `node_modules`. **Not done / remaining:** artifacts are deliberately unsigned (no signing key;
