**Windows installer finish lane `release/final-win` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-final-win`, base `1cee62a2c`):** Finished and validated the root Windows installer without touching live services/config. **Fixes:** (1) `-QubitHome` drives the default checkout (`InstallDir = <QubitHome>\qubit`) and an explicit `-InstallDir` still wins; the layout now prints `source` alongside `checkout`. (2) A stable-channel rerun on an existing checkout no longer runs `git pull --ff-only` on a detached tag checkout (which failed with "not currently on a branch"); a checkout already at/after the resolved tag is kept, so reruns never downgrade. `Initialize-Checkout` also re-runs the Qubit-source assertion itself (defence in depth). (3) The VoiceBridge tree now matches `voicebridge/paths.py` and `install.sh`: fresh installs create `$QUBIT_HOME/voice/{config,data,state,cache,models}`, the launcher exports those resolved roots as XDG, and an existing `qubit-voicebridge` XDG tree is detected and preserved read-old (never moved/copied/deleted). (4) The runtime config example falls back to `qubit_runtime/cli-config.yaml.example`, which is where a source checkout actually carries it; the previous root-only lookup silently skipped `config.yaml`. Stable tags are still resolved only from the Qubit `RepoUrl` (semver `v?X.Y.Z`, latest wins), and the refused non-Qubit source/typed prerequisite classes are unchanged. **Tests:** `tests/test_root_installer_windows.py` gained static checks (`-QubitHome`→`InstallDir`, stable-tag resolution, detached-rerun guard, new voice tree, runtime-config fallback) plus executed checks that run when a PowerShell host exists: real-parser syntax, `Get-LatestStableTag` semver selection, a local-origin stable clone + rerun (release kept, no downgrade), and `Install-Config` initialising the new voice tree. Added `.github/workflows/windows-installer.yml` (managed: `permissions: contents: read`, concurrency, SHA-pinned actions): static contract + real PowerShell parse + `-Help`/`-DryRun` no-write on every PR/push, and the real checkout install (`-NoModels -NoDashboard`) on `main` pushes and opt-in `workflow_dispatch`, asserting the launcher, `config.yaml`, the voice tree, and no `.hermes` home. **Verified (this Linux host, PowerShell 7.6.9 installed via mise):** `tests/test_root_installer_windows.py` **25 passed, 1 skipped** (the skip is the Windows-5.1 `-Help`/`-DryRun` execution test, which is CI-only); the related installer set (`test_root_installer_release.py`, `test_installer_integrity_wave25.py`, `test_clean_clone_wave5.py`, `test_update_packaging_brand_wave21.py`) **66 passed, 2 skipped**; `qubit_runtime/tests/scripts/test_install_repo_identity.py` + `test_install_ps1_stable_default.py` + `test_powershell_script_syntax.py` **14 passed, 10 skipped**; manual `-DryRun`, `-Help`, scriptblock (`irm|iex` shape) and read-old layouts all exited 0 with no files created; `check_workflow_security.py` **OK (4 managed workflows)** and `actionlint` clean. **Not verified / Windows-only gaps:** no Windows host here, so no real Windows install E2E has passed; the new workflow is the instrument, not evidence. Pinned model downloads, dashboard build on Windows (needs bash/node), update/uninstall, user PATH/ACL behavior, and a full Windows matrix are **unverified**; README/INSTALL keep "validation pending"/not-supported wording and `VERIFIED` is not claimed. Pre-existing at base `1cee62a2c`, not caused here: `test_branding_scan8tf::test_installer_and_examples_use_canonical_paths` fails because the voice-paths lane changed `install.sh` without updating that assertion. No live service/config, no full matrix, no publish.

**Final installer-layout closure lane `release/final-layout` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-final-layout`, base `1cee62a2c`):** Closed the remaining contradictions between the Installer and the single VoiceBridge path resolver. **Runtime:** `voice_layout()` now treats an `uninitialized` machine as a new install: `install_mode == "new"` **and** `effective`/`new` resolve to `$QUBIT_HOME/voice/{config,data,state,cache,models}`. Previously an uninitialized runtime wrote the XDG `qubit-voicebridge` tree first, which the next resolution then misread as a read-old install. `canonical_runtime_home`, `canonical_runtime_env_path`, `qubit_runtime.default_home`, and config default-path rewriting now key off `install_mode` (so uninitialized behaves as new); `runtime_home_for_cfg` returns the canonical runtime home for the layout (custom `logging.jsonl_path` still owns its own runtime child), keeping one `state.db`. `qubit_home()` matches the vendored runtime/Windows installer platform defaults (`~/.qubit` on Linux/macOS, `%LOCALAPPDATA%\qubit` on Windows) and refuses the pre-rebrand external `~/.hermes` / `%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes` as the new home, falling back to the canonical default instead of writing inside a read-old external tree. **Installer:** `scripts/install.sh`'s no-Python fallback mirrors the new uninitialized rule and detects all five read-old XDG dirs; `--dry-run` prints the resolved layout plus every `10-qubit-home.conf` drop-in path and writes nothing; `--prefix` remains a portable new tree (`QUBIT_HOME` defaults to the prefix, explicit `QUBIT_HOME` wins) and never adopts the real HOME's XDG tree. New-layout drop-ins now cover the hotkey helper (`qubit-voice-typing.service`) as well as the bridge and gateway. `scripts/install.ps1` now installs VoiceBridge under `$QUBIT_HOME\voice` (config/data/state/cache/models) instead of `<root>\config|data\qubit-voicebridge`, while keeping the old XDG roots in the launcher for read-old detection. `INSTALL.md`/`README.md` were corrected from the stale "XDG by default / Linux only" text to the QUBIT_HOME layout, read-old rule, and real platform support. `scripts/capture_orb_live.py` / `verify_orb_stop_live.py` journal fallbacks now use `preferred_state_dir()`. **Verified (focused):** `tests/test_voice_paths_new_install9v.py` **19 passed** (uninitialized creates only the new tree, no XDG; runtime home/env; config default rewrite; `runtime_home_for_cfg`; Linux/macOS/Windows default + explicit shapes; external `~/.hermes` refusal; fresh/new/read-old/both; model preference; no mutation); `tests/test_root_installer_release.py` **18 passed** (fresh + prefix + read-old dry-runs, no writes, drop-in paths, existing HOME XDG untouched); `tests/test_root_installer_windows.py` **17 passed** (new `$QUBIT_HOME\voice` layout, no old per-identity voice target); `tests/test_macos_install_path.py` **12 passed**; `tests/test_voice_orb_live_capture9u.py` + `test_voice_orb_stop_live9u.py` **57 passed**; `tests/test_independence_clean_home_fix4.py` + `test_gateway_daemon_wave16.py` + `test_authority_unification8u.py` **37 passed**; `tests/test_packet8s.py` **67 passed** with only the two known environment failures (worktree has no `.venv`; the live unit belongs to another checkout), and one layout assertion updated to the new-install contract; `bash -n scripts/install.sh`, `py_compile`, and `git diff --check` clean. **Not done / deferred:** migration/copy/switch of an existing XDG install, live service/config edits, a full matrix, and a push; `VERIFIED` not claimed. Unrelated pre-existing failures reproduced on base (`test_session_continuity9u` model-stub signature, `test_migration_coverage_wave17` `agent.transports.qubit_tools_mcp_server` import, `test_migration_independence_wave13::test_session_store_resolves_qubit_home_not_hermes` `qubit_state.SessionDB` shadowing) were not touched.

**VoiceBridge install paths lane `release/voice-paths` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-rel-voice`, base `5a04893b5`):** Added one dependency-free resolver (`voicebridge/paths.py`) for the two layouts the product must support without breaking either. **New installs** resolve to `$QUBIT_HOME/voice/{config,data,state,cache,models}` (QUBIT_HOME defaults to `~/.qubit`); **existing installs** keep their XDG paths (`~/.config|~/.local/share|~/.local/state|~/.cache/qubit-voicebridge`, models under XDG data) as explicit read-old paths. `voice_layout()` reports `new` / `read-old` / `uninitialized`, the new tree, the read-old tree, what exists, and the runtime `effective` paths; it never moves, copies, or deletes an old tree. Config loading now prefers the new tree once it exists, otherwise reads the old XDG config in place (`legacy_source` is set), and for a new tree rewrites the shipped example's old default paths (`key_env`, workspace, templates, L1.5 model) into the new tree at load time. `Config.state_dir/data_dir/model_dir`, model resolution, the runtime home/default (`qubit_runtime.default_home`/`canonical_runtime_home`), model paths, feature flags, heartbeat/wake/logview/sound, orb state, local actions, the desktop helper, and the compat config candidate all use the same resolver. `scripts/install.sh` now asks that resolver for the layout (fallback mirror only when no Python exists before the venv), uses `$QUBIT_HOME/voice` for new installs, prints `layout`, five paths, and a read-old notice in `--dry-run`, never initializes the new tree when old XDG data exists, and adds a systemd drop-in (`10-qubit-home.conf`) for new-layout services instead of editing the shipped templates. **Verified (focused):** new `tests/test_voice_paths_new_install9v.py` **9 passed** (fresh/new/read-old/both, no mutation, Config new/read-old, model preference, runtime home/env); `tests/test_root_installer_release.py` **15 passed** (new default/prefix layout, read-old preservation, dry-run no writes, systemd drop-in plan); `test_packet8s.py` **86 passed** with only the two known environment failures (worktree has no `.venv`; the operator's live unit belongs to another checkout) plus `test_no_hardcoded_operator_home_in_product` flagging the pre-existing `voicebridge/cdp_supervisor.py:157` literal; `test_packet8kp`/`test_qbpv_pkg5_packaging`/`test_packaging_wave3`/`test_packet8s` path subset **224 passed, 1 skipped**; no live service/config edit, no full matrix, no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Release artifact-install E2E gate `release/install-e2e` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-rel-e2e`, base `5a04893b5`):** Added `scripts/artifact_install_e2e.py` over the existing `scripts/clean_install_rehearsal.py` engine (the Python sibling of `clean_install_check.sh`; the artifact dashboard check delegates to `scripts/release_qualify.py::_has_dashboard`). It installs a built or supplied wheel into a throwaway venv/HOME/prefix with no checkout on `sys.path`, then asserts every console entry point, `qubit --version`, the installed dashboard assets/plugins, public `GET /api/health`, and the vendored runtime import from package data. Install profiles: `--wheel` (skip build), `--profile NAME` (roots `QUBIT_HOME` at `<work>/state/profiles/<name>`), `--without-dashboard` (liveness still probed), `--without-models` (asserts no model artifact written), `--require-network`, `--require-clean`. Isolation is asserted: the engine snapshots the operator's live `QUBIT_HOME`/`HERMES_HOME` (env plus `~/.qubit`/`~/.hermes`) before/after and the installed probe carries a `sys.addaudithook` guard that raises on any open under those homes. Typed `PASS`/`FAIL`/`DEGRADED` exit 0/2/4 and a JSON report (`--report`, default `dist/artifact-install-e2e.json`). Also fixed the shared venv creation to pin `uv venv --python <this interpreter>` (uv otherwise picked 3.11 and the product's `requires-python >=3.12` rejected the install) and refreshed `clean_install_check.sh` to assert `/chat`, `/api/health`, and `qubit --version`. Wired a `workflow_dispatch`/release-tag job in `.github/workflows/qubit-ci.yml` (builds the dashboard bundle if absent, runs `--build --require-network --require-clean`, uploads the report) and documented the commands in `README.md` §Release certification. **Verified:** real build/install run `PYTHONPATH=/tmp/qubit-rel-e2e TMPDIR=/tmp .venv/bin/python scripts/artifact_install_e2e.py --build --work-root /tmp/qubit-artifact-real2 --report /tmp/qubit-artifact-real2.json --keep --json` → **PASS** (wheel `qubit_voicebridge-0.0.1-py3-none-any.whl`, sha256 `e4827293…`, `qubit 0.0.1`, dashboard=True, `health=DEGRADED` from the clean-host inventory, vendored runtime imported from site-packages, 2 live homes unchanged); supplied-wheel minimal profile `--wheel … --without-dashboard --without-models --profile minimal` → **PASS** (profile home created, models dir empty, dashboard skipped); `bash scripts/clean_install_check.sh --require-network` → **PASS** (dashboard plugins=15, webrtc VAD fallback). Hermetic tests: new `tests/test_artifact_install_e2e.py` **13 passed, 1 skipped** (the opt-in real run needs `QUBIT_ARTIFACT_E2E=1`); `tests/test_update_packaging9u.py` **17 passed**; `tests/test_packaging_wave3.py` + `test_qbpv_pkg5_packaging.py` + `test_qbpv2_agent5_packaging.py` **47 passed, 1 skipped**. **Not done:** no live service/config change, no full matrix, no publish; the CI job needs a self-hosted runner with node/npm and network, and the real dependency install is the only step that needs real network (a full OS/VM matrix is still not covered). `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Public-tree feature/page parity gate (2026-11-04, branch `release/feature-parity`, worktree `/tmp/qubit-rel-parity`, base `5a04893b5`):** Added a static gate so a clean export cannot silently drop a Qubit-only dashboard surface. `scripts/public_tree_feature_parity.json` declares 13 pages (`qubit-office`, `qubit-office2`/Quest, `qubit-office3`/Voice Chat, `qubit-tv`, `achievements`, `qubit-boards`, `reports`, `operations`, `qubit-runtime`, `qubit-gateway`, `qubit-approvals`, `memory`, `qubit-models`), 15 required routes (Memory and Models add their built-in aliases), their plugin manifests/flags/assets, and backend handlers in `voicebridge/qubit_compat.py`, `voicebridge/webui.py`, `voicebridge/office/*`, `voicebridge/office_stream.py`, `voicebridge/kanban.py`, `voicebridge/communications/reports.py`, `voicebridge/dashboard_ops.py`, `voicebridge/runtime_images.py`, `voicebridge/gateway_peers.py`, `voicebridge/approvals.py`, `voicebridge/active_memory.py`, and `qubit_runtime/plugins/qubit-achievements/dashboard/plugin_api.py`. `scripts/check_public_tree_feature_parity.py` reads the exporter's own selection (`scripts/export_public_tree.py::selected_paths`) and fails with a `MISSING <kind> <path> — <reason> (source: <path>)` list; `tests/test_public_tree_feature_parity.py` runs it hermetically (no server/browser/network). Groups require the committed TUI `dist`, all of `voicebridge/`, the plugin catalog YAML + published JSON, every `systemd/` unit, and 18 user-facing/legal docs. **Exporter fix:** `PRODUCT_DIST_SUBTREES` now admits the committed product dist assets that `dist` used to drop — `qubit_runtime/ui-tui/dist` (3 files) and the pinned dashboard plugin bundles under `qubit_runtime/plugins/{qubit-achievements,kanban}/dashboard/dist` and `frontend/qubit-web/public/plugins/{qubit-achievements,kanban}/dist` — while every other `dist`/`node_modules`/cache stays excluded. `voicebridge/webui.py` now lists the previously catch-all-only `/qubit-tv`, `/qubit-memory`, `/achievements`, and `/kanban` in `SPA_ROUTE_PATHS`. **Verified (focused, hermetic):** new parity gate **4 passed** (`pages 13`, `routes 15/15`, `1623` required exported paths, `0 missing`, 3 documented omissions); `tests/test_export_public_tree.py` **7 passed**; pre-existing failures in `test_office_tower_plugin`/`test_office_plugin_wiring` (absent `frontend/qubit-dist`), `test_office23_registration::test_app_tsx_has_a_nav_tab_for_each_path` (stale expectation for the intentionally hidden Office2/Office3 rows), and the frontend inventory/hermes-route files reproduce byte-identically on base `5a04893b5`. **Deliberate omissions:** `voicebridge/cdp_supervisor.py` is excluded because its HEAD content carries the operator path `/home/trick` (reported as `personal_path`); internal status/audit/report docs stay filtered; Office2/Office3 are hidden URL-only pages; Office2 and TV need no backend. **Release path contract lane `release/paths` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-rel-paths`, base `1b83c2198`):** Closed the disconnected path readers for the `~/.qubit` / `%LOCALAPPDATA%\qubit` contract without touching live data or services. **Code:** `apps/desktop/electron/data-paths.mjs` now defaults to `.qubit` (POSIX) and `%LOCALAPPDATA%\qubit` (Windows), with `.hermes`/`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes` resolved only when the canonical root is absent and never during suffixed runs; `get_launchd_label()` now returns `ai.qubit.gateway[-profile]` so it matches the installed plist stem, while fleet/label derivation, update cleanup, `gateway_runtime_service._launchd`, and uninstall still enumerate the pre-rebrand `ai.hermes.gateway*` family read-old; `scripts/install_systemd_unit.sh` and `voicebridge.product.service_owner` resolve `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/systemd/user` (absolute value wins, otherwise `$HOME/.config/systemd/user`); systemd templates quote the `ExecStart` interpreter; desktop package discovery/launch prefers `Qubit.app`/`Qubit.exe` and `Qubit` with `Hermes.app`/`Hermes.exe`/`Hermes` as read-old fallbacks. **Verified (focused):** electron data-path contract via `node` 4 passed; ground-truth `tests/test_release_paths_contract.py` 4 passed; runtime release-path/launchd/uninstall/gui set `146 passed, 55 skipped`; launchd lifecycle set `24 passed, 3 skipped`; `test_packet8s` service/unit set 6 passed (2 environment-only failures: the worktree has no `.venv`, and the operator's live unit was not part of this lane); `test_gui_uninstall*`/`test_update_zip_completion` `55 passed, 2 skipped`; systemd template tests in `test_packaging_wave3`/`test_lifecycle_wave4` green. **Not done:** no destructive migration, no `~/.qubit`/`~/.local` write, no live service restart, no full matrix, no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Root installer hardening lane `release/root-installer` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-rel-root`, base `1b83c2198`):** Turned `scripts/install.sh` into the start of a real product installer without changing its end-to-end install model. **Added:** strict multi-flag parsing (`-h/--help`, `-s/--with-systemd`, `--dry-run`, `--without-models`, `--without-dashboard`, `--prefix DIR`/`--prefix=DIR`, `-y/--yes`, hidden `--launcher-only`; unknown options and stray positionals exit **2**); typed `MISSING`/`DEGRADED` preflight for required bash/curl/uv plus optional ollama/node/npm and model-integrity tools (sha256sum/shasum/openssl, tar); one resolved layout (`--prefix` > `QUBIT_PREFIX`; `QUBIT_HOME` printed, defaults to the prefix or `~/.qubit`) with all data/config/state/bin paths printed before any change; `--dry-run` performs preflight + layout + per-phase plans and writes nothing (verified with an empty temp HOME/prefix and no `.venv`/`qubit-dist` creation); `--without-models` skips both pinned-sha256 model downloads and ollama provisioning; `--without-dashboard` skips the bundle gate/build; model checksum verification and the typed dashboard DEGRADED gate (exit 4) are preserved. **Systemd:** `--with-systemd` now renders the two units and then runs `systemctl --user enable --now qubit-voicebridge.service qubit-gateway.service` (interactive confirmation; non-interactive proceeds because the flag is explicit; missing/failed systemctl prints exact manual commands instead of a silent no-op). **README:** replaced the stale packaged-first install text with the source-checkout path (`--help`, `--dry-run`, install, `--with-systemd`), documented the new flags, and renamed stale `qubit-brain4` references to `qubit-brain`. **Tests:** new hermetic `tests/test_root_installer_release.py` **13 passed** (syntax, help/unknown/positional/`--prefix` errors, no-write dry-run, prefix/`QUBIT_PREFIX` layout, explicit `QUBIT_HOME`, systemd plan, DEGRADED optional tools, typed missing-required exit 1, source guard for `enable --now`); existing `tests/test_installer_integrity_wave25.py` **12 passed**; `test_clean_clone_wave5.py::test_installer_has_typed_dashboard_degradation_gate` **1 passed**; `bash -n`, `--help`, `--dry-run` (models/dashboard on and off), and unknown-flag all exercised on the branch. **Not done:** no live service/config touched, no systemd units enabled/started, no full matrix, no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Public-release CI surface (2026-11-04, branch `release/ci`, base `1b83c2198`):** Added the minimal Qubit release CI without porting the upstream workflow set. `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs `pull_request`, `push: main`, and `workflow_dispatch` on GitHub-hosted Linux with `permissions: contents: read` and PR-cancelling concurrency: workflow lint (actionlint 1.7.12 pinned by digest + `.github/scripts/check_workflow_security.py`), runtime compile/root smoke, frontend `npm ci`/typecheck/`npm run test`/`npm run build`, and a packaging job that restores the built `frontend/qubit-dist`, runs `python scripts/release_qualify.py --build --install --reproduce --json --out dist/release`, and uploads `dist/release/build1` plus the JSON report. `.github/workflows/release.yml` runs on `v*` tags, builds/verifies with the same qualifier, writes `SHA256SUMS`, and creates or updates a **draft** release only (job gated by the `release` environment); there is no publish step and no secret. `.github/workflows/release-install.yml` installs from the published release wheel/URL via `gh release download` (no checkout), verifies `SHA256SUMS`, and proves the installed dashboard/runtime resources. Actions are pinned to full commit SHAs, Docker actionlint is pinned by digest, and `.github/dependabot.yml` tracks `github-actions` weekly. `scripts/build_dashboard.sh` now installs `frontend/qubit-shared` dev dependencies (`--no-package-lock`) before typecheck, which a clean clone needs because `qubit-web`'s tsconfig includes the shared source. **Verified (focused):** `npm ci --legacy-peer-deps` + shared install; `npm run typecheck` clean; `npm run test` **157 files / 1249 tests passed**; `npm run build` succeeded and wrote `frontend/qubit-dist`; `actionlint` clean on all 10 workflows; `python3 .github/scripts/check_workflow_security.py` → `OK (3 managed workflows)`; PyYAML parse of all workflows clean; `test_workflow_job_graph` **2 passed** after allowing only the intentional `release.yml` `v*` tag trigger. **Known pre-existing release blocker (not this lane):** a local `release_qualify.py --build --allow-dirty` produced a wheel+sdist that DO ship `frontend/qubit-dist`, but the content scan FAILed/BLOCKED (exit 8) on tracked developer paths (`voicebridge/cdp_supervisor.py`, `docs/VOICE-BRIDGE-AUDIT.md` → `AUDIO_SETUP.md`, two frontend tests, and internal root/sdist documents). A clean checkout has the same tracked paths, so the packaging job will correctly stay red until that content is sanitized; the release draft job depends on it and therefore cannot create a draft yet. The report/artifacts upload is `if: always()`. **Deferred:** Windows release-install (the root installer is checkout-based, not artifact-based); no signing/GPG; no package-registry publishing; full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Seven-lane fix4 merge — independence, channels, MCP, parity, voice, UI, provenance (2026-11-04, `main`, HEAD `1f36af390`):** Merged seven isolated worktrees (provenance, channels, mcp, parity, voiceid, ui, independence) on base `26c479d91`; all branches were focused-tested, merged, and the dashboard was rebuilt (`frontend/qubit-dist`, build-info `head=436aea6d0 dirty=false`). **Live:** four user services active; `/api/health` 200; `/chat` 200; `/files` 200; `qubit channels status` now reports `channels 47, configured 3` (discord/telegram/whatsapp), `dashboard_configured 19`, `connected 3`, listener still `NONE`; `/api/media/proxy` is now served and always token-gated. **Code:** three-tree finish-line provenance artifacts remain anchored at `69948`/finish pin `088f` with explicit missing/omitted sets; dashboard now hides the duplicate chat header; MCP dashboard installs preserve `auth: oauth`, parked OAuth is terminal/login-required instead of retrying; voice identity gates wake and confirmation; independence moved runtime homes/launchers to `.qubit` and removed external-Hermes product dependencies while preserving required compatibility identifiers. **Focused verification:** channels 83; MCP root 48 + runtime 89; parity 73; voice 169; independence 11 + runtime 85 passed/9 skipped; UI 67 + `tsc` clean; vendor/provenance 26 passed. `hermes_vendor_manifest --check`, `source_inventory --check`, and `--finish-line-check` are clean. **Not done:** full matrix; live MCP Airtable/memelord require operator OAuth login; external exposed secrets still need rotation; no push; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Vendor provenance — finish-line report, two anchors kept honest (2026-11-04, branch `fix4/provenance`, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix4-provenance`, base `26c479d91`):** The W9 manifest is anchored at the vendoring source (`69948c0057`); the finish line is `088f657ecf`. Repointing the W9 pin would falsify provenance, because a differing file can be a Qubit rebrand of the old source or unported upstream work, and one tree cannot distinguish them. `scripts/hermes_vendor_manifest.py` now generates two sibling artifacts from three real trees (source, finish line, local): `docs/generated/QUBIT_HERMES_FINISH_LINE_PROVENANCE.json` classifies every finish-line file as `IDENTICAL_AT_FINISH_LINE`, `QUBIT_PATCHED` (upstream unchanged since source), `BEHIND_FINISH_LINE` (local equals source; upstream moved), `DIVERGED_FROM_BOTH` (local patch plus upstream drift — never credited as a patch), `MISSING_FROM_LOCAL`, or `INTENTIONALLY_OMITTED`, with explicit missing/omitted/behind/diverged sets; `docs/generated/HERMES_SOURCE_COMPARISON_FINISH_LINE.json` carries the same classification per frontend row. Commands: `--finish-line PATH --finish-line-only` writes, `--finish-line-check` verifies byte-identically, and the canonical W9 artifacts are untouched unless a non-finish run is requested. **Measured at 088f:** runtime files `8162` identical / `2867` patched / `1028` behind / `7631` diverged / `1718` missing / `467` omitted; production `.py` missing **0**, behind `128`, diverged `2130`. The finish-line report snapshots git-tracked files only, so it is reproducible from a clean checkout. The canonical manifest/inventory were refreshed at the same 69948 anchor: one stale tracked hash (`ui-tui/dist/hermes-build.json`, changed after the last provenance refresh) and 74 untracked build/run artifacts (`web_dist`, `egg-info`, lock/results) that are absent from a fresh clone are now excluded; `test_qubit_runtime_vendor8u::test_manifest_covers_every_file_on_disk` mirrors that tracked-only contract. **Verified:** `test_vendor_finish_line_provenance` **8 passed**, `test_qubit_runtime_vendor8u` **7 passed**, `test_vendor_manifest_test_classification` **4 passed**, `test_provenance_completeness` **1 passed/4 skipped**, `test_qubit_vendor8tf` with `QUBIT_PINNED_HERMES=/tmp/opencode/hermes-pin-69948` **11 passed/1 skipped**; `hermes_vendor_manifest.py --check`, `source_inventory.py --check`, and `--finish-line-check` all exit **0** in the clean worktree. **Known environmental failure (not this lane):** `test_source_inventory.py::test_achievements_plugin_represented_and_closed_in_ledger` needs the gitignored `docs/generated/QUBIT_GAP_LEDGER.hermes.json`, which is absent in a clean worktree and in the base worktree too. No service/live config touched, no full matrix, `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Channels status/config split-brain lane `fix4/channels` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix4-channels`, base `26c479d91`):** `qubit channels status` reported `configured: 0` while the dashboard store held 19 rows and the gateway served Discord/Telegram/WhatsApp. `voicebridge/channels_inbound.py` now joins all real config sources: inbound registry references, the dashboard Channels store (`msg-platforms.json`), the gateway runtime `.env` credential keys (key names only, never values), and live gateway-connected platforms. `status` adds `configured_platforms` + `registry_configured`, `catalog`/`accounts` rows carry a real `configured` flag, dashboard-disabled rows show `DISABLED`, and a configured channel with no Qubit transport keeps the typed `CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE` gate; the listener remains `NONE` and `available` stays empty. `voicebridge/qubit_compat.py::_platform_payload` now serves `ingress_url` from the live gateway's own `gateway_state.json` only while the gateway is running (reference parity; stopped-gateway records are not served), and `configured` also recognises required keys present in the gateway `.env`. **Verified (focused, `PYTHONPATH=.:qubit_runtime TMPDIR=/tmp`, known unraisable-ResourceWarning filter):** new `tests/test_channels_status_config_splitbrain9u.py` **9 passed**; channel/messaging set (`channels_*`, `oc_messaging_agent6_ingress/transport`) **83 passed**; read-only replay of the live stores in a temp tree shows `channels 47, configured 3, configured_platforms [discord, telegram, whatsapp], registry_configured 0, dashboard_configured 19, connected 3, listener NONE` and `CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE` rows. Three pre-existing unrelated failures reproduced byte-identically on base `26c479d91`: `test_qubit_api_parity_wave1::test_messaging_platform_test_reads_real_link_state`, `test_openclaw_wiring8u::test_plugins_voice_executes_on_shared_backend`, and `test_qubit_cutover8u::test_flag_on_serves_fork_reads_and_gates_sensitive` (health `auth_required`). No service restart, no live config write, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**MCP OAuth lane `fix4/mcp` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix4-mcp`, base `26c479d91`):** Fixed the product-side fresh-install path and parked-state guidance; live config and services were not touched. **(1) Dashboard catalog install dropped the OAuth marker.** `voicebridge/qubit_compat.h_mcp_catalog_install` built `url`/`command`/`env` only and `voicebridge/mcp_servers._runtime_entry` mirrored only connectivity fields, so installing a shipped `auth.type: oauth` entry (Airtable) wrote `config.yaml` without `auth: oauth` and the runtime could never build a provider. New `_catalog_install_fields` carries `auth: oauth` (plus a pre-registered `oauth` block), `_validate` accepts/preserves a bounded `oauth` mapping, and `_runtime_entry` mirrors the marker for HTTP servers. The reviewed `airtable` manifest itself already declared `auth: type: oauth`; its `post_install` now names `qubit mcp login airtable` (was the legacy command). The config example now shows `auth: oauth` on the Notion sample with the one-time login guidance. **(2) Parked state was log-only.** `GET /api/mcp/servers` (`h_mcp_servers`) dropped the cached `error`, so a parked server rendered as a bare "not connected". It now carries the redacted/bounded typed reason. `list_servers` also derives an honest read-only status for a separate dashboard process: an enabled OAuth entry with no cached token reads `login-required` with `Run qubit mcp login <name>`, and a legacy URL-only entry that matches a reviewed OAuth catalog row (the live Airtable row) reads `misconfigured-oauth` with the exact `mcp_servers.<name>.auth: oauth` + login fix. **(3) memelord log storm.** A configured OAuth server with no cached token in a non-interactive process raised `OAuthNonInteractiveError`, which used the bounded auth self-probe ladder (repeating `MCP OAuth setup failed for 'memelord'`). It is now a terminal login-required park (no self-probe) that records the exact `qubit mcp login memelord` command and revives only on an explicit reconnect/login or a token-file change. **Verified (focused):** `tests/test_mcp_oauth_fresh_install9u.py` + `tests/test_mcp_config_inventory9u.py` **14 passed**; root MCP read/registry/repair/connected/store/banner/inventory set **48 passed**; runtime `test_mcp_oauth_required_misconfig.py` (new login-required no-retry case) + `test_mcp_oauth_config_example.py` + `test_mcp_catalog.py` + `test_mcp_catalog_env_boundary.py` **62 passed**; runtime auth-park/failure/initial-connect/parked-probe/park-dedupe set **33 passed**. **Operator-only prerequisite (not performed here):** the live profile still has the legacy Airtable row without `auth: oauth` and memelord without a cached token; set `mcp_servers.airtable.auth: oauth` (or reinstall it from the catalog after this lands) and run `qubit mcp login airtable` / `qubit mcp login memelord` interactively. No service restart, no live config edit, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Reference-parity lane `fix4/parity` (2026-11-04, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix4-parity`, base `26c479d91`):** Closed three reference-dashboard gaps without touching live services/config. **(1) `/api/media/proxy`** is now a real Qubit compat route (`voicebridge/qubit_compat.py::h_media_proxy`) served at both the reference literal `/api/media/proxy` (new `webui.py` front-door branch, token-gated) and the SPA `/agent/api/media/proxy` (added to `QUBIT_READ_AUTHORITY` so it is never fork-proxied). It is deliberately not an open proxy: the reference CDN allowlist (`fal.media`, `fal.run`, `v3.fal.media`, `storage.googleapis.com`, `*.fal.media`, `*.fal.run`) plus the canonical `voicebridge.websites.blocked_host_reason` SSRF policy is re-checked on **every redirect hop** (the reference follows redirects blindly), the body is capped at 25 MiB and must be an image content type, explicit non-image extensions are refused, and no cookies/Authorization/credentials are attached. Failures are typed/redacted (`MEDIA_PROXY_BAD_URL`, `_HOST_NOT_ALLOWED`, `_REDIRECT_BLOCKED`, `_UNSUPPORTED_TYPE`, `_OVERSIZED`, `_TIMEOUT`, `_HTTP_ERROR`, `_FETCH_FAILED`). **(2) `/api/model/options` usage:** verified the compat handler already forwards the vendored builder's `usage` payload (`usage.windows` single-account, `usage.accounts` pooled) unchanged; added a regression test that stubs the builder and pins the nested shapes, plus the missing reference fields (`usage`, `plan_usage`, `usage_levels`, `limit`, etc.) on the TS `ModelOptionProvider` interfaces. No values are invented: a provider with no snapshot stays without `usage`. **(3) `/api/model/moa`:** verified the vendored runtime does contain MoA internals (`agent/moa_loop.py`, `qubit_cli/moa_config.py`), but Qubit's canonical model resolver never advertises a selectable `moa` provider and ships no presets, so the typed refusal is preserved (`enabled:false`, `presets:{}`, `moa_put` still 501 `MOA_SUPERSEDED`); only the detail wording was made accurate. **Verified (focused, hermetic, `TMPDIR=/tmp`, `PYTHONPATH=/tmp/qubit-fix4-parity`):** `tests/test_reference_parity_fix4.py` **18 passed** (includes a real ephemeral loopback server for the literal route: 401 without token, 403 disallowed host, 200 allowlisted image); `tests/test_provider_catalog_options.py::test_options_forwards_the_reference_usage_shape` **1 passed**; `tests/test_toolset_catalog_serving.py` + `test_web_tool8u.py` + `test_websites_probe_broker9u.py` **46 passed**; `test_dashboard_audit_truthfulness9u::test_moa_defaults_do_not_fabricate_providers` + `test_dashboard_matrix9u::test_gated_routes_are_typed_refusals` **2 passed**; `test_qubit_routes_parity8u.py` **22 passed, 1 pre-existing failure** (`test_schedules_runs_read_equals_backend`, byte-identical on base `26c479d91`). Three pre-existing root-runner failures also reproduce on base and are not caused here: `test_provider_catalog_options` options/recommended-default cases and the two `test_qubit_vendor8tf` compat cases (the root `pytest.ini` `pythonpath = tests` makes `tests/providers`/`tests/qubit_state` shadow the vendored packages). No live Qubit service/config touched, no restart, no full matrix, `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Voice-ID action gate — wake and confirmations (2026-11-04, branch `fix4/voiceid`, worktree `/tmp/qubit-fix4-voiceid`, base `26c479d91`):** Closed the security gap where `speaker_verify` gated barge-in but not hands-free wake activation or spoken confirmations, so any voice could arm/execute a command while an owner profile was enrolled. `voicebridge/voice_id.py` adds `command_identity_policy` (off / inactive / required) and `check_command_audio`, which fails **closed** for a required profile: an unmatched speaker, too-short audio, or a verification error is refused with a typed reason (`unverified`, `insufficient_audio`, `verify_error`) instead of silently downgrading. A stored enrollment whose verifier failed to load still counts as REQUIRED (typed refusal), while no profile enrolled keeps the historical any-voice wake (`no_profile`). `WakeMonitor._handle_segment` now checks the injected `speaker_check` **before** transcribing, so an unverified segment never arms the listener, settles a staged confirmation, or executes; the desktop helper binds it to the SAME lazy verifier it uses for barge-in (`_wake_speaker_check`). Explicit opt-out is preserved: `speaker_verify.enabled: false` or the new `speaker_verify.require_commands: false` (barge-in gate only); default `require_commands: true`. **Verified (focused, hermetic):** new `tests/test_voice_identity_wake9v.py` **15 passed** (verified/unverified/absent-profile/broken-enrollment/opt-out, wake + confirmation integration, helper seam); `test_speaker_verify_gate9v.py` 8; wake set (`test_voice_edit_wake9u`, `test_voice_wake_stall9u`, `test_voice_wake_responsiveness9u`, continuous/kws/media/reliability) **153 passed**; wake-routing/capture **25 passed**; confirm/FSM/bargein/helper **94 passed + 1 known Python 3.14 unraisable-sqlite warning** (passes alone); voice-edit/routes/STT **164 passed**. No live service/config touched, no restart, no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed. **Owner prerequisite:** enrollment (`qubit voice enroll`) is what makes the policy active; existing live config has no `require_commands` key, so it inherits the `true` default on the next helper restart — set `require_commands: false` there to keep barge-only gating.

**Security wave — bridge auth/media roots, Office exit checks, token bootstrap (2026-11-04, branch `fix2/security`, base `5978fe44e`):** Three high-confidence defects fixed with focused tests; no live config was edited and no service was restarted. **(1) WhatsApp bridge:** the Node bridge now requires `X-Qubit-Bridge-Token` on every endpoint except `/health`; the gateway writes a 32-byte token to `<session>/bridge.token` (0600) and passes it in `WHATSAPP_BRIDGE_TOKEN`, and both the adapter and the standalone sender attach it (an old bridge ignores it; a new bridge requires it). `/send-media` resolves the realpath and refuses anything outside `WHATSAPP_MEDIA_ROOTS` (profile cache roots + tempdir by default) with `MEDIA_OUTSIDE_ROOTS`; the Python client applies the same check before posting. **(2) Office:** `exit_check` no longer reaches `shell=True`. A new `voicebridge/office/exit_check.py` accepts only `true`/`false`, `test`/`[`, read-only `grep`, and a read-only `sqlite3` SELECT optionally piped through `grep`; `work.create` and `task_proposal.propose` refuse anything else with `OFFICE_EXIT_CHECK_UNSAFE`, and the runner executes the validated argv without a shell. Autowork now emits `sqlite3 -readonly -safe … | grep -q 1`. **(3) `/chat` bootstrap:** token injection now also requires Fetch Metadata to be absent, `none`, or `same-origin` and a missing/loopback-only `Origin`; a browser-marked cross-site request gets the shell without the token. The same rule applies to the Office iframe document. **Verified (focused):** Node `node --test bridge_security.test.mjs` **5 passed**; `tests/plugins/platforms/test_whatsapp_bridge_security.py` **9 passed**; existing WhatsApp gateway/tool files **62 passed, 3 skipped, 4 xfailed, 4 xpassed**; `tests/test_office_exit_check_security.py` **23 passed**; existing Office loop/proposal/dispatch/audit files **133 passed**; `tests/test_webui_token_bootstrap9u.py` **7 passed**; `tests/test_office_pages_security_contract.py` **45 passed** after its `_send` test helper was given the `extra_headers` argument the server already passes. **Not fixed / owner decisions:** the bridge token is a same-host secret, not an OS boundary (same-UID processes can read it), and loopback `/chat` still cannot authenticate the OS user (no peer-UID primitive on TCP) — the remaining protection is loopback-only binding plus `webui.token`; sending a file outside the configured media roots now requires adding its directory to `gateway.media_delivery_allow_dirs` (or `HERMES_MEDIA_ALLOW_DIRS`) before the bridge starts. No full matrix, no `VERIFIED`.

**Fix lane 16 — Office backend: browser-consumable SSE, bound reads, real write half (2026-11-02, branch `fix16/office-backend`, base `deb119370`):** The live Office stuck at "Connecting to the office…" was a **transport** defect, not a missing tower: `_serve_office_stream` answered HTTP/1.1 with `Connection: keep-alive` and no `Content-Length` or `Transfer-Encoding`. curl streamed it, but Chromium's EventSource waited for framing and never delivered a `snapshot` (measured live: `open` fired, zero `snapshot` events in 4 s). The stream is now `Transfer-Encoding: chunked`, one chunk per SSE frame plus a terminating chunk; a real browser probe against a temp HTTP server now reports `open` + snapshots with `rooms=1, agents=1`. **Reads:** `GET /api/agents` (list), `/api/rooms`, `/api/permissions`, `/api/messages`, `/api/snapshot` (one-shot JSON, not a second SSE), and `/api/status` are bound to real tower/store data; `/api/snapshot` no longer hangs as a stream, and `/api/status` keeps the classic bridge contract unless the request carries the office nonce. **Writes:** the vendored client's mutations now pass the same loopback + same-origin + own-app Referer + live nonce gate as reads (`_office_client_mutation_ok`); permission decisions settle the SAME shared approval journal (approve executes, deny cancels), messages are persisted in a new `office_message` table and delivered as a real claimed `office_task`, spawn queues a real task, and room rename/model assignment remain real store writes. Raw PTY/terminal/takeover/character stay typed refusals. `/api/access` now reports `permissions:true, messages:true, spawn:true, terminal:false`; the snapshot's `meta.terminal`/`meta.messages` gates are true because those controls are bound, and agents carry `canMessage:true`. **Verified (focused, hermetic):** 210 passed / 1 skipped across the habblaud compat, server-contract, route-binding, stream, transport, plugin-app, game-shell and full-integration files; a further 458 passed / 1 skipped across the CLI/tower/plugin/stream set (4 failures are the absent untracked `frontend/qubit-dist/plugins/fleet.json` build artifact, not code). **Not done:** no service restart, so live `:8890` still runs the old stream framing until restarted; no full matrix; `VERIFIED` not claimed.

**Fix lane 16 — Office iframe/status + deterministic page voice routes (2026-11-02, branch `fix16/office-ui`, base `deb119370`, worktree `/home/trick/qubit-worktrees/fix16-office-ui`):** No service restart, no full matrix, nothing pushed. **Office iframe root cause (measured live at `:8890`):** the host seat/status adapter polled `/agent/api/office/tower` every 400 ms and its `load()` aborted the previous request, but the tower fold takes ~0.6–1.3 s, so every poll ended `net::ERR_ABORTED`; the resulting request pile-up starved the office store and `/api/stream` delivered no snapshot, leaving the iframe on "Connecting… / 0 rooms / 0 agents". **Fix:** `OfficeAdapter.load()` now skips while a load is in flight (one request on the wire; no abort loop), the default cadence is 2 s (`POLL_INTERVAL_MS`), and the host wrapper reports the real tower state (connecting / connected with room+agent counts / stale / error) in a visible `office-app-connection` banner; a failed first read is an error, a retained payload with a failed refresh is stale. The tracked served entry `qubit-office.game.js`/`.css` was rebuilt from the changed source. **Voice:** deterministic L0 routes now exist for `open office` / `qubit office` / `open qubit office` → `/qubit-office` and `open agent quest` / `agent quest` → `/qubit-office2`; they beat L1.5, which had mapped these onto `cli_worktree` and `skills_workshop`. The immediate local-action path (`open_website`) and the registry bridge (`pages` backend) both open the real page and speak the opener's own reply; bare `office`/`qubit office` is covered after `_norm` strips the wake word. Structured `qubit office ...` ledger/mutation refusals are unchanged. **Agent Quest** stays honestly static: its placeholder banner and per-panel tags are untouched, and the page is now reachable by voice/page route. **Verified:** Vitest office-app **47 passed / 4 files** (new adapter serialization/stale-retention tests + connection-banner tests); `tsc` on the changed office files reported only pre-existing unused/Phaser typings in untouched modules, none in the changed files; `tests/test_voice_office_pages9u.py` + `test_voice_website_shortcuts9u.py` + `test_voice_inventory_coverage.py` **27 passed**; `test_voice_commands8tf.py` **26 passed, 1 failed** (the known unclosed-`sqlite3` finalizer warning, unchanged on base); `test_office_game_adapter.py` + `test_office_game_packaging.py` **39 passed**; `test_office_game_shell.py` **30 passed** (its pre-existing `createOfficeApp`-only regex was corrected to accept the seat-routing wrapper, which itself renders `createOfficeApp`); `test_voice_parity_gap8u.py` + `test_qubit_command_parity8u.py` + CLI family wave 12/13 **31 passed, 2 failed** on Python 3.14 unclosed-sqlite finalizer warnings only. **Blockers / not verified:** live `:8890` was not restarted or rebuilt, so the browser fix is source-verified only; Office write half and live telemetry remain out of scope; Agent Quest remains static; the reference `:9119` dashboard is down, so behavioral parity is still blocked. **Fix lane 15 — voice import environment, hotkey startup retry, `new session` routing (2026-11-02, branch `fix15/voice`, base `5c644f9b7`, worktree `/home/trick/qubit-worktrees/fix15-voice`):** Three live voice defects fixed; no full matrix, no service restart, no push/merge.

- **Hotkey startup retry.** `qubit-voice-typing` could log `hotkey.unavailable: xinput could not connect to :0` and give up; `HotkeyListener.start_with_retry` retries through a bounded ~3.75 s schedule and `desktop_helper` uses it. Headless hosts still fail promptly.
- **`new session` is a real create, never a silent rename.** Added deterministic `chat_session_new` (aliases `new session`, `new chat`, …) backed by the canonical chat store's `create()`; `session_rename` is now confirmation-gated; L1.5's entry markers include `rename`/`create`, so an adversarial embedding cannot decide a write.

**Voice wake reliability + STT quality (2026-10-29, branch `fix/voice-wake` off `2f9c86eef`):** Fixed the regression where "hey qubit" was dropped while the assistant spoke and commands decoded as "One GitHub". Root causes: (1) the KWS-miss ASR probe was switched to the streaming nemotron (`420c47514`), which mangles short commands; (2) the barge-in RMS gate default 0.10 sat above the live mic's owner-speech level (~0.08 RMS after `ParecMic`'s 12x per-frame gain), so a wake during TTS never tripped it; (3) the TTS echo guard was keyed on the reply's START with a 5 s window, so wake stayed deaf for seconds after a short reply ended; (4) an expired staged confirmation blocked every later wake command; (5) the accurate decoder's CPU retry did not recognise `cudaErrorInvalidDevice: invalid device ordinal`. Fixes: the wake fallback uses the same accurate/English decoder as Right-Ctrl (streaming only as the internal last resort); barge-in default 0.06 (measured live: reply echo 0.012-0.057 RMS, owner speech ~0.08); echo guard is a 0.8 s tail after `speak_done` (service TTS keeps 5 s); expired pending clears on a new non-affirm command; invalid CUDA ordinals retry on CPU. **Verified:** exact requested focused set **342 passed**; plus the new/extended sets (`test_voice_wake_reliability9u.py`, `test_voice_confirm_session9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_bargein9u.py`, `test_asr_accurate9u.py`) → **376 passed** together. **Live:** restarted only `qubit-voice-typing.service`; old pid `7283`, worktree-code pid `315668` (wake monitor started `engine: kws`, `segmenter: silero`, `asr.load moonshine/cpu`, `kws.frames` flowing), then restored the unit to the main repo (pid `333715`). **Not live-verified:** an actual spoken wake/command — the user was asleep and the synthetic TTS playback is not acoustically reachable from the mic on this host (default sink is the Scarlett S/PDIF; a capture during playback measured ~0.01 RMS). The fix is committed on the branch, not merged or live.
**Voice panel no longer latches after a reply or confirmation (2026-10-29, `fix/voice-panel`):** The panel could stay non-idle and the wake gate kept dropping commands in two ways. A staged confirmation was preserved against every non-affirm utterance until its 120 s TTL, and a wake command arriving while a stale Right-Ctrl capture was LISTENING was refused (`wake.turn_refused state=listening`, live pid 799155). The echo guard also measured its full 5 s window from the START of a short reply, so the first "Hey Qubit" after one looked dead. Fixed: a fresh directed wake cancels a stale pending without running it (unrelated speech still keeps it staged); a bare wake notifies the helper and shows the panel listening; a wake preempts only a stale LISTENING capture and fences the old transcript/error by request generation; the echo guard ends at the journaled `speak_done` / `playback.stopped` with the old 5 s window only as fallback; errored/timed-out wake turns clear pending and return to IDLE. **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_edit_hud9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_hud_actions9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_live_panel9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_helper9u.py`, `test_voice_confirm_session9u.py`, `test_voice_session_fsm9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_wake9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_routes9u.py` -> **194 passed**; wake/barge/ask/STT/talk set -> **344 passed**. No service restart (deferred to the orchestrator); no spoken live turn. Commit `91cba8e8e`.

DRM services open in the real browser and are not embedded or controlled by the dashboard; there is no native media SDK or DRM player, and no claim of native playback. Voice aliases for `open qubit tv` and the services were added to `_SITE_SHORTCUTS`/`open_website`; they load on the next bridge restart (not restarted here because another agent is working on the dashboard UI). **Verified:** `QubitTvPage.test.tsx` + `resolve-page-title.test.ts` -> **18 passed**, `tsc -p . --noEmit` clean, `/qubit-tv` renders in Playwright with 10 service cards and **0 console errors**. **Wake restored; moonshine for commands, streaming for the wake phrase (2026-10-29):** Right-Ctrl worked but “Hey Qubit” did not. KWS misses, and the fallback had been switched to moonshine, which misheard the wake as “Hate your bit”, “Thank you a bit”, “You’ll be”. The KWS-miss probe now uses the streaming nemotron (which hears “Hey Qubit”), while the command segment uses English moonshine (the browser-local decoder; parakeet misdetected commands as Portuguese). Also added `opa`/`opah` as “open” ASR aliases (`Opa, YouTube!` → `open_website`). **Verified:** 362 focused tests; live wake triggered on the first attempt after helper restart (`799155`). Bare `Stop.` is an interrupt (no pending task); weather fails fast; `start the brain` is recognized.

**Bare “Stop.” is now an interrupt, not a task (2026-10-29):** Live logs showed the stuck/“heard”/“say hey qubit 2-3 times” failure: at 05:37:11 a bare `Stop.` was routed `mode=task`, staged as a confirmation, and every later `Hey Qubit` / `Stop the brain` hit `voice_edit.wake.pending_guard` until the panel's Cancel cleared it. The helper now recognises exact bare stop phrases (`stop`, `stop it`, `stop talking`, `cancel`, `never mind`, `be quiet`, `enough`, …) before routing, calls `stop_speaking()` (which aborts the in-flight `/ask` and marks the turn stopped), and returns to idle. It does not stage a task, so it cannot block later wake commands. `stop the brain` is deliberately **not** in that set (it is the model-power command). **Verified:** `test_voice_edit_helper9u.py` + the wake/brain set -> **180 passed**; services restarted (bridge `735604`, helper `735602`); spoken retest pending.

**Weather fails fast; “start the brain” is recognised (2026-10-29):** A 05:27 weather turn took 52 s: the weather script timed out twice (10 s each) and fell through to the unloaded `qubit-brain`, which timed out after 30 s, retried, and then returned fabricated weather. `start`/`boot`/`resume`/“welcome to the brain” were not brain-warm patterns either, so `Start the brain.` called the unloaded model and froze. Fixes: `_live_weather` is capped at 4 s and `_direct_fact_answer` now returns `I couldn't reach the weather service right now.` instead of falling into the model; `brain_warm` matches start/boot/resume/wake and the ASR `welcome to the brain`. **Verified:** the focused weather/brain/helper set -> **332 passed** before the stop fix; live 05:35/05:38 weather answered in ~0.5 s and start-brain acked in ~0.1 s. Services running bridge `735604`, helper `735602`.

**Safe local commands now execute immediately (2026-10-29):** After the brain commands proved the confirmation hop was the latency, the owner asked for the same for site/app/media commands (`youtube`, `instagram`, `messenger`, etc.). `_immediate_local_action` now accepts every registered local action with `confirm=false` except the risk set `window_close`, `empty_trash`, `send_message`, `repo_edit`, `compound` and `time_date` (which keeps the richer direct clock answer and the sleep-clear ordering). `service.route` sends those commands to the answer lane and `ask` runs them through the shared broker with `confirmed=True`, so `open youtube` / `open messenger` / `open instagram` / `open gmail` / `open github` / `play`-and-media / volume / tabs / screenshot / display switch ack without a “say yes” round-trip. Destructive actions still stage and ask. **Verified:** the same focused set plus the site/display/local-action files -> **319 passed**; new route tests pin `mode=answer` with no `requires_confirmation` for sites and `mode=task` + `requires_confirmation` for `window_close`/`empty_trash`. Services restarted (bridge `696823`, helper `696816`). **Spoken retest:** `Open YouTube`, `open messenger`, and `open GitHub` each logged `voice_edit.local.immediate` + `local.open_website` (broker `ALLOWED`, no `task.proposed`, no confirmation) and spoke the ack ~0.7-0.9 s after the command.

**Brain commands now execute immediately (2026-10-29):** The first spoken test revealed the real latency: `Stop brain while I'm playing.` was staged as a normal confirm-gated task, so the helper asked “say yes” and the unload only ran after the owner answered `Yes.` ~13 s later; `Wake up the brain.` did the same. `service.route` now sends the two immediate actions (`brain_unload`, `brain_warm`) to the answer lane and `ask` runs them through the shared broker with `confirmed=True` before the sleep-mode check, so the ack is the only feedback and there is no confirmation round-trip. Bare `go to sleep` now unloads instead of entering the old sleep-mode gate (owner's requested meaning for gaming); destructive local actions keep their confirmation policy (see the next entry). **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_edit_routes9u.py` + `test_voice_edit9u.py` + `test_voice_edit_sleep_mode9u.py` + `test_voice_wake_routing_ask9u.py` + `test_voice_edit_tool_task9u.py` + `test_voice_brain_power9u.py` -> **216 passed**; the route tests pin `mode=answer`, no `requires_confirmation`, and `confirmed=True`. Services restarted (bridge `696823`, helper `696816`); spoken retest after the change is pending.

**Brain off/on voice controls (2026-10-29):** Two new data-registered local actions release and restore the local model without involving the brain itself. `brain_unload` matches `stop brain`, `stop brain while I'm playing`, `unload/release/drop the brain`, `free the VRAM`, `clear the GPU`, `I'm going to game`/`gaming`, `sleep`, `go to sleep`, and `brain off`; it runs `ollama ps`, then `ollama stop` for the loaded `qubit-brain` plus any loaded embedding model (`embeddinggemma`, `nomic-embed-text`), using the full loaded tag, and acks `Brain unloaded.` / `Brain and embeddings unloaded.` / `The brain isn't loaded, so there's nothing to unload.` It never calls the model. `brain_warm` matches `wake up`, `load/warm the brain`, `bring the brain back`, `I'm done gaming`, and friends; it acks `Warming the brain.` and starts a detached `voicebridge/local/brain_warm.py` process that sends one token (`num_predict: 1`) at the voice context (`num_ctx: 32768`, `keep_alive: 10m`), so the bridge is not blocked. Bare `qubit, wake up` / `qubit, sleep` / `qubit, I'm going to game` now execute through a narrow safe-bare-wake exemption; all other bare `qubit, ...` room chatter still drops. **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_brain_power9u.py` + the wake routing set -> **173 passed**; the local/tool/router set -> **155 passed**. Live Ollama proof: unload removed `qubit-brain` and `embeddinggemma:300m`, a second unload said `nothing to unload`, and `wake up the brain` reloaded `qubit-brain` at **32768 ctx, 100% GPU, 4 s**. No spoken turn was possible because the microphone is silent (entry above).

**Voice input restored — 48V phantom was OFF (2026-10-29):** The silence was the condenser mic on Scarlett input 1 with `Line In 1-2 Phantom Power` off (`amixer -c 1 sget` now reports `Capture [on]`). After `amixer -c 1 sset 'Line In 1-2 Phantom Power' on`, a fresh 28 s capture showed real speech peaks (`0.107`, `0.044`, `0.064`) instead of the previous `0.001`, and the live helper transcribed again (`Hey Qubit.`, `Test 1, 2, 3.`, `What's your name?`). Right-Ctrl and the wake path were already firing; only the input signal was missing. The mixer change is not persisted by the service: `/var/lib/alsa/asound.state` is root-owned and `alsactl store 1` needs the owner password. Run `sudo alsactl store 1` so it survives a power cycle/replug (the service cannot write the root-owned ALSA state). No code change for this one.

**Voice input silent — hardware/input, not the hotkey path (2026-10-29):** After the display-switch work the owner reported Right-Ctrl and "hey qubit" no longer working. The live journal shows Right-Ctrl **is** detected (`hotkey ptt_start`/`ptt_end` for pid 550551) but both turns ended in `voice_edit.helper.transcribe.accurate_fallback: accurate empty`; the bridge had yielded the key to the helper (`hotkey.yielded_to_helper`) and its startup line falsely said `Hotkeys unavailable (no X)`. An independent 58 s `parec` capture while the owner spoke measured **peak 0.0011 on channel 0 and 0.0 on channels 1-3**, so no microphone signal is reaching PipeWire. ALSA card 1 (Scarlett 2i2): source unmuted at 100%, capture switch on, **Line In 1 gain 52.24 dB, Line In 2 gain 0.00 dB, 48V phantom off**, source is 4-channel analog-surround-40. The helper's wake spotter is running (`kws.frames` every 30 s) but sees the same near-silence. Code changes: the empty-transcript UI now says `No microphone signal - check the mic input, gain, and 48V phantom power.` instead of the internal `accurate empty`, and the bridge prints `Right-Ctrl is handled by the desktop helper.` when it yields. **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_edit_mic_silence9u.py` + `test_voice_edit_helper9u.py` + `test_voice_edit_stt_mode9u.py` -> **53 passed**; services restarted (bridge `696823`, helper `696816`), `/chat` `200`, bridge log now shows the yielded-helper line. **Not fixed:** no voice turn can succeed until the physical input is corrected; no spoken live turn was made.

**Voice confirmation latch fix (2026-10-29):** The live journal showed the helper stuck after a couple of turns: `Open Messenger.` staged a task, the spoken confirmation was transcribed `Yeah, yes.` and rejected as `other`, then every later wake command hit `voice_edit.wake.pending_guard` until the panel's Cancel cleared the pending action (that is why Cancel made it work again). Root cause: `classify_confirmation` matched only exact phrases, not repeated/padded ASR shapes. It now accepts short all-affirmative sequences (`Yeah, yes.`, `Yes, yes.`, `yeah yep`, `yes do it now`) and repeated negatives (`No, no.`), while unrelated sentences still stay `other`; `chat_transport._is_affirmative` now delegates to the same classifier instead of its separate phrase set. **Verified:** the focused wake/confirm/talk set (`test_voice_session_fsm9u.py`, `test_voice_confirm_session9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_wake9u.py`, `test_voice_wake_media_guard9u.py`, `test_voice_talk_events_wire9u.py`) -> **124 passed**; both services were restarted, no spoken live turn.

**Voice site shortcuts (2026-10-29):** The deterministic local-action `open_website` now covers the owner's sites: `youtube music` -> `https://music.youtube.com` (kept distinct from plain YouTube), `opencode` / `opencode.ai` -> `https://opencode.ai`, and `qubit` / `qubit dashboard` / `qubit web dashboard` -> the local dashboard at `http://127.0.0.1:8890/` (live root redirects `302 -> /chat`). Instagram, Messenger, Gmail and GitHub were already mapped and are now pinned by tests. L0 routes all of these as `local:open_website`, so no model call or confirmation is involved. **ASR repair (same day):** the live journal showed "Pop on messenger." (ASR for "open messenger") reaching the brain, and "Open code." reaching `open_app`/VS Code; `pop on`/`pull up`/`bring up` are now open verbs, and `Open code` / `Open, open code` / `opencode` resolve to `https://opencode.ai` before the bare `code` app alias, while explicit "vs code"/"code editor"/"in code" phrasings still use `open_app`. **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_website_shortcuts9u.py` + `tests/test_voice_browser_open9u.py` + `tests/test_qubitchat_local_actions9u.py` + `tests/test_voice_edit_tool_task9u.py` -> **95 passed**; the staged voice-edit tool is now `local.open_website` for both ASR shapes, not `local.open_app`. Both `qubit-voicebridge.service` and `qubit-voice-typing.service` were restarted to load the routes, but no spoken live turn was made.

**Voice display switch (2026-10-29):** The deterministic local-action `display_switch` moves the **active window** between the two physical outputs: `first`/`primary` = `DP-2` (2580x1080+0+0) and `second` = `HDMI-1` (1920x1080+2580+0). Phrases like `switch tab to first display`, `switch tab to the second display`, `move this window to the second screen`, `send it to the first monitor`, `put this on display 2`, and `switch display to second` route to `local:display_switch` before the browser `tab_switch` patterns, so a display word is never treated as a tab name. The handler resolves outputs through `voicebridge/orb/monitors.py`, reads the focused window with `xdotool`, moves/resizes it inside the target rect with `wmctrl -i -r <id> -e`, re-activates it, and removes/restores `fullscreen` and `maximized_*` state (with a short WM settle) so a maximized browser does not snap back to the old monitor. It fails honestly if X11, `wmctrl`/`xdotool`, a second display, or an active window is missing. **Verified:** `tests/test_voice_display_switch9u.py` + `tests/test_qubitchat_local_actions9u.py` + `tests/test_voice_website_shortcuts9u.py` -> **100 passed**; live direct-handler test moved a disposable `xmessage` window from `X=114` to `X=2606` (HDMI-1) and back to `X=80` (DP-2). Both `qubit-voicebridge.service` and `qubit-voice-typing.service` were restarted to load the route (bridge pid `696823`, helper pid `696816`); root `302`, `/chat` `200`, helper has `DISPLAY=:0` and its `xinput`/`parec` children. No spoken live turn was made; the command moves a window, not an individual browser tab.

**Wave 69 — Five-lane continuation: wake spotter, barge-in takeover, role tiers, matrix, connectors (2026-10-28, `main`):** Five isolated worktrees off `a627e814a`; all branches reviewed and merged.
- **Wake spotter (`c42398453`):** live peaks were 0.03-0.10 with zero `kws.hit`; added boost-only bounded KWS input conditioning (`kws_normalize` default ON, target 0.5, max gain 6) and `kws.frames`/`kws.hit`/`kws.miss` level diagnostics. ASR audio and the wake gate are unchanged. New conditioning tests **14 passed**; merged wake set **181 passed**.
- **Barge-in takeover (`c1cf4deaf`):** the gate now returns the wake remainder, executes it once, and fences the interrupted turn with `_turn_gen`; stale `PREVIEW`/error writes are discarded, a second wake cannot double-emit, bare wake arms, and status returns to idle. Barge/talk set **167 passed**.

**Wave 68 — Wake latency: shorter endpoint + immediate spotter cue (2026-10-28, `main`):** The wake beep was only emitted after a full command was endpointed and transcribed, and an established segment waited 1.8 s of trailing silence; that is why a one-breath "hey qubit, …" felt late while a bare "hey qubit" was fast.
- **Code:** `voicebridge/voice_edit/wake.py` adds opt-in `wake.cue_on_kws` and `wake.cue_on_speech` (both default **off**) using the single-note `ready` cue; the existing `wake` beep remains the one confirmation per executed command and is suppressed for a segment that already cued. `voice_edit/settings.py` exposes/validates both.
- **Live config** (`~/.config/qubit-voicebridge/voice.yaml`, backup saved): `wake.hold_silence_ms: 900` (was implicit 1800), `wake.cue_on_kws: true`, `wake.cue_on_speech: true`; `config/voice.example.yaml` documents them. The helper (`qubit-voice-typing.service`) was restarted (pid 2395701) and the wake monitor reports `engine: kws`, `segmenter: silero`.
- **Why `cue_on_speech`:** the live event log shows every wake is an ASR-fallback segment (`forced: false`, `kws.hit` absent), so the spotter is deaf to this voice; cueing at the VAD speech onset gives feedback in ~300 ms instead of after endpoint+ASR. The cost is that any idle room speech also cues; it is config-off by default.
- **Barge-in follow-up:** barge-in is now **wake-phrase gated**. Confident speech first pauses the player, transcribes a short buffer, and resumes unless the transcript contains "hey qubit"/a custom trigger; only a real wake stops the reply, arms the wake for 8 s, and shortens the TTS echo guard to **1 s**. The HUD "Stopped" pill clears to "Ready" after 1.2 s when idle. Live helper pid 2423940.
- **Verified:** wake set plus barge-in/talk-wire set (`test_voice_edit_wake9u.py`, `test_voice_wake_responsiveness9u.py`, `test_voice_wake_stall9u.py`, `test_voice_wake_settings9u.py`, `test_voice_wake_trailing9u.py`, `test_voice_edit_bargein9u.py`, `test_voice_talk_events_wire9u.py`) **160 passed**; live wake logs `kws.frames` and no errors; the stale `xinput test-xi2` child that blocked a restarted helper's hotkey was removed and the new helper owns it. Not verified by a live spoken turn in this session.
- **Not done / honest limit:** the gate transcribes mixed TTS+user audio; if ASR cannot hear the phrase over the reply, the reply continues and there is no interrupt. A command spoken during the gate is used only to decide/arm, not emitted; the owner may need to repeat it. No full matrix, no `VERIFIED`.

**Wave 59 — RuntimePort Phase 4b: the read-only `monitor_operative` recipe (2026-10-27, branch `feat/p4-monitor-operative`, base `95108edb0`):** Added the second Phase 4 recipe over existing Qubit surfaces. **Shared contract:** `SectionUnavailable`, result normalization, `collect_sections` and the fact/brief renderers moved into `recipes/sections.py`; `RecipeSpec` now carries its deterministic collectors (not serialized) and the runner uses them when no fixture/override is passed, so a recipe no longer has to duplicate the collector orchestration. `morning_digest` keeps its public wrappers and behavior. **`monitor_operative`:** four collectors — `cron` reads `jobs.json` with the store's own parser/normalizer but no repair/ensure-dirs write path (counts, failed jobs, overdue stamps past the 15-minute grace, next runs, ticker heartbeat/last tick error), `kanban` reads the board DB `mode=ro` through `kanban_db.board_stats` (per-status/open counts, blocked, oldest-ready staleness at 24h, tasks with repeated failures; overdue is an explicit `not_supported` fact because the board has no due-date field), `memory` reports the configured provider and whether it is in the installed-provider registry, `compression` reads the session store's durable counters (`ineffective`, `overload`, `fallback`, recovery timers) via `SessionDB(read_only=True)`. Each section degrades independently with `missing_connector` / `connector_not_configured` / `tool_error` / `not_supported`; facts are bounded to the existing limits, redacted through `agent.redact`, and no job/task/session is mutated. The model only writes the brief. **CLI/voice/cron:** `qubit recipe list` now shows `monitor_operative` and `qubit recipe run monitor_operative [--engine] [--dry-run] [--json]` needs no new command code (the registry drives it; exit codes unchanged). An additive `CLI_BRIDGE_REGISTRY` entry (`recipe_monitor_operative`, aliases "what's my operative status", "monitor my work", "status of my work", …) speaks the redacted brief through the existing backend; the shared summary now uses each recipe's own title. `build_cron_job_spec`/`cron_create_command` produce the metadata-only `0 */4 * * *` job and owner command; no job was created or enabled. **Verified (focused, `nice -n 10`):** `qubit_runtime` `tests/recipes` + `tests/traces/test_trace_recipe_integration.py` → **71 passed**; root voice set (`test_recipe_voice_monitor_wave4x.py`, `test_recipe_voice_wave4x.py`, `test_voice_inventory_coverage.py`, `test_voice_cli_family_bridges_wave13.py`, `test_voice_cli_literal_bridges_wave18.py`) → **37 passed**; manual temp-home dry-run printed the four sections and created no cron output/job. The known pre-existing `test_voice_commands8tf::test_cli_families_all_mapped_with_voice_or_reason` (`office` missing from `CLI_COVERAGE`) still fails unchanged. **Not done:** no service restart, no live cron/kanban/memory probe against the owner profile, no full matrix, no `VERIFIED`, and no live cron job created.

**Wave 58 — RuntimePort P3 completion: `qubit chat -q` runs through the port (2026-10-27, branch `feat/p3b-chat-query`, base `8258c57da`):** The single-query execution seam now crosses the RuntimePort. **Native:** `_run_quiet_single_query` (`-Q`) and `CLIChatTurnMixin._chat_run_agent` (non-quiet `-q`, gated on `_single_query_mode`) call the shared `qubit_cli.oneshot.run_agent_through_port`; the caller's agent is returned through a `_CapturingAgent` delegate so the raw result envelope, `messages`, exit mapping and re-raise contract are unchanged. The port carries the same `user_message`/`conversation_history`/`task_id`/`stream_callback`/`persist_user_message`/`moa_config` arguments (empty history is passed as `[]`, not dropped), the display streaming callback and TTS sink keep their existing semantics, and thread-local approval/vault/secret callbacks are re-bound on the port worker thread. **Non-native:** `qubit chat -q --engine openjarvis` (and any registered engine) bypasses `AIAgent` entirely and runs the P2 backend through `_run_port_oneshot`; unknown engines exit 2, an absent/unpinned OpenJarvis is a typed refusal with no native fallback, and native-only flags (`--model`, `--provider`, `--reasoning`, `--toolsets`, endpoints, turn budgets, `--checkpoints`, `--ignore-rules`, `--safe-mode`, `--ignore-user-config`, `--yolo`, `--accept-hooks`, `--pass-session-id`, `--worktree`, `--source`, `--image`) are refused instead of dropped. `--resume` supplies native-store history but the P2 turn is not persisted; the prompt carries the same "no interactive user" bound as `-z`'s clarify callback, and stream-json emits system/text/result events. Interactive chat/TUI still refuses non-native engines. **Verified (focused, `nice -n 10`):** the combined runtime/oneshot/single-query/chat/parser set → **206 passed, 1 skipped**; the new `tests/qubit_cli/test_chat_query_engine.py` → **16 passed**. No service restart, no full matrix, no `VERIFIED`.

**Wave 57 — RuntimePort Phase 6 slice: local traces + offline eval bench (2026-10-27, branch `feat/p6-traces-evals`, base `d23cc4d8e`):** Added the bounded first half of Phase 6. **Traces:** `qubit_runtime/traces/` is a local SQLite store (`$QUBIT_HOME/traces.db`) with a versioned schema/migration, insert, get/list, JSONL export and age/record-count prune. Rows carry id, created_at, session_id, engine, model, bounded prompt/response excerpts, tool-call names/count, latency_ms, prompt/completion tokens, and nullable cost/energy/flops plus success/error_code. **Redaction happens at write** through the existing `agent.redact`/`agent.monitoring.redaction` scrub (force-on, fails closed) and truncation happens after it; no message bodies, raw tool args/results or credentials are stored, and the store has no network path. **Opt-in only:** default off; `traces.enabled: true` or `QUBIT_TRACES_ENABLED=1` enables it, `QUBIT_TRACES_DB` can relocate the file. Missing/bad stores and bad inputs are typed errors (`trace_store_missing`, `trace_store_corrupt`, `trace_store_version_unsupported`, `trace_not_found`, `invalid_trace`), never crashes; read commands never create a store. **Eval bench:** `qubit_runtime/evals/bench/` reads a JSONL dataset (`--dataset`) or the trace store (`--from-traces`) and reports sample count, accuracy only when labels exist (explicit `correct` or normalized exact match), latency p50/p95 (linear interpolation), prompt/completion token totals/means, cost when reported, and an explicit `unavailable` status for energy and FLOPs when the source did not report them. It makes **no model calls and no network calls**; an optimization run is explicitly `unavailable` (no DSPy/optimizer is installed or vendored). **CLI:** `qubit traces list|show|export|prune` and `qubit evals run --dataset FILE [--json] | --from-traces`; exits 0 ok / 2 typed refusal or bad input / 1 failed run. **Integration:** `recipes.runner.run_recipe` records one trace after a real turn behind the opt-in gate (latency measured; usage/energy/flops only when the engine reports them); default behaviour is unchanged and `qubit -z` remains unwired (its envelope exposes no usage). **Verified (focused, `nice -n 10`):** `tests/traces` + the three new eval files → **41 passed**; the final combined command (`tests/traces tests/evals/test_bench_dataset.py tests/evals/test_bench_metrics.py tests/evals/test_bench_cli.py tests/recipes tests/runtime tests/qubit_cli/test_argparse_flag_propagation.py tests/qubit_cli/test_parser_help_home.py`) → **169 passed**. Manual CLI: `evals run` JSON report rendered; `traces list` with no store exits **2** with `trace_store_missing` and creates nothing. Provenance for the adapted OpenJarvis trace field set/eval metric names is in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`; no OpenJarvis code is vendored or imported. **Not done:** no `qubit -z` integration, no optimizer/learning run, no energy/FLOPs sampler, no service restart, no full matrix, no `VERIFIED`.

**Wave 56 — RuntimePort Phase 5 first connector: read-only Google Contacts (branch `feat/p5-google-contacts`, base `4584ebe19`):** Added the first Phase 5 connector: `qubit_runtime/connectors/google_contacts.py` (`GoogleContactsConnector` with `configured()`, `search(query, limit)` and `list(limit)`, plus a bounded `Contact` row) and the registry tool `qubit_runtime/tools/google_contacts_tool.py` (`google_contacts`, toolset `google_contacts`, `check_fn` = existing token present). **Credential path is reused, not duplicated:** it reads `$QUBIT_HOME/google_token.json` (resolved at call time via `get_qubit_home()`) and refreshes an expired token through google-auth's authorized-user machinery, writing the refreshed payload back to that same file; no second OAuth flow, token store or `~/.openjarvis` path. The scope preflight accepts `contacts.readonly` or the broader `contacts` scope and returns typed `missing_scope` naming `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/contacts.readonly` (never a silent empty list); no token is `missing_credential`; HTTP 401/403/429 are `unauthorized`/`forbidden`/`rate_limited`, timeouts are `timeout`, other statuses `http_error`, bad payloads `invalid_response`. Reads only: People API `connections.list` and `people:searchContacts` GETs; there is no create/update/delete/send path. Results are bounded (list ≤100 / search ≤30 People API cap, page size 100 for list, names 200, emails 320 ×5, phones 64 ×5, org/title 200) and error details are redacted/bounded with no response body or token; tests pin that token text never reaches the error. Integration: `toolsets.py` (`_QUBIT_CORE_TOOLS` + a `google_contacts` toolset) and `qubit_cli/tools_config.py` expose it to default CLI/voice sessions when the token exists. Provenance for the adapted OpenJarvis People API field mapping is in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`; no OpenJarvis code is vendored or imported. **Verified (focused, `nice -n 10`, shared venv):** `cd qubit_runtime && python -m pytest -q tests/connectors` → **45 passed**; the combined focused set (`tests/connectors tests/tools/test_registry.py "tests/tools/test_tool_search.py::TestClassification" tests/qubit_cli/test_tools_config.py tests/tools/test_toolsets.py tests/tools/test_toolset_distributions.py tests/tools/test_model_tools.py`) → **184 passed, 6 skipped**. Manual discovery check with a temp `QUBIT_HOME`: a token yields `['google_contacts']` in `get_tool_definitions(enabled_toolsets=["google_contacts"])`, and no token yields `[]`. **Not done:** no live Google probe (no owner credentials in this worktree); no service restart; no full matrix; no `VERIFIED`.
