**Wave 54 — RuntimePort P3: the `qubit -z` oneshot path runs through the port (2026-10-26, `feat/p3-oneshot-port` off `7facb6a9f`):** Routed the simpler oneshot path (`qubit -z` / `--oneshot`, including `_run_oneshot_from_args`, the light and Termux parsers) through `RuntimePort` with the native backend, and added `--engine` (default `native`) to the top-level and chat parsers. The converted seam is `qubit_cli/oneshot.py::_run_agent`: its agent construction is now a factory and the turn is `RuntimeBackend.run_turn(TurnRequest(...))`; a `_CapturingAgent` delegate retains the raw result envelope (so `--usage-file`, `messages`, exit mapping and the historical re-raise behavior are unchanged) while the port supplies the bounded event stream. Default `-z` keeps the old wire call shape: the native adapter omits `None` `system_message`/`task_id`, passes `conversation_history` exactly as before, and does not enable `stream_callback` unless a delta sink is supplied (`native_stream`), so provider calls stay non-streaming. The adapter now propagates the caller's `contextvars` into its worker thread (the one-shot stateless-delivery marker would otherwise reset). `--engine openjarvis` uses the P2 backend via the registry with no in-process import; a missing/unpinned CLI is a typed refusal printed as `[openjarvis_unavailable] …` plus its install hint and exits 2, with no native fallback. Unknown engines exit 2 and list the registered engines; non-native failed turns exit 1 and cancelled turns 130; native keeps the historical `-z` mapping (2 for failed/partial, 130 interrupted). Explicit native-only flags (`--model`, `--provider`, `--reasoning`, `--toolsets`) are refused on a non-native engine (exit 2) instead of being silently dropped; `qubit chat`/`-q` accepts `--engine` but refuses anything except `native` (that path is not ported yet). **Verified (focused, `nice -n 10`):** `tests/runtime` + all oneshot files + parser/flag-propagation/single-query files → **205 passed** (includes 12 new engine tests plus a contextvars pin). **Not done:** no service restart, no full matrix; `qubit chat -q` is still native-only; OpenJarvis turns are not written to the native session store (P2 is stateless), so `--resume` supplies history but does not persist the new turn. No `VERIFIED`.

**Wave 53 — Voice Edit panel: live words while speaking, new UI/UX (2026-10-26, `feat/voice-edit-ui` → `54cf64b0c`):** `voicebridge/voice_edit/overlay.py` is now a review-focused panel: dark Qubit palette, status pill (Listening/Thinking/Review/Done/Error), live voice meter, borderless flat actions with a primary Apply (review actions disabled until a preview exists), detected/model/privacy chips, a word-level add/remove diff with counts, compact two-line history rows with per-kind icons and timestamps, error/detail banners, and automatic listening-vs-preview window sizing. Words now stream **while the user is still speaking**: the helper shows the panel on the hotkey, forwards every STT partial (`set_live_transcript`), and the STT client composes cumulative segment text (`replace`/`segment` honoured; raw `partials` unchanged) before the final rescore replaces it. The wake path shows its final words immediately, and the live capture level drives the meter at ~12 Hz. `tests`: **757 passed** across the Voice Edit/STT/overlay set; new focused tests cover overlay structure/actions/history on a real display, partial ordering before final, panel-before-first-partial, wake final, level meter, and cumulative STT segments. Visual check via Tk on `:0` (listening + preview states; screenshots `/tmp/opencode/new4-*.png`). **Not live yet:** the panel runs in `qubit-voice-typing.service`, which was not restarted (out of the authorized restart set); run `systemctl --user restart qubit-voice-typing` to load it.

**Wave 52 — OpenJarvis tests: the adapted surfaces are now pinned by upstream scenarios (2026-10-26, `feat/openjarvis-test-port` → `7773ed559`):** Reviewed `open-jarvis/OpenJarvis@0583901` tests for the two surfaces we actually adapted and ported the missing **scenarios** as Qubit-native tests (no upstream import, no network, local git repos/fake CLIs). Skills (7 new): partial/empty search, non-skill dirs, absent-frontmatter fallback, initial-clone failure, empty cache cannot hide a failure, success clears the stale warning, failed refresh still serves the cached bundle + commit, cache cannot satisfy a missing skill. Runtime (4 new): empty successful stdout is malformed, unknown agent is a non-zero error (not done), a binary removed after resolve is a typed `openjarvis_unavailable`, and configured model/tools reach the argv/`done` payload. Provenance for the adapted test paths added to `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`. **Verified:** `tests/runtime` + `tests/tools/test_skills_hub_git_sources.py` → **88 passed**; focused skills hub → **144 passed, 1 skipped**; merged-main focused set → **111 passed**. **Not ported (documented):** their importer/trust gate, eval telemetry/energy sampler, OpenClaw `payloads` runner, GitHub recursive resolver, commit-drift override, live external suite — these belong to layers Qubit deliberately owns or did not port.

**Wave 51 — Cron messages: structured, transport-aware, and readable (2026-10-26, `feat/cron-message-polish` → `23308b56c`):** New `qubit_runtime/cron/notice_format.py` builds one `CronNotice` and renders it per transport: a one-line headline with exactly one status icon (⏰/▶️/✅/⚠️/❌/⏸️/🔁/🧾), a bounded 1–3 line summary (no wall of text; clear `…`), bold labelled details (Job/Schedule/Duration/Next run/Run id), local absolute + relative next-run times, humanised durations, and links only from an existing configured `dashboard.public_url` (credentials stripped). Telegram keeps the existing MarkdownV2 converter and disables link previews for cron; WhatsApp uses single-asterisk bold; Signal/SMS/email/unknown platforms get a Markdown-free plain fallback; Yuanbao still strips the legacy wrapper and passes new notices through. Redaction stays before excerpt bounding; machine-readable receipts/status are untouched. **Verified (lane):** `test_cron_notice_format.py` **23 passed**; the 34-file cron/gateway notification matrix **599 passed, 1 skipped**. **Verified (merged main):** notice tests + key cron/Telegram/Yuanbao delivery tests **111 passed**. **Live:** `qubit-gateway` + `qubit-voicebridge` restarted at 22:47; identify `state=ready`, protocol 1, `authority_epoch=25`; `/chat?resume=20261019_094333_0a4efc` → 200; Telegram `Connected to Telegram (polling mode)`; the new format applies to deliveries after the restart.

**Wave 50 — RuntimePort Phase 2: the out-of-process OpenJarvis engine (2026-10-26, `feat/openjarvis-backend`):** Added `agent/runtime_openjarvis.py` — a pinned, out-of-process adapter that spawns `jarvis ask --json --no-stream --no-context --agent "" -- <prompt>` (OpenJarvis `05839014127772bd077ba9ba2a2393a007f63ac6`, Apache-2.0; provenance in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`). No `openjarvis` import enters the Qubit process; Qubit owns session state and renders system/history into the single CLI prompt. Events map to `delta` (whole answer, `capabilities.streaming=False` is honest), per-tool `tool`, bounded `usage` from the direct-engine result, and exactly one `done`/`error`. Refusals are typed and never fall back to native: `openjarvis_unavailable`, `openjarvis_commit_unverifiable`, `openjarvis_checkout_dirty`, `openjarvis_commit_mismatch`, `openjarvis_binary_mismatch`, `openjarvis_interface_mismatch`, `openjarvis_invalid_prompt`, `openjarvis_prompt_too_large`, `openjarvis_home_unavailable`. The executable resolves absolutely and must match both the clean pinned checkout and its `g<sha>` `--version` stamp. Cancel/timeout/KeyboardInterrupt terminate and SIGKILL the whole process group (a SIGTERM-ignoring descendant cannot survive), output is capped (2 MB stdout / 200 KB stderr), prompts are byte-capped, secrets are redacted before truncation, and a successful child whose descendant holds the pipes completes via a bounded post-exit drain instead of waiting out the budget. `qubit runtime engines` annotates unavailable engines; `qubit runtime run --engine openjarvis` exits 2 on a refusal and 130 on Ctrl-C. **Verified:** `tests/runtime` → **70 passed**; three independent read-only adversarial reviews, each with real findings fixed and pinned (factory/verify paths, descendant reaping, double-terminal guard, git/probe decode, constructor symlink loop, test honesty). Live against a real OpenJarvis 0583901 editable install (`/tmp/openjavis`-equivalent venv, `OPENJARVIS_HOME` isolated) and Ollama `huihui_ai/llama3.2-abliterate:3b`: `QUBIT_OPENJARVIS_BIN=…/jarvis QUBIT_OPENJARVIS_SOURCE=/tmp/openjarvis … python -m qubit_cli.main runtime run --engine openjarvis --prompt "Reply with exactly: PORT_OJ_OK5" --json` → `delta PORT_OJ_OK5`, `usage {prompt_tokens:34, completion_tokens:6}`, `done {completed:true, commit:0583901…, binary:…/jarvis}`; missing binary → typed refusal, exit 2; `runtime engines` → `native` + `openjarvis (unavailable: openjarvis_unavailable)`. **Not done:** no product caller routes through the port yet (Phase 3); no service restart needed (diagnostic-only; nothing live changed); no `VERIFIED`.

**Wave 49 — RuntimePort Phase 1: the engine boundary, native backend, and diagnostic CLI (2026-10-26, `feat/runtime-port`):** Implemented the first phase of the runtime-boundary program: `agent/runtime_port.py` (TurnRequest/TurnEvent/RuntimeCapabilities/RuntimeBackend, typed `UnknownRuntimeError`), `agent/runtime_native.py` (native `AIAgent` adapter: `stream_callback` deltas, bounded `done`/`error` data, `interrupt`-based cancellation with a bounded uncooperative-cancel fallback, no thread leak on normal completion), `agent/runtime_registry.py`, and the diagnostic `qubit runtime engines|run --engine native` CLI (`qubit_cli/subcommands/runtime.py`, `runtime_cmd.py`, wired in `main._build_cli_parser`). No product chat/oneshot/gateway caller changed. **Verified:** `tests/runtime` → **22 passed** (factory failure yields one `error`, uncooperative cancel yields one terminal within the grace window, bounded `data` keys, ordered deltas, unknown engine/missing prompt/missing query-file exit 2, parser wiring); focused chat checks (`test_cli_context_reference_passthrough`, `test_cli_codex_context_reference`) → **18 passed**; live diagnostic with the live profile (`QUBIT_HOME=~/.local/state/qubit-voicebridge/runtime`) → `{"engine":"native","events":[{"kind":"delta","text":"PORT_OK"},{"kind":"done","text":"PORT_OK","data":{"completed":true,...}}]}` on `deepseek-v4.1-flash`. A fresh read-only reviewer rejected the first cut for two real defects — the agent factory ran outside the `try` (zero terminal events on a missing-credential failure) and an agent that ignores `interrupt` could hang the consumer forever — both fixed (factory inside the turn `try`; a cancel watcher synthesises one `error` after a bounded grace, first terminal wins), with tests pinning both. Non-blocking findings also fixed (data-key bounding, parser test, query-file error, watcher release on normal completion, docs). **Not done:** Phase 2 (OpenJarvis backend), recipe/connector/learning phases; no service restart needed for this diagnostic-only phase.

**Wave 48 — OpenJarvis skill sources ported; Telegram handle rebranded; owner waived the clean full-matrix run (2026-10-26, `canonical/main` `4d768d778`):** **Owner decision:** skip the single uninterrupted full-matrix run and accept the recorded split-pass evidence (completed full pass + every failing file re-run green alone); `VERIFIED` remains unclaimed by that choice, and Windows stays out of scope (ruling 11). **Skill sources:** reviewed `open-jarvis/OpenJarvis@0583901` (`src/openjarvis/skills/sources/{base,hermes,openclaw}.py`, Apache-2.0) and ported the *resolver semantics* — clone/pull cache, layout walk, frontmatter preview, commit pin, stale-cache refresh fallback, OpenClaw `_meta.json` sidecar — into `qubit_runtime/tools/skills_hub_git_sources.py` as two `SkillSource` adapters (`hermes`, `openclaw`). The Qubit hub (GitHub auth, index, trust, SSRF guards, bundle validation, installer) is unchanged; the OpenJarvis hub was **not** substituted because it is smaller/less defended. `skills_hub_search.create_source_router()` registers both; `_select_active_sources` keeps them out of an unfiltered search (a whole repo clone only happens on an explicit `--source` or `hermes:`/`openclaw:` identifier). CLI choices, `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` pin, and tests added. **Verified:** `tests/tools/test_skills_hub_git_sources.py` → **7 passed** (local git repos only; bare-name ambiguity, no-clone-on-search, sidecar, stale-cache fallback); focused hub regression (`tests/qubit_cli/test_skills_hub.py`, `test_skills_install_flags.py`, `tests/tools/test_skills_hub.py`, `test_skills_hub_git_sources.py`) → **137 passed, 1 skipped**; end-to-end with local origins: `skills search --source hermes` returned `hermes:demo`, `skills install hermes:research/demo --yes` → scan SAFE → installed `SKILL.md` + `references/notes.md`, and `skills install openclaw:acme/demo --yes` → installed `odemo`. **Telegram:** BotFather `/setusername` does not exist (usernames are permanent); created a new bot **@QubitBot** (display name `Qubit`), replaced `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN` in `runtime/.env`, restarted; `getMe` → `QubitBot`, gateway `Connected to Telegram (polling mode)`. **Wave 33 — dashboard request storm fixed; 56+67 → 2+3 requests per load (2026-10-23, `main`):** Follow-up to the wave-32 browser E2E observation. **Root cause (measured live with Playwright):** the durable `/api/events/stream` replays ~1,913 historical journal frames on connect, and every `session.updated` / `model.changed` frame triggered a `/api/model/info` read and — through the reasoning-picker key — a `/api/config` read. **Residual-gateway wave 6 — `same_create_retry` + `session.create` contract (2026-10-20, branch `fix/residual-gw-wave6`, NOT merged):** Two residuals named by the 5-lane wave. **(1) `same_create_retry` was imported but never defined.** Porting `30103117b` brought the two call sites (`gateway/session_local.py:269`, `gateway/session_policy.py:704`) without the helper trio they call. Recovered verbatim from upstream `e6f57427:hermes_state_local.py` and brand-adapted: `client_request`, `same_create_request`, `same_create_retry` (`qubit_state_local.py`), comparing decoded JSON (key order irrelevant), an owner-resolved alias by the name the client typed rather than its derived route, and the retry's `client_env` by the rules frozen with the committed policy. **Proof:** on `main:0b5ebc401`, `cd qubit_runtime && python -c "from qubit_state_local import same_create_retry"` → `ImportError`; on the branch it prints `ok same_create_retry`. Focused `test_state_local_create_retry.py` (8) + `test_session_create_retry.py` (3, both live call sites incl. `create_local_session` retry/conflict and the `bind_launch_key` owner-key branch) pass. **(2) `SessionCreateResult` `extra_forbidden` on the boot path.** Root cause measured at `6a8dbf5c1^`: `contracts/__init__.py` imported the topic modules but not `canonical`, so `CANONICAL_METHODS` had **1** entry and no `session.create`; the canonical dispatcher fell back to `METHODS['session.create']` (`SessionCreateResult`) and the canonical snapshot logged **10** validation errors (`running`, `authority_epoch`, `replay_epoch`, `last_sequence`, `subscription_id`, `revision`, `execution_generation`, `pending`, `prompts` extra; `info.title` type). The wave's `6a8dbf5c1` (`from . import canonical`) is the correct-side fix and is already in `main` (`CANONICAL_METHODS` 47 entries, `session.create → CanonicalSnapshot`); a real `AuthorityConnection.dispatch('session.create')` on a scratch home now validates with no contract log. Added `test_session_create_contract.py` (2) pinning the registration and the live result. No production change was needed for (2) — the residual text was stale. Focused run: 14 passed (both retry files + contract test + `test_resume_alias_remote_config.py`). No service restarted; no global E2E/release verdict.

**Dead-symbol wave 6 — 216 → 170, worker output-log port (2026-10-20, branch `fix/dead-symbols-wave6`):** Nine committed batches on `0b5ebc401`. The detector (`qubit_runtime/scripts/check_dead_symbols.py --json /tmp/dead-wave6-final.json -v`) measures `dead_count` **216 → 170**: **46 resolved, 0 new** (set diff). One of the 13 deferred architectural ports was taken end-to-end — the **worker output-log family** (`worker_output_log`, `open_output_log`, `record_exit_command`, `adoptable_log`, `ProcessOutputLogMixin`): a managed worker's child writes to a profile log, the shell records its exit beside it, checkpoint writes merge foreign handoffs under the cross-process lock, and the next interpreter adopts and tails the log even when the PID is gone/recycled. New hermetic tests cover the owner/worker checkpoint merge, successor adoption, and a real worker spawn recording output + exit. The other 41 wired symbols are the previously-empty upstream call sites (session command sink, inbound @-reference expansion, restart-wait/downgrade watcher, served-profile record rule, kanban owner fences, left-core plugin aliases, gateway counts, TUI model-friction/theme/answering-client sinks, Google Meet queue lock, debug paste sweep, and more). Triage of the ~55 triaged-but-not-wired symbols (false positives, correctly-unwired seams, and the rest deferred with named blockers) is in `qubit_runtime/scripts/_dead_symbols/TRIAGE.md` §Wave 6. **No deletions**, so no Declared-exclusions entry. No service restarted. Pre-existing failures encountered were re-confirmed byte-identical on a detached `0b5ebc401` worktree; wiring the answering-client sink additionally turned `test_protocol::test_window_owned_request_refuses_at_once_when_no_window_shows_the_session` from fail to pass. No global E2E/release verdict.

the live `/chat` session is unaffected). **Still open, measured:** 216 dead symbols (13 deferred architectural ports, each needing multi-call-site work and recorded with upstream evidence), 1,691 test symbols / 804 files (non-blocking by the audit's rule), retirement not closed, and three residuals named by the lanes: `gateway/session_local.py`/`session_policy.py` import `same_create_retry` from `qubit_state_local` which does not define it (pre-existing ImportError on an exact `session.create` retry/resume path), a `SessionCreateResult` `extra_forbidden` contract-noise log on `session.create`, and the `test_source_release_channels/probe` pre-existing failures untouched.
