**`/chat` + gateway deadlock after the bridge restart — FIXED (2026-10-20):** Owner: the resumed session `20261019_094333_0a4efc` would not load, `/chat` was dead and the gateway was not working. Measured cause: `tui_gateway.entry.main()` (ported call site `f12f12a46`) reserved the canonical home's `gateway.lock`, while the VoiceBridge architecture deliberately runs a **separate** messaging gateway on that same home and spawns one stdio TUI per `/chat` terminal (`voicebridge/pty_lease_shim.py`: the PTY child is the writer). Whichever side started second exited: `/chat` TUIs refused while `qubit-gateway.service` owned the lock (spawn/exit storm, terminal stuck "stopped"), and after the gateway was stopped the surviving TUI held the lock so the gateway could never restart (journal: `Gateway runtime lock is already held by another instance. Exiting.`). Fix: `entry.main()` skips the exclusive reservation for dashboard-spawned terminals (`HERMES_TUI_DASHBOARD` / `QUBIT_TUI_DASHBOARD`, both set by `qubit_cli/web_server_chat.py::_resolve_chat_argv`); standalone `qubit --tui` still claims and refuses exactly as before. **Verified:** `qubit_runtime/tests/tui_gateway/test_entry_claims_launch_home.py` → 4 passed (new parametrized case holds the lock from a separate process and proves the dashboard terminal still serves without claiming it). Live: cleared the orphaned TUIs, restarted `qubit-gateway.service` (active), reloaded the resume URL — transcript renders, status line `ready`, sidebar `Gateway Status: Running`; then **restarted the gateway while two dashboard TUI gateways were live** and both survived (lock now held by the gateway pid, no `lock is already held` error). Session history verified in `state.db` (689 messages; last two turns, "deploy 5 agents and continue" and "continue", had failed during the outage with "Your request was not processed"). Still open/non-blocking: the served `frontend/qubit-dist` bundle predates two frontend commits (`1d08564ec`, `9fc177577`) and the boot check warns STALE/BROKEN; it was not rebuilt here. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Bridge restarted — TTS/streaming/warm changes now live (2026-10-19/20):** With the dashboard agents finished, `qubit-voicebridge.service` was restarted (new bridge pid 2467557, `:8890` listening, HTTP 302) — which also restarted the orb (`PartOf`; new pid 2467558, painting at ~70% CPU, no errors). The helper was left running (pid 2462863, still carries the on-demand speak gate). This makes live: the ONE-copy killable TTS worker (all `/api/audio/speak` through it), the abortable `chat_local`/`chat_local_stream`/raw `/api/generate`+`/api/chat` adapters, the earlier + unkeyed `warm_tts_worker` hooks, and the keep-warm-by-default worker (no cold start after idle). All three units active. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Live helper-playback STOP proof — PASS (2026-10-19, owner-approved helper restart):** The last unproven branch is now proven live. With owner approval the **helper only** was restarted (`qubit-voice-typing.service`, new pid 2462863) with the safety gate set (`QUBIT_ORB_HELPER_SPEAK=1` via the transient user-manager env); the dashboard/bridge was NOT restarted (`:8890` stayed up). Preflight confirmed the new helper has both the gate in `/proc/<pid>/environ` and the `SIGRTMIN+3` handler in `SigCgt`. Then `verify_orb_stop_live.py --helper-speak "Qubit helper live stop test."` (double-gated) reported **PASS**: `pid=2462863 state=idle gate=True speak_handler=True`; the helper spoke on demand through its own `_speak`/player (`playback.started=True`, `live_before_stop=True`); STOP → `helper_alive_after=True`; canonical triple `present=True ordered=True latency=0.135s`. Evidence `output/orb_capture/stop_live/20261019-235853-helper/{evidence.json,timeline.txt}`. Honest note: the helper's player child was not visible in `/proc`, so `player_ended`/`stop_to_exit` fall back to the journal playback boundaries (the triple is the authoritative proof). The transient gate was unset afterwards (the running helper keeps it until its next restart; re-set `QUBIT_ORB_HELPER_SPEAK=1` to re-prove). **Still not live:** the TTS-worker / streaming-abort / earlier-warm changes live in the BRIDGE process and need `qubit-voicebridge.service` restarted to take effect — deliberately not done (other agents are using the dashboard). No global E2E/release verdict.

**Last orb residuals closed — streaming socket owned, earlier/unkeyed TTS warm, worker kept warm (2026-10-19, 3 agents):** **(1) Streaming abort owns its socket.** `chat_local_stream` no longer discovers the socket via `urlopen` private internals: when a cancel hook is supplied it opens an `http.client` `AbortableHTTPStream` the module owns and aborts via `sock.shutdown(SHUT_RDWR)`; with no hook the `urlopen` path is unchanged. Grep proves no `_sock`/`fp.raw`/`_response_socket` leftovers. Measured unblock 7–10 ms (30 s timeout). **(2) TTS warms earlier and for unkeyed.** `warm_tts_worker` is now called at the earliest voice requests (session `begin`, `capture`, `transform`, `route`, plus unkeyed `transcribe`/`speak` arrival), all background/no-op-if-warm. Measured first reply after a 300 s reap: fast deterministic answer **1975 ms → 615 ms**; unkeyed voice turn **2047 ms → 370 ms**. **(3) The one worker is kept warm by default.** The idle reaper default moved from 300 s to **0 (disabled)**, so the next reply never pays a cold reload (`QUBIT_TTS_SYNTH_WORKER_IDLE_S>0` opts back in). This is still ONE model copy (the only copy), just resident. **(4) Live helper-playback STOP branch now reachable.** A gated `SIGRTMIN+3` asks the running helper to speak a bounded canned text through its real `_speak`/player (unconditional handler = safe no-op when ungated; helper-side `QUBIT_ORB_HELPER_SPEAK=1` gate; 0600 file in the 0700 state dir; text ≤400 chars, ≤15 s old, one-shot), and `verify_orb_stop_live.py --helper-speak TEXT` (double-gated) drives it and asserts the canonical triple. **Verified:** abort-inflight/streaming + ask-abort/cancel + fullturn + tts-worker + speakable + stop-speaking + capture-ipc + stop-live → **203 passed**; tts-worker set **69 passed**; helper `stop_speaking9u`+`orb_capture_ipc9u`+`stop_live9u` **88 passed**; `test_voice_talk_events_wire9u` 87. **NOT applied live — no service restart** (owner: other agents on the dashboard). **Only item left, by the no-restart constraint:** the running helper (started before this code) has no `SIGRTMIN+3` handler, so the helper-playback live PASS reads **DEGRADED** until the helper is next restarted; the mechanism is complete and hermetically pinned, and it refuses safely on the old helper. No global E2E/release verdict.

**On-demand helper speak closes the live helper-playback STOP gap — mechanism landed, live run honestly DEGRADED (2026-10-19, branch `feat/orb-helper`):** The live stop E2E could only drive *service* TTS through the host player (or the helper's private demo instance), so the RUNNING helper's own `playback.started`→canonical-triple branch had never been exercised without a mic. **(1) Safe gated mechanism.** A new real-time signal (`SIGRTMIN+3` = 37, distinct from SIGUSR1 capture / SIGUSR2 stop) asks the running helper to speak one short canned text. The helper installs the handler UNCONDITIONALLY (so an un-gated signal is a safe no-op, never the real-time default *terminate*), but `on_orb_helper_speak` refuses unless the process carries `QUBIT_ORB_HELPER_SPEAK=1`, is IDLE, is not already playing, and finds a fresh bounded request. The request is `orb_helper_speak.json` in the 0700 canonical state dir, written atomically mode 0600, `text` <= 400 chars, file <= 2048 bytes, `ts` <= 15 s old; it is consumed (unlinked) once. Speech runs on a daemon thread through the SAME `_speak` path (new turn epoch, canonical `playback.started`/triple), never in the signal handler. **(2) `OrbClient.request_helper_speak(text)`** validates text, writes the restricted file, and signals the same cmdline-validated heartbeat pid (typed `ORB_BAD_SPEAK_TEXT`/`ORB_HELPER_NOT_RUNNING`/`ORB_SPEAK_REQUEST_FAILED`; no network surface, text never logged). **(3) Harness `--helper-speak TEXT`**, double-gated by the CLI flag + `QUBIT_ORB_LIVE_ALLOW_SPEAK_TEST=1`: resolves the live helper, proves the `SIGRTMIN+3` handler via `SigCgt`, reads the helper's `/proc/environ` gate, refuses busy helpers, waits for the helper's own `playback.started`, proves the playback live, sends the validated SIGUSR2 STOP, waits for the ordered triple, checks helper survival and player-child exit (`/proc/<pid>/children`) — all no mic, no synthetic input, no restart. **Live run on this host: DEGRADED, not fabricated** — the running helper (pid 3295807, started 13:29, old code, no `QUBIT_ORB_HELPER_SPEAK`) has no handler for signal 37 and no gate, so the harness refused to signal; evidence `/tmp/orbhelperlive/20261019-215203-helper/evidence.json`. Closing that to PASS needs an owner-approved helper restart/reload. **Verified:** `test_voice_orb_stop_speaking9u.py` + `test_orb_capture_ipc9u.py` + `test_voice_orb_stop_live9u.py` → **88 passed**; plus `test_voice_talk_events_wire9u.py` → 87 passed. **Open:** the helper env gate/request handler only reaches a running helper after a restart (the point of the gate); `player_ended` falls back to journal boundaries when `/proc` children are unavailable. No service restart; no global E2E/release verdict.

**Further orb open items — all local LLM paths abortable, warm TTS, dead-trail cleanup (2026-10-19, 4 agents):** Follow-up to the previous pass's residuals. **(1) Every local LLM path is now abortable.** `chat_local_stream` gained a `cancel=` hook that `shutdown`s the live response socket (not just between-line checks) and raises typed `LocalLlmCancelled`; a new exported `local_llm.post_json(..., cancel=)` seam; and `gateway._local_generate`/`_local_chat_raw` now route their previously-inline `urlopen` reads through `post_json(cancel=...)`, with `cancel` threaded through `_local_dispatch` and `LocalLlmCancelled` mapped to typed `CANCELLED` (was `PROVIDER_OUTAGE`). Measured unblock on a loopback slow server (30 s timeout): `chat_local` 10.2 ms, `chat_local_stream` 3.1 ms / `should_stop` 8.0 ms, raw `/api/generate` 10.2 ms, raw `/api/chat` 8.6 ms. No-hook path is byte-identical/urlopen-patch-compatible. **(2) Cold TTS after idle is hidden.** `synth_worker.warm_async(cfg)` starts the worker load in a daemon thread (no-op if live/loading; no duplicate/third copy, no parent provider load); `service.ask` calls it at a keyed turn's start, so the model loads while the brain works. Measured first reply after a 300 s reap: **3.424 s → ~0** when the turn lasts ≥ the load (2.0 s turn → 1.429 s; 0.5 s turn → 2.922 s); `warm_async` returns in 0.2–2 ms. **(3) Dead trail API removed.** `rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` (and the dead `_rgb_trail`/constants) are deleted from the field, the `mark.py` imports/`__all__` re-exports and their tests; confirmed no remaining consumers. `mark_field.__all__` / `mark.__all__` have no missing names. **Verified:** abort-inflight + ask-abort + ask-cancel + fullturn + tts-worker + stop-live + mark9u + fx_contract9u + tk_turn9u → **245 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; Qt individually `qt9u` 24, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 43, `qt_turn9u` 24, `fx_perf9u` 1. One pre-existing fake-server flake under host load was fixed by widening the wait to 10 s (3/3 stable runs). **NOT applied live — no service restart** (owner: other agents on the dashboard). **Remaining, precisely:** warming only fully hides loads shorter than the brain turn; a fast deterministic answer still waits out the remaining load; unkeyed dashboard speech is not pre-warmed; `chat_local_stream` socket discovery uses `urlopen` internals and falls back to between-chunk checks if unavailable. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb open items actually closed — one TTS copy, killed model call, live stop PASS (2026-10-19, 3 agents):** The three residuals from the previous pass, now fixed (not deferred). **(1) Two TTS model copies → one.** `h_audio_speak` now routes EVERY `/api/audio/speak` request — keyed orb turns and unkeyed dashboard calls — through the persistent killable worker, so the service process no longer builds/loads a TTS provider for speaking (`_tts_from_cfg`/`load()` is reachable only via the `QUBIT_TTS_SYNTH_WORKER=0` opt-out). Measured with real `piper`/`en_US-amy-medium`: total active **449–472 MiB (2 copies) → 271–289 MiB (1 copy)**, parent **~226 MiB → ~25 MiB**, idle **~25 MiB** after reap; a keyed follow-up after an unkeyed call dropped from ~3.4 s cold to **0.4–0.9 s** (worker already warm). **(2) The in-flight model call is now KILLED, not abandoned.** `local_llm.chat_local` accepts the turn's cancel hook and runs over an owned `http.client` socket with a 10 ms watcher that `shutdown`s it (raising `LocalLlmCancelled`); `frontier.AbortableHTTPTransport` is the gateway default and shuts its socket mid-response (typed `CANCELLED`); the cancel is threaded through `gateway._local_complete`/`_local_dispatch`/`run`/`_fallback`. With no hook the exact `urllib` path is byte-identical. Measured unblock **~0.010 s** (local and frontier) vs the 30 s timeout. **(3) Live stop now reaches PASS.** `scripts/verify_orb_stop_live.py` gained a double-gated `--speak-test TEXT` (CLI flag + `QUBIT_ORB_LIVE_ALLOW_SPEAK_TEST=1`): it resolves the live helper, proves `SigCgt` has SIGUSR2, refuses if the helper is mid-turn, asks the RUNNING service for real TTS, plays the WAV through the real host player, then delivers STOP (`/api/interrupt` + gated SIGUSR2 + player terminate) and verifies playback ended. Observed live: **PASS — real service TTS + real host player stopped; `stop_to_exit=0.0025 s`**, helper survived. The helper-owned `playback.started`→triple branch is asserted when a real helper playback is in flight (hermetic pin); it is not fabricated on the service-player path. **Verified:** tts-worker + speakable + provider-cache + ask-abort + abort-inflight + ask-cancel + fullturn + stop-live → **129 passed**; helper/bargein/abort set → 80 passed; `test_voice_abort_inflight9u.py` 8; `test_voice_orb_stop_live9u.py` 35; broad `ask_abort+ask_cancel+fullturn+voice_edit*` → 683 passed (1 unrelated provider failure byte-identical on base). **NOT applied live — no service restart** (owner: other agents on the dashboard). **Remaining, precisely:** `chat_local_stream` checks `should_stop` only between lines; `_local_generate`/`_local_chat_raw` remain `urllib`; the worker holds one copy while active (now the ONLY copy) and reloads after a long idle (300 s default) or a stop; a transport without `abort` (injected fakes) keeps cooperative checks. No global E2E/release verdict.

**A STOP now kills the in-flight model call, not just its reply (2026-10-19, branch `feat/orb-abort`):** Closing the residual named in the TTS-worker/ask-cancel entry below ("abandoned, not killed"). `local_llm.chat_local` accepts the turn's cancel hook; when supplied, the request runs over an `http.client` socket the module owns and a 10 ms watcher `shutdown`s it (`shutdown`, not `close`), raising `LocalLlmCancelled` at once instead of waiting out the ~30 s timeout. With no hook the exact `urllib` path is kept, byte-identical (all existing urlopen-patching tests still pass). `frontier.AbortableHTTPTransport` is now the gateway default transport: same headers/status mapping as `urllib_transport`, but it keeps the live socket and `abort()` shuts it down mid-response; `FrontierClient` watches the same hook and returns typed `CANCELLED` (pre-send/between-retry checks preserved, injected fake transports unchanged). `gateway._local_complete`/`_local_dispatch`/`run`/`_fallback` thread the cancel through to the adapter. Service wiring was already there (`ask` passes `_stopped`); no service/TTS/helper/renderer file changed. **Measured** on a loopback fake slow server: local and frontier both unblocked in **~0.010 s** (watcher poll cadence) vs the 30 s timeout. **Verified:** new `tests/test_voice_abort_inflight9u.py` 8 passed; required command `ask_abort + ask_cancel + fullturn + voice_edit*` → **683 passed**; provider/8Q/local set → 79 passed, 1 failed (`test_session_model_is_pinned...`, byte-identical on base `a5ab47dff`). No service restart, no global E2E/release verdict. **Remaining:** `chat_local_stream` still checks `should_stop` only between lines (not the `/ask` brain path); `_local_generate`/`_local_chat_raw` remain urllib; a transport that does not expose `abort` (injected fakes) keeps the older cooperative checks.

**Orb open items closed — TTS worker, `/ask` cancel, live E2E (2026-10-19, 3 agents):** Follow-up to the fluid/refinement pass's three residuals. **(1) TTS synth worker.** Kept the killable child (a STOP still SIGTERM→SIGKILLs it) and added **eager pre-warm** (a cancelled worker is replaced in a background thread, and a reply arriving mid-load joins it) plus **idle reaping** (`QUBIT_TTS_SYNTH_WORKER_IDLE_S`, default 300 s; `0` disables). Measured: the next reply ~1.1 s after a stop drops **0.936 s → 0.051 s** (warm); the idle extra model copy is released (**109 MB → 0**, process gone). The ACTIVE worker still loads its own provider copy — eliminating that needs fork/COW from a multi-threaded service with ONNX/sherpa, not safely provable, so it was not claimed. **(2) `/ask` cancel is prompt even when the model ignores hooks.** A keyed turn now runs on a tracked daemon worker; the HTTP handler watches the cancel event (10 ms poll) and returns typed `VOICE_EDIT_ASK_CANCELLED` as soon as `cancel(rid)` fires, instead of waiting for the call to unwind. Cooperative abort (`cancel=`/`should_stop=`) is unchanged; a late reply is discarded (no speech, no `_remember_ask_exchange`) and a tombstone keeps `/api/audio/speak` from shipping a stopped wav. Residual, precisely: the underlying work is **abandoned, not killed** — `local_llm.chat_local` is a blocking `urlopen().read()` with no hook (up to its 30 s timeout), and `FrontierClient.complete` checks cancel only before send/between retries; the abandoned daemon worker is tracked, bounded and self-cleaning. **(3) Live E2E against the RUNNING helper.** `scripts/verify_orb_stop_live.py` gains `--live-helper` (read-only) and `--live-signal` (one SIGUSR2, gated): it resolves the live helper from the heartbeat, proves the SIGUSR2 handler is installed via `/proc/status` `SigCgt` before signalling, and analyses the journal tail for an open `playback.started`. Live run on this host: helper detected, handler installed, `--live-signal` survived — **DEGRADED honestly** because the helper was idle (nothing playing); the speaking→PASS branch is hermetic-only here. Read-only by default, never restarts a service, never sends synthetic input. **Verified:** tts-worker + ask-cancel/abort + stop-live + stop-speaking + speakable + fullturn → **121 passed**; orb non-Qt set → 122 (2 xfailed, 2 xpassed); `qt9u` 24. **NOT applied live — no service restart** (owner: other agents are using the dashboard). **Still open:** the active TTS worker's extra provider copy; the abandoned (not killed) in-flight model call; the live speaking→PASS path needs a real reply in flight. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb fluid/complex refinement — field + Qt + Tk + stop residuals (2026-10-19, 6 agents, 2 waves):** Owner: "refine orb complex animations more make more fluid, more professional, more detailed, more complex". **(wave 1)** *Field* (`mark_field.py`): two-frequency non-harmonic sines on `camera_offset`/`_phase_wobble`/`glint`/`ring_intensity`/`ring_shimmer`, a counter-travelling second filament wave, bounded per-body `micro_offset`, a new `haze_state` parallax bed, drifting `z`/`z2` (`_volume_axes`), a 6-shell core with per-shell `swirl`, and additive keys (`wobble`/`flicker`/`crest`/`drift`/`glow`/`clump`/`lobe`/`sparkle`); `shockwave` gained an eased attack. *Qt* (`qt_app.py`): the nucleus is now a lit sphere, one atmospheric bloom path under the whole mark, depth-of-field via the field's `dof`, dot-tail motion detail — primitive-neutral, cap held. *Tk* (`mark.py`): richer nucleus (aura bed, tumble-swept specular, terminator shade, 12-stop body), asymmetric duotone glints, finer ring/filament splines. **(wave 2)** Both renderers now CONSUME the field's new detail (otherwise invisible): `haze_state` drawn as a faint parallax bed behind everything; `micro_offset`/`wobble`/`flicker`/`swirl`/`crest`/`drift`/`glow`/`clump`/`lobe`/`sparkle` wired where they improve the look. **Stop residuals closed:** `POST /api/audio/speak` now runs a keyed orb reply's synthesis in a **killable child process** (`voicebridge/tts/synth_worker.py`; parent polls the turn's cancel state every 20 ms and SIGTERM→SIGKILLs the worker's group), so a STOP during synthesis aborts it (measured: a long synth cancelled 50 ms in → `SynthCancelled` at 0.092 s; warm synth 0.079 s); unkeyed dashboard speech keeps the warm in-process path; `QUBIT_TTS_SYNTH_WORKER=0` opts out. Also fixed a STOP race that dropped the `interrupt` event when a blocked `_wait_playback` returned normally. New read-only live harness `scripts/verify_orb_stop_live.py` (no mic, no synthetic input) — live run **PASS**: player ended and the canonical `interrupt`/`playback.stopped`/`response.done` triple journaled. **Verified:** non-Qt orb + field + stop/tts-worker set → **203 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; Qt files individually `qt9u` 24, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 43, `qt_turn9u` 24, `fx_perf9u` 1; rendered and looked at `/tmp/orbint/`. **NOT applied live — no service restart** (owner: other agents are using the web dashboard). **Still open:** the `/ask` POST is still one blocking request (helper aborts its own socket; no server-push); the killable worker holds one extra model copy per service and reloads after a stop (~1.7 s one-off); `rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` remain published for API compat but unused by the renderers. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Qt orb studio-lighting pass (2026-10-19, branch `feat/orb-qt2`):** Owner: "refine orb complex animations more make more fluid, more professional, more detailed, more complex". Rendering-only changes in `voicebridge/orb/qt_app.py` (sibling lanes own `mark_field.py`/`mark.py`/`app.py`). **(1) Nucleus is now a lit sphere.** The body gradient uses a FOCAL point at the tumble-carried specular, so the hot core sits off-centre with the surface falling away to a shadow side (6 stops, smooth terminator); a crisp key specular, a bounce light on the shadow side, and an ALWAYS-ON inner caustic (the old one existed only during the beat) replace the retired static specular disc (net −1 primitive). **(2) Atmospheric bloom.** One wide, low-alpha, state-tinted smoke path under the whole mark (`_smoke(cx,cy,1.18R,…,0.09,power=2.35)`), so the vector shapes read as floating in light, hue taken from the field's own `PALETTE_HUES`. **(3) Depth-of-field.** `_focus()` prefers the field's per-body `dof` (falls back to the depth convention) and feeds `_shade`; the far half is honestly dimmer. **(4) Motion detail.** `_layer_batch` gained `streak_s`: accretion/infall bodies get two smaller trailing discs appended INTO the same bucket path — a chain of dots, never a ribbon, at zero extra primitives. **(5) Budget.** Bloom/stops are primitive-neutral; `VOLUME_DRAW_LIMIT` 18→16 and `FILAMENT_DRAW_COUNT` 10→8 free room, so the perf window max is 416 and 0..8 s peaks at 420 (cap 420). **Verified:** Qt files individually `qt9u` 24, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 40 (2 new pins: the bloom path, and no-regression), `qt_turn9u` 24, `fx_perf9u` 1; plus `fx_contract9u` 10 (2 xfailed, 2 xpassed), `fluid9u` 8, `capture9u` 5, `equalizer9u` 20, `colors_fluid9u` 8, `activity9u` 15, `app9u` 9. **A first cut grew far dots (bokeh) and broke `test_voice_orb_fx_contract9u::test_depth_beads_scale_and_contrast_together[qt]`** (grew far beads past near ones); the radius growth was removed — DOF stays alpha-only — and that contract is green again. Rendered and LOOKED at 15 frames + zooms in `/tmp/orbqt2final/`: the core now reads as a glossy lit sphere with a key highlight and a cooler bounce, the field sits in a soft haze. **Open:** the host was at load ~29 during this session, so `fx_perf9u`'s 45 ms mean cap measured ~50–65 ms on BOTH the branch and a stashed baseline (environmental, not a regression; primitives pass). No live capture, no service restart, no global E2E/release verdict.

**Two more circles at 1.6× opacity + varied particle directions (2026-10-19):** Owner: "add 2 more circles again and make add 60% more opacity than from the others so they be a little more visible" and "add more particle and different motions for it not all in same direction". **(1) A second orbit PAIR.** `_ring_specs` gains `accent_a`/`accent_b` (layer `accent`, `rx_frac` 0.58, far/near tilts) with `RING_ALPHA_ACCENT = RING_ALPHA_MAIN * 1.6` (exactly 60% more); Qt draws them at `RING_DRAW_ALPHA["accent"] = 1.6 × near_main` and Tk follows the field alpha. The declared set is now 10 circles. **(2) Different motions.** The layers that moved as one now take a hash-seeded ±1 sign — `particle_state`, `dust_state`, `sparkles` and `binary_state` (plus their `vx`/`vy`) mix clockwise and counter-clockwise motion instead of all orbiting the same way. **(3) More particles.** `DUST_COUNT` 80→110, `SPARKLE_COUNT` 80→110, `BLINK_COUNT` 40→52, `BINARY_COUNT` 128→168. **Verified:** `mark9u` 69, `fx_contract9u`+`tk_turn9u`+`colors_fluid9u`+`tk_capture9u` → 125 (2 xfailed, 2 xpassed), Qt files individually `qt9u` 24, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 39, `qt_turn9u` 24, `fx_perf9u` 1 (primitive budget still under cap); new pins for the 1.6× accent pair and both-direction motion. Rendered and looked at `/tmp/orbrings/`: the two extra ellipses read a little stronger than the rest, field denser. Applied live: `qubit-voice-orb.service` restarted. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Nucleus rim chroma split retired (2026-10-19):** The last galaxy-colour leftover from the look fix. The ring red/blue fringe was removed earlier, but the NUCLEUS rim still carried its own red/blue chromatic split (`NUCLEUS_CHROMA_ALPHA` in Qt, `NUCLEUS_RIM_FRINGE_ALPHA`/`_FRINGE_RED`/`_FRINGE_BLUE` in Tk) — the same multi-hue artifact the owner called "too colored … like galaxies". Retired in both renderers, leaving a single neutral Fresnel limb; Qt's dead `fringe` offset and the Tk `NUCLEUS_RIM_OVALS` 3→1 fall out, and `mark.__all__` drops the removed names. The two Qt tests that pinned the split now assert **no** red/blue chromatic stroke anywhere. Verified: `qt_turn9u` 24, `qt9u` 24, `qt_fx9u` 39, `qt_mark9u` 4, `tk_turn9u`+`mark9u`+`fx_contract9u` 111 (2 xfailed, 2 xpassed), `tk_capture9u` 6; `mark.__all__` has no missing names. Applied live: `qubit-voice-orb.service` restarted. **Still open:** piper/sherpa `synthesize` is a blocking C call (a stop inside it lets that one synth finish server-side, wav discarded); no live audio end-to-end press capture; `rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` remain published for API compat but unused by the renderers. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Tk render capture now headless (2026-10-19):** Closing the last open item from the look fix. The Tk render-capture harness (`tests/test_voice_orb_tk_capture9u.py`) was display-gated, so the Tk painter's real-pixel PNGs were never produced on a headless host. It now starts a private `Xvfb` when no DISPLAY/WAYLAND_DISPLAY exists (no-op if one does, or if Xvfb is absent), crops the postscript to the canvas (`-dEPSCrop`), and composites the alpha raster onto the orb background with PIL — so a headless run writes 9/9 faithful dark-background frames instead of skipping. Verified: `env -u DISPLAY … pytest tests/test_voice_orb_tk_capture9u.py` → 6 passed, `real_pixels=yes`, 9/9 PNGs (e.g. `output/orb_capture/tk/idle_t4p7.png`). No product code touched. **Note:** a concurrent session `git reset` the `main` working tree mid-session and wiped an uncommitted copy of this edit; it was re-applied in an isolated worktree (`feat/orb-tkpng`) and merged. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb look fix — L/V gone, translucent rings, denser field (2026-10-19, 2 lanes):** Owner, looking at the live orb: "you didnt replace the L and V's!!!!! and the black holes need to be more transparent and they are too colored they look like galaxies, and add more particles and random 1 0 that spawn everywere small ones it looks like shit". **(1) The L/V marks were TWO things, and the earlier lane fixed the wrong one.** The thick bent strokes were the `_streak` wake ribbons (`TRAIL_DRAW_SAMPLES=3` → a 3-point angular ribbon; a t=4.7 primitive dump showed 53 gradient paths, 7–13 elements, up to 72×72 px). But the *literal* L/V hooks were a BUG in `_glint`: its second arm ended at `gy + ax` on BOTH endpoints, so the "cross" was a horizontal stroke joined to the first arm — an L or a V. Fixed to a real perpendicular cross (`(gx−ay, gy+ax) → (gx+ay, gy−ax)`); the `_streak` wake painter and all four call sites are deleted in both renderers (`rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` stay field-only for API compat). **(2) Rings no longer a galaxy.** Removed the chromatic red/blue fringe (`QColor(255,45,85)`/`(45,130,255)`) and the multi-hue spray; ring alphas cut hard (`RING_ALPHA_MAIN` 0.34→0.18, echo 0.20→0.12, satellite 0.14→0.06), Qt uses absolute translucent targets, and Tk adds `RING_TRANSLUCENCY`. **(3) Denser, smaller field.** `DUST_COUNT` 64→80, `SPARKLE_COUNT` 52→80, `BLINK_COUNT` 32→40, `BINARY_COUNT` 88→128, all smaller; binaries spread across the whole volume (`BINARY_R_FRAC` 0.80/0.25→0.14/0.95) so small `1`/`0` fragments spawn everywhere. **Verified:** non-Qt orb set + ask-abort/cancel → **164 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; Qt files individually `qt9u` 24, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 39, `qt_turn9u` 24, `fx_perf9u` 1. **Rendered and LOOKED** at 12 offscreen frames (`/tmp/orbfinal/`): no thick bent L/V strokes, rings faint translucent single-hue glass, dense small pinpricks + tiny `1`/`0` across the volume. **Applied live:** the in-flight-abort lane restarted `qubit-voicebridge.service` (bridge pid 3295239, :8890) + `qubit-voice-typing.service` (helper pid 3295807); `kill -USR2` → survived; a live orb frame captured via `scripts/capture_orb_live.py` confirms the new look on the running orb. **Open:** the Tk painter is not live-captured to PNG without a display; `rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` remain published but unused. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb STOP now interrupts the in-flight `/ask` POST and its TTS synth (2026-10-19, branch `feat/orb-ask2`):** Closing the two functional gaps the cancellable-turn lane left open. **(1) The blocking `/ask` POST returns PROMPTLY on a stop, not after the 90 s timeout.** `HelperClient.ask_result`/`speak` now open the request on a socket the client OWNS (`http.client`, not `urllib.urlopen`; a custom `opener` seam is preserved byte-for-byte), and new `HelperClient.abort()` `shutdown()`s that live socket from another thread — `shutdown`, not `close`, is what actually wakes a thread blocked in `recv` on Linux — so the worker unblocks at once and raises the typed `VOICE_EDIT_ASK_CANCELLED`, never a generic failure. `DesktopHelper.stop_speaking` calls the new `_abort_client_request()` in both the playing and THINKING/WORKING branches (non-blocking, `RLock`-guarded, safe from the SIGUSR2 handler); a client without `abort` (older/fake) is a quiet no-op and an idle stop with nothing in flight stays a no-op. `_speak` now emits the canonical stop triple when a synth is aborted (`VOICE_EDIT_ASK_CANCELLED`/`AUDIO_SPEAK_CANCELLED`), so the orb FSM closes instead of waiting; a generic TTS error still does not fake a stop. **(2) The `/api/audio/speak` synth is keyed to the turn and gated.** `speak` carries the turn id learned from `ask_result` (no signature change — fakes keep `speak(text)`); `voice_edit.service.ask_turn_stopped(cfg, rid)` reads the SAME service the helper's `/<rid>/cancel` reached (`routes.service_for`) and `qubit_compat.h_audio_speak` refuses BEFORE synthesis (earliest cancellation point) and again AFTER (never ships a stopped wav). **Residual, precisely:** piper/sherpa `synthesize` is a blocking C call not interruptible mid-flight, so a stop that lands inside it lets that one synth complete server-side; the result is discarded and never returned/played, and the helper's socket abort means it does not wait. **Not touched:** the `/ask` POST has no server-push, so the *service* still runs its (now cancel-aware) turn to completion to notice the cancel; a player ignoring SIGTERM would linger (pre-existing). **Verified:** new `tests/test_voice_edit_ask_abort9u.py` 24 passed; full `test_voice_edit*` set **651 passed**; helper/orb-adjacent set (`stt_onset/preroll`, `timer_reminder`, `confirm_session`, `open_dictate`, `orb_equalizer/level`, `session_create_wave5`, `speak_brand`, `spec_publish`, `talkback_lane`, `talk_events_wire`, `wake_continuous/routing/stall`, `wake_no_key_synthesis`, `wake_turn_watchdog`) **205 passed**; orb set **126 passed**; the named lane command (ask_cancel+stop_speaking+talk_capture+orb_capture_ipc+ask_abort) **74 passed**. Pre-existing, byte-identical on base (verified via stash): `test_webui_ws_gate8u` 3F + `test_qubit_routes_parity8u` 1F, and `test_voice_e2e_hermetic9u`/`test_chat_voice_e2e9u` 10F (environmental: Ollama runtime context too small / no Qubit auth). No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb open items closed — cancellable /ask turn, Tk + live capture (2026-10-19, 3 agents):** Follow-up to the last round's three remaining gaps. **(1) A STOP now cancels the in-flight `/ask` turn, not just the helper's own reply.** The service (`voicebridge/voice_edit/service.py`) registers the turn by `request_id`; a cancel sets a per-request event that `ask`/`_full_turn_answer` honor — a cancel before the brain call prevents it, a cancel during it is wired to the gateway's real abort hooks (`cancel=` on the frontier path, `should_stop=` on the local streaming adapter, which aborts mid-generation), and no `_full_turn_answer` fallback runs once stopped. The helper's `stop_speaking()` (SIGUSR2) now also best-effort POSTs `/<request_id>/cancel` on a daemon thread (once per turn, never fatal from the signal handler); a typed cancelled response closes the orb FSM with the canonical `interrupt`/`playback.stopped`/`response.done` triple, not an error. **Still not cancellable, precisely:** the `/ask` HTTP POST itself is one blocking request with no server-push, so the socket is not interrupted (the work inside is aborted and no reply is returned; the POST returns 400 promptly); a `/api/audio/speak` synthesis already in flight for a reply is a separate request not keyed to the ask `request_id` (the helper discards the resulting wav via `_turn_reply_stopped()`, so it never plays, but the synth call is not server-aborted); a player that ignores SIGTERM would linger (pre-existing). **(2) Deterministic Tk render-capture harness.** New `tests/test_voice_orb_tk_capture9u.py` renders idle/listening/speaking at fixed clocks through the Tk painter, writes a primitive dump + sha256 per frame to `output/orb_capture/tk/` (gitignored) and a real-pixel PNG when a Tk display + `gs` exist, asserts each owner-2026-10-19 layer is actually drawn (stub-the-seam → primitive stream must change), that no retired math glyph reaches `create_text` (only binary `0`/`1`), and byte-determinism. The interrupted agent's lane shipped with a test bug — `_render(t, state)` was called as `_render(state, t)`, so `t="idle"` coerced to 0.0 and every re-render returned the t=0 still frame (it looked like a product freeze; it is not — plain-python and explicit-arg renders are pure and distinct per t). Argument order fixed; 6/6 pass. **(3) Live capture harness.** New `scripts/capture_orb_live.py` (read-only) grabs a frame time-lapse of the live orb window + a `voice_edit.talk.*` journal timeline into `output/orb_capture/live/<ts>/`, with a hermetic test pinning the parse/timeline logic; synthetic input is fail-closed (needs `--simulate-press` AND `QUBIT_ORB_LIVE_ALLOW_SYNTHETIC_INPUT=1`). **Verified:** `ask_cancel9u`+`tk_capture9u`+`live_capture9u`+`stop_speaking9u` → 56; broader orb/helper set → **222 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; the cancel agent's wider runs → 657 passed (12 + 6 failures byte-identical on base). **Applied live with owner approval:** `qubit-voicebridge.service` restarted (bridge pid 2824980, dashboard back on :8890) and `qubit-voice-typing.service` restarted (helper pid 2825384); `kill -USR2` → survived. **Open:** the `/ask` POST socket and an already-in-flight `/api/audio/speak` synth are not interrupted (above); no live audio end-to-end press capture; the live X11 frames are not visually asserted (look stays owner-verified). No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb open items closed — pre-TTS stop, fx-perf cap, render capture (2026-10-19, 2 agents):** Follow-up to the stop fix's three remaining gaps. **(1) A STOP during THINKING/WORKING now suppresses that turn's reply.** `stop_speaking()` was a silent no-op unless `_speaking`/a player subprocess existed, so a stop before TTS started did nothing and the reply was spoken anyway. Now the helper tracks a per-turn epoch: `_process_transcript` bumps `_turn_seq` (and resets `_turn_stopped_seq`/`_turn_stop_announced`) at the top of every turn; `stop_speaking()` marks `_turn_stopped_seq = _turn_seq` when the state is `PROCESSING` (and keeps the old terminate-playback behavior when live); `_speak` checks `_turn_reply_stopped()` before AND after synthesis and aborts, emitting the canonical `interrupt`/`playback.stopped`/`response.done` once per turn. Because a new turn bumps the epoch, a stale stop can never silence a later reply (idle stop is scoped out). **Not done:** aborting the in-flight model call — `ask_result` is one blocking HTTP POST with no cancellation token, so the reply is suppressed only, not the call; the service-streamed TTS path (`{"spoken": True}`) bypasses helper `_speak` and is not covered. **(2) `fx_perf9u` cap corrected.** The 25 ms mean cap predated the FX stack; measured with `time.process_time` the full mark is CPU-bound at **33.6 ms/frame** (wall 34.7 ms — so not host load), primitives 352–395 (cap 420), and the orb runs it at ~25 fps / 40 ms by design. Cap raised to 45 ms (~30% headroom); the primitive and spread bounds are unchanged. **(3) Deterministic render-capture harness.** New `tests/test_voice_orb_capture9u.py` renders idle/listening/speaking frames offscreen at fixed clocks, writes PNGs + a digest to `output/orb_capture/` (gitignored), asserts each new layer is actually painted (volume 37–47, accretion 77–101, photon 5–26, infall 40–71, screen-break 64–74 changed px/frame), that screen-break bodies reach past R (1.300·R, pixels beyond R), that no retired math glyph is drawn, and that the render is byte-deterministic (same-clock sha256); an optional `scripts/capture_orb_x11.sh` grabs the live X11 window. **Verified:** `stop_speaking9u`+`capture9u`+`orb_capture_ipc9u` → 37; broader orb set → **190 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; `fx_perf9u` → 1 passed. **Applied live:** `qubit-voice-typing.service` restarted (helper pid 2723934) and `kill -USR2` → survived. **Open:** no live X11/audio end-to-end press capture; Tk painter is not PNG-captured (needs a display). No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb stop fix — helper-side SIGUSR2 + Tk parity, applied live (2026-10-19, 2 agents):** Follow-up to the orb refinement's two open items. **(a) The owner's "stops too slow" was really the HELPER.** `_speak` (`voicebridge/desktop_helper.py:2901`) blocks in `_play_with_barge_in` on a `paplay`/`aplay` subprocess and had no stop path; the orb's cancel only reached `VoiceEditService.cancel`, which popped a staged voice-edit request and never touched playback. Now `_speak` keeps `self._playback_proc`, new `DesktopHelper.stop_speaking()` terminates it (works with barge-in enabled or disabled) and the owning `_speak` emits the canonical `interrupt`/`playback.stopped`/`response.done`; a SIGUSR2 handler (`on_orb_stop`, installed/restored alongside SIGUSR1) drives it; `OrbClient.stop_speaking()` sends SIGUSR2 to the same cmdline-validated heartbeat pid as `trigger_helper_capture`; and `VoiceEditService.cancel` (signature unchanged) also best-effort signals the helper, so a cancel is never a no-op for audio. **(b) Tk parity.** `voicebridge/orb/app.py` now mirrors the Qt state-machine fix — learns the live turn id from the journal feed via `state.active_request` and passes `orb_state`/`active_rid`/`active_ts` to `plan_toggle` (cancel while thinking/working/speaking, finish the capture while listening) — and BOTH renderers halt playback via `cli.stop_speaking()` on the stop press, falling back to `cancel_request(rid)` when it is absent OR raises. The raise-fallback matters: on the stage-only fallback (helper down) `stop_speaking` raises `ORB_HELPER_NOT_RUNNING`, and without the fallback the staged request stayed stuck (caught in review, fixed + regression-pinned in both renderers). **Verified:** helper `test_voice_orb_stop_speaking9u` + `test_orb_capture_ipc9u` → 25; Tk/poll/talk (`app9u`, `poll_thread9u`, `poll_cadence9u`, `talk_state9u`, `talk_capture9u`) → 50; `qt9u` → 24; broader hermetic helper set → 685. **Applied live with owner approval** — the running helper (pid 664852, a stray VSCode-scope process) predated the SIGUSR2 handler, so a stop press would have TERMINATED it (SIGUSR2 default disposition): stopped the stray gracefully, started `qubit-voice-typing.service` (new helper pid 2682996), restarted `qubit-voice-orb.service` (pid 2683838, Qt). **Live proof:** `kill -USR2 2682996` → the helper SURVIVED (handler installed) and the heartbeat stayed live. **Open:** the stop is best-effort (a player that ignored SIGTERM would linger; `paplay`/`aplay` do not); a stop during synthesis before `playback.started` is a no-op on the audio path (the orb only reaches `speaking` after playback starts); no live audio/X11 end-to-end capture. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb refinement — six owner asks across three lanes + graft MCP unblock (2026-10-19, orchestrator):** Owner brief: "deploy 3 agents and refine voice orb animations" with six asks, plus "fix graft if its an issue". Shipped as three isolated worktree lanes merged `field → Qt → Tk` (plus a graft-lane config fix), all on `main`. **1. check marks/v-lines gone.** The field's math-symbol TEXT layer (√ Δ ∇ ≠ — the "check marks / v lines") is retired (`math_state` → `[]`); it is replaced by `volume_particles`, a real 3D particle field (52 bodies on inclined planes, per-body `pulse`/`spin`, depth/z/z2, no `char`). Qt draws them as round dots, Tk as spin-raked glints. **2. black-hole detail.** New `accretion_stream` (28), `photon_ring` (44, lensed over the nucleus), `infall_streaks` (14) + per-ring `intensity`/`shimmer`/`streamers`; both renderers now draw live accretion structure instead of dead outlines. **3. circles refined.** The shared rigid ring motion is no longer one bare sine but a sum of three weighted sines on a non-harmonic period family `(23.3, 8.5, 31.7)` — still ONE shared offset, so no ring drifts out of proportion (the rigidity contract was strengthened, not weakened). **4. palette.** `PALETTE_HUES=(0.54,0.60)`; the last local rainbow walk (nucleus corona) is gone — Qt/Tk blend the field's two hues with the matte-band sats. **6. more 3D / screen-break.** `DEPTH_POPOUT` 0.70→0.95, `DEPTH_PERSPECTIVE` 0.20→0.30, `DEPTH_PARALLAX` 0.20→0.30, `SKEW_GAIN_DEG` 7→9; `SCREEN_BREAK_FRAC` 1.18→1.34 `R`, `COUNT` 8→10, unclipped past the widget edge. **5. STOP LATENCY — root cause CONFIRMED and fixed.** `_pending_rid` was set ONLY on the `ORB_HELPER_NOT_RUNNING` stage-only fallback, so on the live helper path every press re-planned `("begin", None)` and re-triggered capture — the reply kept playing. Fixed: `state.active_request()` learns the live request id from the journal capture rows the orb already polls (`stt.cancelled` clears it), and `plan_toggle(..., orb_state=, active_rid=, active_ts=)` cancels the live turn while `thinking/working/speaking`, still finishes the capture while `listening`, and keeps the 3-arg call byte-compatible. Qt learns `_active_rid` from the 100 ms journal tail (not the 500 ms poll) and cancels it on the second press. **Graft.** Root cause of the recurring red `MCP error -32001: Request timed out` reproduced: every graft MCP query runs `ensureFreshGraph`, which on a drifted tree waits ≤2 s then SYNCHRONOUSLY rebuilds the whole graph before answering; this repo is ~12 GB and graft indexes the 2 GB vendored `qubit_runtime/` (18,017 tracked files), so `graft/` is ~985 MB (wiring ~263 MB, ask-index ~191 MB, ~14,811 cards) and a query on a dirty tree ran >120 s. Fixed by setting `GRAFT_NO_REFRESH=1` for the graft MCP server in all four client configs (`opencode.json`/`.mcp.json`/`.vscode/mcp.json`/`.gemini/settings.json`); run `graft build` to refresh on demand. MCP env changes take effect on the next opencode/client restart. **Verified (merged `main`):** non-Qt group `mark9u, fx_contract9u, tk_turn9u, colors_fluid9u, ember9u, fluid9u, poll_thread9u, poll_cadence9u` → **159 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; Qt files individually `qt9u` 22, `qt_mark9u` 4, `qt_fx9u` 38, `qt_turn9u` 33 → **97 passed** (the combined Qt run segfaults on teardown — pre-existing, offscreen). Live orb (`qubit-voice-orb.service`, Qt backend per `orb.qt_stats`) restarted on the merged tree, active, no errors. **Open / not fixed:** (a) the Tk/`app.py` press-to-talk path still has the `_pending_rid` stop-latency bug (task 5 fixed in the live Qt path only); (b) `state.cancel_request` pops the service's staged request — whether it aborts an already-playing **helper** TTS is a helper/service concern; the orb-side requirement (cancel, not re-capture) is met and pinned; (c) `fx_perf9u` fails only its 25 ms mean-cost timer under host load (base measures the same); (d) no live visual capture harness exists for the X11 overlay, so the look is owner-verified, not machine-verified. No global E2E/release verdict.

**Model pick did not apply in the chat sidebar — missing reload wiring (2026-10-18):** Owner: "i try change model to opencode go and its not working in model provider side panel" — no error, the pick simply did not take, and the panel kept showing the previous provider (cheapvibecode.ru). Measured all four backend layers before changing anything: `active_model_profile` = `opencode-go-deepseek-v4.1-flash` (gate OK), `resolve_model()` = provider `opencode-go` / model `deepseek-v4.1-flash` / `source: "active"`, runtime `config.yaml` model slot = `opencode-go` / `deepseek-v4.1-flash`, and every recent row of the live `runtime/state.db` (1,455 sessions) = `deepseek-v4.1-flash`. **The switch was never failing.** Root cause: `ChatSidebar.tsx` never imported or rendered `ModelReloadConfirm` — upstream wires it at `web/src/components/ChatSidebar.tsx:33,514` and Qubit's component copy is byte-identical, but the only Qubit reference was a `vi.mock` in the test file. So the write landed in `config.yaml` while the RUNNING chat kept its old model and the panel kept rendering the previous provider (a deliberate Qubit divergence — the old comment argued "No reload" because the `model.changed` SSE frame alone would repaint; it repainted the card but not the running chat). Fixed by porting the reference wiring: import the dialog, add `pendingReloadModel`, set it after a successful main-slot apply, render the confirm. The `terminal_follows === false` path keeps its honest notice and does **not** prompt (the terminal refused the pick, so a reload prompt would promise a switch that did not happen). Also corrected a stale assertion: the session-bound path sends `provider` alongside `model` (a bare id re-enters first-match routing server-side), so the test had been failing against correct code. **Verified:** `ChatSidebar.test.tsx` 18 passed (2 new pin the prompt and its suppression), `src/components/` 114 passed, `tsc -b` clean, `npm run build` succeeded, and the SERVED chunks confirm the fix reached the browser — `/assets/ChatSidebar-BvtsL_wq.js` (8,165 bytes) carries the `ModelReloadConfirm` reference and `/assets/ModelReloadConfirm-CTF35xwF.js` carries the "starts a fresh chat" copy. Committed `9fc177577` (only the two files; the `GRAFT_NO_REFRESH` MCP config edits in the tree are another session's and were left unstaged). **Still open, not fixed:** (1) opencode-go's models.dev row resolves to `https://opencode.ai/inference/go/openai/v1` → HTTP **404**, while the vendored plugin profile and `qubit_cli/auth.py:234` both say `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1` → HTTP **200** — latent, because the config slot has the correct URL hand-written, but any *fresh* opencode-go pick resolves the dead host; (2) opencode-go rejects a plain chat call with `400 MissingSessionID` and needs the `x-opencode-session` header, which upstream supplies via `agent/opencode_affinity.py` — not confirmed on the bridge's own request path. **Not verified:** the panel's literal DOM rendering (the Playwright MCP server still fails on `PWTEST_SOCKETS_DIR` socket-path length; the config now sets `/tmp/pw` but the running server predates it and needs a reconnect). No global E2E/release verdict.
**Orb Qt lane — volume particles, accretion structure, instant stop (2026-10-19, branch `feat/orb-refine-qt`):** Owner brief had six Qt-renderer asks; all landed in `voicebridge/orb/qt_app.py` + `voicebridge/orb/state.py` only (`mark_field.py`/`mark.py` untouched — field/Tk lanes own those). **1–2. Math glyphs gone, real particles in.** The `math_state` TEXT path (`_math`/`MATH_*`) is deleted; `mark_volume_particles` draws the field's `volume_particles` as round **dots** (18 strided of 52, never text/arms → no more "check marks / v lines"), depth-split far/near, sized+alpha'd by the field's own per-body pulse. New `mark_accretion_stream` / `mark_photon_ring` / `mark_infall_streaks` draw the "black-hole" structure: accretion + infall as hue-bucketed batched dots, the photon ring dot-by-dot **over the nucleus** (lensed overlay) with a painter size×2.4/alpha×1.5 boost so its lobes read instead of vanishing into the aura. **3. Circles refine.** `_ring_pass` now consumes the field's per-ring `ring_intensity` (travelling bright hot-spot → alpha) and `ring_shimmer` (sub-percent radius texture), on top of the shared rigid `ring_tilt_spin`/`ring_radius_scale`; echo followers keep their fixed faint band. **4. Palette.** Removed the last local spectrum walk (nucleus corona was `HUE_BASE + HUE_TURNS + HUE_SPAN` over 0.34 of the wheel) — it now blends between the field's two `PALETTE_HUES`; local sats lowered to the field's matte band. **6. More 3D / screen-break.** `VOLUME_X/Y_FRAC` 0.13→0.17; screen-break bodies pushed out by `SCREEN_BREAK_PUSH=1.22` so perspective can't pull them back inside `R`. **5. STOP LATENCY (root cause CONFIRMED):** `_pending_rid` is set ONLY on the `ORB_HELPER_NOT_RUNNING` stage-only fallback, so on the live helper path every press re-planned `("begin", None)` and re-triggered capture — the reply kept playing. Fixed by learning the **live request id from the state feed** (`state.active_request()` scans the journal capture rows the orb already polls; `stt.cancelled` clears it) and extending `plan_toggle(..., orb_state=, active_rid=, active_ts=)`: while the orb is `thinking/working/speaking`, a press now `cancel_request(rid)`s the live turn; `listening` still finishes the capture; the 3-arg call is unchanged. State updates already ride the 100 ms journal tail (not the 500 ms poll). **Tests:** focused command 126 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed; new tests pin `active_request`, the cancel-vs-begin plan, and `toggle_voice` (bare attr-only stub, no display) never re-capturing during a live turn. `fx_perf9u` passes on a quiet host (fails the 25 ms timer only under load ~12+, same as base ~33 ms). **Open/parity gaps:** (a) `state.cancel_request` hits the service's `/cancel` (pops the staged request); whether it aborts an already-playing **helper** TTS is a helper/service concern outside this lane — the orb-side requirement (cancel, not re-capture) is met and pinned. (b) Tk (`app.py`/`mark.py`) has the same `_pending_rid` stop-latency bug and draws none of the new layers — not touched (not in scope). (c) `tests/test_voice_orb_mark9u.py::test_ring_set_moves_as_one_rigid_body` fails on the branch base (field lane's 3-period rigid motion vs its stale single-period assertion) — pre-existing, not this lane.
**Orb Tk fallback mirrors the field's black-hole layer set (2026-10-19, Tk lane):** Owner brief: mirror the live Qt lane in the Tk fallback. `voicebridge/orb/mark.py` retired the math-glyph TEXT layer entirely (the `_math` renderer, the `_field_math_state` seam, the `MATH_*` constants and their `__all__` entries) and now draws the field's `volume_particles` (each body a pinprick glint whose crossed arms are raked by its own `spin`; `pulse` already rides size/alpha), `accretion_stream` (depth-shaded dots), `photon_ring` (lensed pinpricks) and `infall_streaks` (a radial tail drawn back along the field's own velocity plus a head dot). The ring pass now consumes the field's per-ring `intensity` (alpha) and `shimmer` (radius) on top of the shared rigid `tilt_spin_deg`/`ring_radius_scale`, so circles read as accretion rather than dead outlines. The nucleus corona's local `base + span*i` hue ramp was replaced by the field's `palette_hue`. No `state.py`/`app.py`/`qt_app.py`/`mark_field.py` edits. Measured Tk primitive total at R=56 rises 733→989 (volume 52×3 glints + accretion 28 dots + photon 44 dots + infall 14×2); reduced motion is unchanged (68 ovals / 25 lines, binary text only) because every new layer is a life layer dropped under the still clock. **Verified:** focused `tests/test_voice_orb_tk_turn9u.py tests/test_voice_orb_mark9u.py tests/test_voice_orb_equalizer9u.py tests/test_voice_orb_fx_contract9u.py` → **126 passed, 2 xfailed, 2 xpassed**; plus `app9u/9u/colors_fluid/ember/fluid/activity/level/panel` → 129 passed. Two stale Tk tests were corrected to the field's refined contract (the three-term rigid ring-motion family, and per-ring shimmer in the ring radius). No global E2E/release verdict.

**Orb resource optimization — 1:1, 103% → ~76% CPU (2026-10-18):** Owner: "optimize orb without removing any animation keep orb 1:1 as it is now but make consume less resource". Measured first rather than guessed: the field layers cost only **3.5ms/frame**; the other **~32ms is Qt** — a frame makes ~1400 QPainter calls (128 antialiased `drawText` glyphs, 90 `drawPath`, 155 `drawEllipse`, 104 `drawLine`, and ~900 setPen/setBrush/setFont). 160px vs 320px differed by only 8ms, so the cost is per-call overhead, not pixels. **Kept (all byte-identical — frame digest `228c9599af2dca23` unchanged vs base):** `hash01` result cache (per-element constants were re-hashed every frame), `_finite`/`_num` fast paths for already-float values, `turn_fade` memoisation, and a cached glyph `QFont` (128 font constructions/frame). **Reverted after measurement:** a painter state-elision wrapper (cut Qt calls 1390→1147 but gave **zero** speedup and broke 18 recorder tests) and a `_reduced_motion` env cache (stale under env toggles, broke reduced-motion tests). **The real lever was the frame budget.** `FRAME_MS` was 16ms while a frame costs ~33-40ms, so the loop repainted back-to-back and pegged a core. `FRAME_MS` now comes from `ember.frame_budget_ms()` (default 40ms/25fps, `QUBIT_ORB_FRAME_MS` overrides) and `_frame_tick` sizes the NEXT interval as `max(FRAME_MS, last_paint_ms * 1.4)` — an **adaptive** budget, so the orb always leaves idle time and cannot peg a core regardless of host load (paint cost is recorded in a `finally`, since `paintEvent` has several early-exit branches). Measured live at 320×320: **103% → 91% (fixed 40ms) → 71% (fixed 50ms) → 75.7% (adaptive)**; on a quiet host the adaptive interval collapses to the 40ms floor and the win is larger. Full orb suite green except `fx_perf9u`, which fails only its 25ms mean-cost timer (base measures ~41ms, now ~39ms) at host load ~20 — pre-existing and environmental; primitive counts pass. Nothing drawn changed.

**Obsidian skills vendored + cheapvibecode key rotated (2026-10-18, evening):** Two unrelated requests in one session.

**cheapvibecode.ru key rotation.** The old key had died: measured `HTTP 402` on `/v1/models`, while the new key returns `HTTP 200` and a real completion (`"KEY_OK"`, 19 tokens, `deepseek-v4.1-flash`). The non-obvious part is that the system reads this provider under **two different env var names**, and both held the identical old value: `CHEAPVIBECODE_API_KEY` (the `model.key_env: env:...` route, the `.mcp.json` `${...}` expansion, and Horizon's OpenAI-compatible client) and `CVC_API_KEY` (the `providers.cheapvibecode.key_env` registry entry at config.yaml:1153). Rotating only one would have left half the system on a dead key. Both updated; value piped via stdin so it never entered a command line, file mode preserved `0600`, backup at `runtime/.env.bak-rot-20261018-203618`. Verified through the **real** loader (`qubit_cli.env_loader.load_qubit_dotenv` → both fingerprints `7daafdcc7513`) rather than by reading the file. Service restarted 20:42; **0 auth-error lines, 0 tracebacks** since.

**Obsidian skills (kepano/obsidian-skills).** Six Agent Skills from Obsidian's own author, pin `3ccff5338ea7`, MIT (Steph Ango). Cloned to `~/.agents/obsidian-skills` and symlinked into `runtime/skills/note-taking/` — the same source-clone + symlink pattern as `book-to-skill` and `coop-anything`. They **complement** the existing `obsidian` skill rather than duplicating it: ours owns vault *access* (the HTTP route, the token-scrape, the ASCII-path trap), theirs own Obsidian-native *formats*. Verified by resolving every `skill_md_path` through the real `agent.skill_commands.get_skill_commands()` registry: `obsidian-bases` 13,006 chars (1 reference), `obsidian-markdown` 5,367 (3), `json-canvas` 7,624 (1), `knap` 3,942, `defuddle` 829, `obsidian-cli` 3,177 — all non-zero and readable through the symlink. Note `skills inspect` searches registries, not local skills, so it is the wrong command for verifying a local install; `skills show` returned empty, which is a CLI gap, not a missing skill.

**Unrelated pre-existing failures left alone:** 8 session-area test files (`test_completion_admission`, `test_compression_failure_session_sync`, `test_first_turn_session_meta_rebaseline`, `test_heartbeat_session_boundaries`, `test_session_continuity`, `test_session`, `test_host_rendezvous`, `test_compression_concurrent_sessions`) fail **byte-identically on the pre-merge baseline**. Several are a deliberate conftest guard (`TEST BUG: file I/O against the REAL hermes home`) refusing tests that would write to the live profile — that is the suite protecting real data, not a defect. Not fixed here; they are not merge damage.

**926-commit upstream port — regression repair (2026-10-18):** Branch `port/merged-926` (7 port branches merged, 75 commits, 4 conflicts). A per-file run of `tests/gateway` on BOTH trees (1,019 files each, 300s bound, 2h16m) gave the first real comparator: **225 regressions** on the merged tree. All 225 are now fixed (`225 → 0`, 60/60 files clean); the only two remaining failures in the wider 94-file sample — `test_api_server.py` 3F/114P and `test_api_server_media_data_urls.py` 3F/2P — are **byte-identical on the baseline**, i.e. pre-existing, not merge damage. 15 commits. **Every defect had ONE root cause: the facade split moved something into a sibling and left a dangling consumer.** None of them fail at import — `import gateway.run` was green the whole time — so an earlier 2,016-module import sweep reported a false all-clear; only runtime execution catches this class. The worst instances, all silent at runtime: (1) `api_server_openai_routes._implicit_chat_session` — a method that exists nowhere, so **every `/v1/chat/completions` returned HTTP 500** before running anything; (2) `PluginContext.inject_message` had a docstring and **no body** (two unrelated methods were inserted before the implementation, leaving it unreachable after a `return`) — no plugin could inject into a live gateway at all; (3) `_settle` forwarded `superseded=` to `_spawn_agent_run`, which has no such parameter, and the TypeError fired **inside the dispatch task**, so every coalesced webhook group was silently dropped; (4) `_handle_retry_command` called `_retry_readmission_reply`/`_retry_in_admission`, neither of which exists — every `/retry` raised; (5) the display-tier table was deleted in favour of a registry lookup that only **3 of 28** platforms populate, so slack went from `tool_progress: off` to `all`; (6) `wecom/register()` lost its second `register_platform` call, leaving `wecom_callback` in LAZY_DEPS with nothing able to reach it. Also fixed: `_resolve_chained_history` and `scaffolding_replay_fields` (phantom calls), 4 split-module names never imported (`unusable_lock_dir`, `unusable_lock_dir_recovered`, `is_compaction_scaffolding`, `maybe_post_auto_prune_prompt`), `housekeeping_media_caches`, the missing `/autoprune` handler (its absence broke **every** slash command, since `_command_handler_table` resolves all names eagerly), and `PendingEvent.superseded`. Two self-inflicted errors caught and reverted: a `status.py` re-export that made `status_scoped_locks` **circular** (that sibling's own docstring says `gateway.status` never imports it), and a first `decide()` patch that regressed `start`/`replace-host` cases. Host-cluster failures were fixed at the **fixture**, not by weakening code: `record_names_live_owner` now requires the role's host lock, so the stand-ins claim it like a real publishing gateway (`test_host_topology` 2F→5P, `test_host_attach_lifecycle` 8F→14P, `test_host_gateway_profile_env` 1F→9P). **Two genuine gaps left OPEN and marked in code, not invented:** `_retry_in_admission` (the admission-fenced rewrite is unimplemented; `/retry` falls through to the normal, unfenced path — no test covers it) and `session_viewer_close.py:109,155` (calls a nonexistent `forget_persisted_close` *and* reads `close.record`, which `_PendingClose.__slots__ = ('reason',)` does not have). Both need a design decision, not a guess. An AST scan was added for names never defined or imported in `gateway/`+`agent/` (the Call-node-only first version missed bare references like the tuple entry `housekeeping_media_caches`). **No merge to `main`, nothing deployed, no release verdict.**

**Orb palette + math glyphs + rigid rings (2026-10-18):** Owner: "its still too colored", "I like it how it tries to 'break' the screen, do more of that", "the curved lines what are those? add math symbols equations not random lines", plus a ring bug: "shouldn't they all be on same axis, sometime one becomes smaller or bigger than the other". Two lanes (field / renderers). **Palette**: new `PALETTE_HUES=(0.52,0.61,0.70)` + `palette_hue()` — every layer's hue now comes from 2–3 base colours instead of the full wheel (verified: all layers emit exactly those 3 hues). **Root cause of "too colored" found and fixed**: the duotone pair was `HUE_PAIR_OFFSET=0.5` (the OPPOSITE colour), so lerping a streak from cyan→red swept through green and yellow — every streak was a full rainbow. Offset is now `0.07`, so a streak is two NEIGHBOURING tints of one family. Live hue histogram went from a spread to **87% cyan/blue/violet**. **Math glyphs**: new `math_state()` (40 glyphs, ∑ ∫ π √ ∞ ∂ Δ θ λ μ ± ≈ ≠ ≤ ≥ ∇ φ ψ Ω Γ Σ Π + equations e=mc², E=hf, ∇·B=0, ∫dx, U=IR) drawn as text, depth-split; the long "random line" streak wakes were truncated to short stubs in BOTH renderers. **Screen break**: new `screen_break()` (8 fast bodies reaching 1.16·R, beyond the orb envelope) + confirmed nothing clips to the orb radius, so elements run off the widget edge. **Ring bug fixed at source**: `ring_radius_scale`/`ring_tilt_spin` were PER-RING (own periods), so one circle sat at +1.7% while another was at −1.7% — a visible size/axis mismatch. They are now a single shared rigid transform (`RING_MOTION_PERIOD_S=23.3`, non-harmonic with every spin/turn), so every ring keeps its declared size and plane relative to the others while the set still breathes. Full orb suite green (30 files; `test_ring_set_moves_as_one_rigid_body` added, the old per-ring non-harmonic test rewritten to the rigid contract, a fragile in-plane tilt recovery under the 3-D projection replaced with a valid lag check). Orb restarted; live 320×320 verified. **Open**: `fx_perf9u` still fails only the 25ms mean-cost timer at host load ~20 (base measures the same ~34ms) — environmental, not a regression; primitive counts pass.

**Orb orbiters — particles circle the middle (2026-10-18):** Owner: "our particles and stars orbit the circle and not the middle" and "add particles like in a cylinder over middle orb and fix particles to be everywhere". Three lanes (field / Qt / Tk) merged. **Field**: new `orbiter_particles(t,R)` (30 bodies on their OWN inclined circular orbits around the NUCLEUS — each sweeps a full 360 in its own tilted plane, so its depth crosses both sides of the core every revolution, which is what reads as circling the middle rather than sliding along a ring); `ring_particles` already populated all 8 declared rings; `cylinder_particles` gives the vertical column. Also volume axes `z`/`z2` on every element, `core_shells` (interior nucleus depth), `flare_dots`, `filament_state`, per-ring plane precession (`tilt_spin_deg`) + radius breath (`ring_radius_scale`), denser counts (dust 64, sparkles 52, binary 88, blink 32). **Qt**: interior shells drawn depth-sorted on the body; z/z2 volume offset on every element; orbiters/ring-bodies/cylinder/flares batched into a few spectral paths to hold the primitive ceiling. **Tk** mirrored. **Two real defects caught**: a bad `ort` merge had DUPLICATED the z-seam block in `qt_app.py`, so `NUCLEUS_TUMBLE_SCALE` was computed then discarded (fixed); the orbiter batch's glint-arm stroke read as a third "orbit" outline to the echo-ring contract (orbiters are now plain dots — arms are sub-pixel at that size anyway). Primitive budget held at 387–417 (cap 420) by trimming `CYLINDER_BUCKETS`/`ORBITER_BUCKETS` 3→2. Full orb suite green (30 files; `test_orbiter_particles_circle_the_middle_in_3d` added, `tk_turn9u` strip helpers updated). Orb service restarted; live 320×320 shows coloured bodies orbiting the core. **Open**: `fx_perf9u` fails ONLY the 25ms mean-cost timer at host load ~20 — base measures the same ~34ms/frame, so it is environmental, not a regression; primitive counts pass. That also means the live orb is running well under 60fps on this loaded host and should be re-measured on a quiet one.

**Orb never-thin fix (2026-10-18):** Owner: at some point the mark became a thin line; "I don't want that thin". Root cause found by measurement, not guessing: `depth = 0.5+0.5*cos(angle)` while orbit x is `~rx*cos(angle)` — depth was a LINEAR function of x, so every point satisfied `z ≈ c·x` and the whole "3D" mark was secretly one flat plane, which any rotation still takes thin twice per turn (measured 52×16px, 35×12px slashes). Fix: break the lock. Points get fully uncorrelated projection depth (`turn_qdepth` = per-element `hash01` of the index, flat — a genuine volume, shading still from field depth); ring vertices get a smooth phased sine lobe per ring (`TURN_RING_TILT` 0.35, phase from the ring seed — a true plane tilt; per-vertex hash was tried first and turned every orbit into a lightning zigzag, reverted). Face-on/reduced frames are bit-identical (fade zeroes z). Worst phase now 23.5px minor vs 29.5 face-on (was 12px); live 320×320 shows 34–46px minor on all 6 frames across ~20s. Both lanes mirrored with identical salts (Qt `qt_app.py` + Tk `mark.py`); contracts updated to route expectations through `turn_qdepth` (the old helpers measured raw-depth positions the renderer no longer paints). Full orb suite green (31 files, 0 failures). Orb service restarted. Open honesty: individual rings still go edge-on one at a time (real rigid loops do that — different rings at different times now); only the ASSEMBLY never thins, which is what the brief asked.

**Orb v3 — RGB trails, declared circles, Y-axis 360 spin (2026-10-18):** Owner: "stars still have old aura, we need RGB trail not outer glow", more 360 for 3D, refined circles, "advanced studio level", and an ADDITIVE left-to-right horizontal 360 spin. Three lanes merged A→B→C. **Field** (`mark_field.py`, +438): new `rgb_trail`/`dust_trail` (spectral samples: `hue` in turns, per-sample `sat`/`val`/`alpha`/`size`), `ring_geometry`/`ring_path`/`ring_alpha` declaring all 8 circles in one back-to-front list, `hue_to_rgb`, `dof`, `skew`, `ease`; a THIRD shell (`satellites()` now 3), faster full turns (near 12→9.5s, far 17→13.5s), `hue`/`dof`/`skew` added to every element dict. **Qt (live)**: removed the glint outer-halo; stars now stroke per-sample RGB streaks via `hue_to_rgb` (hue is never lerped across the wrap — a 0.98→1.02 streak stays red); every circle drawn from the one declared geometry (fringe only on the 2 main orbits); **Y-axis 360 spin at 20.5s** layered additively over the 24s Z spin (`x' = cx + (x-cx)*cos θ`, negative past 90° mirrors correctly, dimmed by `|cos θ|`, far half darker, nucleus deliberately un-squashed so it keeps a circular silhouette); studio nucleus gained a chromatic corona, beat flare and caustic. **Tk** mirrored all of it (`mark.py` rewritten). 447 orb tests pass, 0 failures, incl. NEW `test_voice_orb_qt_turn9u` (15) and `test_voice_orb_tk_turn9u` (21); primitive count actually DROPPED 195–239 → 141–198 (cap raised 300→420). **Two real field defects the Qt lane caught and reported rather than silently patching**: `ring_geometry` double-lagged its echo tilt (`echo_rings(tt - ECHO_LAG_S)`), putting followers 35° off the shared contract, and it unpacked that unguarded so stubbing the layer raised inside the field and segfaulted the contract test. Both are reconciled/locked in the painters, NOT fixed at source — open. The brief's assumed 19s Y-spin was exactly 2× the 9.5s near-ring period (visibly locks); changed to 20.5s with a test enforcing non-harmonicity. Live: 320×320, ~77k changed px across 4 frame gaps, mark visibly tumbles and mirrors. No global E2E/release verdict; offscreen-only render verification.

**Orb richness + smoky aura (2026-10-18):** Owner asked for more complexity, more fluid motion, a more "real" 3D feel with particles spinning 360° around the nucleus, a bigger nucleus, and a studio-level smoky aura. Field gained `binary_state()` (56 deterministic `1`/`0` data-fragment glyphs) and `inner_ring_tilt()` (third core orbit), plus `DEPTH_POPOUT` 0.55→0.70, `DEPTH_PERSPECTIVE` 0.15→0.20, `SPEED_MOD_HZ` 0.7→0.45. A 24s master 360° spin of the whole mark was added on top of all existing motion. The nucleus is 1.25× bigger with counter-rotating swirl lobes and an orbiting specular. **The Qt lane's first agent returned empty (no commits) — Qt is the LIVE renderer, so the whole feature set was invisible until a second agent ported it from the Tk lane; verified Qt/Tk parity at 131 vs 133 filled ellipses.** The 3-stop glow gradient was the "teenager" artefact the owner flagged: it held an alpha plateau beside the opaque body and read as a flat grey disc. Replaced with a many-stop power falloff (24 stops, power 3.9) plus three slow drifting smoke plumes — radial luminance now falls 133→22 within r=14→20px and reaches background by r≈30, i.e. a tight smoky corona with no rim. Test contract fixed: `_nucleus()` picked the *nearest* ellipse, which the new orbiting specular beat, inventing 4 pulse peaks instead of 2; it now takes the largest centred ellipse. `test_app_draws_no_chrome_but_keeps_indicators` asserted `texts == []`, which the binary glyphs broke; it now asserts every text is `1`/`0` (the old `x`/`-` chrome intent preserved). **Baseline:** The worktree was already substantially dirty before this session, including Habblaud route/auth/stream changes, modified vendor bundle/provenance, and game entry. I preserved existing edits and made scoped changes. Expected root docs (`README.md`, `STATUS.md`, `PARITY.md`, `MIGRATION.md`, `BRAND.md`, `PLAN.md`, `EXECUTION_PLAN.md`, `CHECKLIST.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`) and cited archived licensing/reuse Markdown are absent in this checkout. Extant Office authorities are under `docs/game-office/`.

**Concrete baseline defects:** the vendored integration claimed the `snapshot`/`feed` SSE schema but the active service was returning 401 (live browser showed Habblaud's Disconnected/error UI). Strict stream auth did not allow its credential-less EventSource. It also had a query-token stream helper, no iframe sandbox, an unrecognized `?app=1` compatibility switch, and a status strip over the upstream viewport. Further audits confirmed supported read APIs already existed in `qubit_compat.py`, but the client adapter did not route `/sessions/recent`, timeline or stats calls there, so these hit unrelated API routes and failed.

**Changes:** Stream handler enables the restricted loopback + same Host + own-app Referer auth path for EventSource; query-token construction and dead stream-global injection are removed. The translator emits retry, snapshot, and only a non-empty real feed, with the Habblaud-compatible payload schema. Iframe sandbox allows required script/same-origin/forms/downloads/popups/modals, not top navigation. `?app=1` forces Habblaud even with `shell`. Status overlay no longer covers the game. API bridge wires Qubit access, agent detail, sessions/recent, timeline days/day and stats days/day to existing Qubit read authorities; unsupported APIs get typed disabled 501 responses and are not proxied. Updated binding map, auth/adapter tests, and provenance/isolation documentation.
